```
1	# Save format & compatibility
2	
3	## Schema
4	
5	`SaveData` (version 3) in `lib/game/types.ts`:
6	
7	- world `seed`
8	- a block **diff** list — `changes: [blockIndex, blockId][]`; edits that revert to the generated baseline are pruned (player-placed crops, beds, and furnaces ride this list — they are ordinary block edits)
9	- inventory slots with durability (36 slots; the first 9 are the hotbar)
10	- equipped armor
11	- selected hotbar slot (0–8)
12	- player position
13	- `dayClock`, `hearts`, `hunger` (all optional) — time of day and player stats, so sleeping through the night and a hurt/hungry player survive a reload
14	- `spawnPoint` (optional, `{x,y,z}` or null) — the bed respawn point; respawn falls back to a random land point when it is null or the block there is no longer a bed
15	
16	Stored in localStorage under `SAVE_KEY` (`minecraft_save_v5`, defined in `lib/game/config.ts`). Read/write and restore validation live in `lib/game/save.ts` (the `Storage` is injectable for tests); `GameEngine.serialize()` produces the save from live state, and `lib/game/engine/blockChanges.ts` maintains the block diff.
17	
18	Audio volume preferences live under a **separate** key (`minecraft_audio_v1`, `lib/game/audio/settings.ts`) and are not part of the world save or its versioning.
19	
20	### Version history
21	
22	- **v3** — adds optional `dayClock`, `hearts`, `hunger`, and `spawnPoint`. `migrateSaveV2toV3` is a pure version bump (the new fields stay absent and restore as engine defaults), so **`SAVE_KEY` is unchanged** and v1/v2 saves load fine. `readSave` chains the migrations (v1 → v2 → v3).
23	- **v2** — same fields as v1, reinterpreted for the 36-slot inventory and 9-slot hotbar.
24	- **v1** — 40 slots, 10-slot hotbar. Still accepted: `readSave` runs `migrateSaveV1toV2`, which packs non-empty slots in order, merges stackable items into earlier stacks, clamps `selectedSlot` to 0–8, and drops items that overflow the smaller inventory.
25	- Unknown future versions are rejected (treated as no save).
26	
27	## Autosave
28	
29	Every 15s via `setInterval`, plus on `beforeunload`.
30	
31	## Compatibility rules
32	
33	- Saves store **diffs against generated terrain**, so changing world generation or the voxel index formula (`x + z*sizeX + y*sizeX*sizeZ`) silently corrupts existing saves — bump `SAVE_KEY` when you do.
34	- Worldgen output is pinned by SHA-256 characterization tests in `lib/world/generation.test.ts`; they fail on any byte-level change. See [testing.md](testing.md) for the re-baseline policy. Caveat: the noise functions use `Math.sin`, whose exact results are engine-defined — the tests prove refactor purity on the pinned Bun version, not cross-browser save portability.
35	- Changing the save shape requires bumping the `version` field and handling (or discarding) old data. Round-trip tests live in `lib/game/save.test.ts` and `lib/game/engine/GameEngine.test.ts`.
36	- Note save-format/worldgen impact in PRs.
37	
```

134	}
135	
136	/** Per-frame continuous input, owned by the input controller. */
137	export type FrameInput = {
138	  keys: ReadonlySet<string>;
139	  capsActive: boolean;
140	  leftMouseHeld: boolean;
141	  pointerLocked: boolean;
142	};
143	
144	export const IDLE_INPUT: FrameInput = {
145	  keys: new Set<string>(),
146	  capsActive: false,
147	  leftMouseHeld: false,
148	  pointerLocked: false
149	};
150	
151	/** The engine surface the UI may touch: intents in, save data out. */
152	export type GameApi = {
153	  dispatch(command: Command): void;
154	  serialize(): SaveData;
155	};
156	
157	/** Immutable view for the React UI, replaced only when a visible value changes. */
158	export type GameSnapshot = {
159	  /** Stable handle for dispatching intents; null until the engine exists. */
160	  api: GameApi | null;
161	  inventory: InventorySlot[];
162	  equippedArmor: EquippedArmor;
163	  selectedSlot: number;
164	  hearts: number;
165	  hunger: number;
166	  daylightPercent: number;
167	  passiveCount: number;
168	  hostileCount: number;
169	  respawnSeconds: number;
170	  inventoryOpen: boolean;
171	  paused: boolean;
172	  debugOpen: boolean;
173	  debug: DebugInfo | null;
174	  cameraMode: CameraMode;
175	  /** Total defense points of equipped armor — drives the HUD armor bar. */
176	  armorPoints: number;
177	  capsActive: boolean;
178	  /** True during the sleep fade — drives the fade-to-black overlay. */
179	  sleeping: boolean;
180	  /** Open crafting station (gates smelting recipes in the inventory panel). */
181	  craftingStation: "furnace" | null;
182	};
183	
184	/** One-shot gameplay events for the shell (death screen, audio, ...). */
185	export type GameEvent =
186	  | { type: "died" }
187	  | { type: "respawned" }
188	  | { type: "blockBroken"; blockId: BlockId; x: number; y: number; z: number }
189	  | { type: "blockPlaced"; blockId: BlockId; x: number; y: number; z: number }
190	  | { type: "playerHurt" }
191	  | { type: "ateFood" }
192	  | { type: "jumped" }

194	  | { type: "mobAttacked"; kind: MobKind }

206	  | { type: "mobBred"; kind: MobKind }
207	  | { type: "pickedUp"; items: Array<{ itemId: string; count: number }> };
208

1	import { useState } from "react";
2	import ItemIcon from "@/components/game/ItemIcon";
3	import { itemTooltipFor, useItemTooltip } from "@/components/game/ItemTooltip";
4	import PixelImg from "@/components/game/PixelImg";
5	import { ARMOR_SLOT_LABELS, ARMOR_SLOTS, createSlot } from "@/lib/game/items";
6	import { itemIconUrl } from "@/lib/ui/sprites";
7	import type { EquippedArmor, InventorySlot, Recipe } from "@/lib/game/types";
8	
9	type InventoryPanelProps = {
10	  inventory: InventorySlot[];
11	  equippedArmor: EquippedArmor;
12	  selectedHotbarSlot: number;
13	  hotbarSlots: number;
14	  recipes: Recipe[];
15	  craftingStation: "furnace" | null;
16	  canCraft: (recipe: Recipe) => boolean;
17	  onSwapSlots: (fromIndex: number, toIndex: number) => void;
18	  onToggleEquipArmor: (index: number) => void;
19	  onCraft: (recipe: Recipe) => void;
20	};
21	
22	const STATION_LABELS: Record<NonNullable<Recipe["station"]>, string> = {
23	  furnace: "Furnace"
24	};
25	
26	/**
27	 * The survival inventory: armor column, 27-slot storage grid, hotbar row, and
28	 * a recipe book where every entry shows ingredient icons and the result.
29	 * Items move by clicking one slot and then another (click-to-swap); clicking
30	 * an armor item toggles equipping it instead.
31	 */
32	export default function InventoryPanel({
33	  inventory,
34	  equippedArmor,
35	  selectedHotbarSlot,
36	  hotbarSlots,
37	  recipes,
38	  craftingStation,
39	  canCraft,
40	  onSwapSlots,
41	  onToggleEquipArmor,
42	  onCraft
43	}: InventoryPanelProps) {
44	  const [pendingIndex, setPendingIndex] = useState<number | null>(null);
45	  const { tooltip, bind } = useItemTooltip();
46	
47	  const onSlotClick = (index: number) => {
48	    const slot = inventory[index];
49	    if (slot.kind === "armor" && slot.count > 0) {
50	      onToggleEquipArmor(index);
51	      setPendingIndex(null);
52	      return;
53	    }
54	
55	    if (pendingIndex === null) {
56	      setPendingIndex(index);
57	      return;
58	    }
59	    if (pendingIndex === index) {
60	      setPendingIndex(null);
61	      return;
62	    }
63	    onSwapSlots(pendingIndex, index);
64	    setPendingIndex(null);
65	  };
66	
67	  const isEquipped = (slot: InventorySlot) => slot.kind === "armor" && !!slot.id && equippedArmor[slot.armorSlot ?? "helmet"] === slot.id;
68	
69	  const renderSlot = (slot: InventorySlot, idx: number, extraClass = "") => (
70	    <button
71	      key={`inv-slot-${idx}`}
72	      className={["inv-slot", extraClass, pendingIndex === idx ? "pending" : "", isEquipped(slot) ? "equipped" : ""].filter(Boolean).join(" ")}
73	      onClick={() => onSlotClick(idx)}
74	      {...bind(itemTooltipFor(slot))}
75	      aria-label={slot.id && slot.count > 0 ? `Slot ${idx + 1}: ${slot.label}` : `Slot ${idx + 1}: empty`}
76	    >
77	      <ItemIcon slot={slot} size={32} />
78	    </button>
79	  );
80	
81	  const hotbar = inventory.slice(0, hotbarSlots);
82	  const storage = inventory.slice(hotbarSlots);
83	
84	  return (
85	    <div className="inventory-panel">
86	      <div className="inventory-columns">
87	        <div className="inventory-main">
88	          <div className="inventory-heading">Inventory</div>
89	          <div className="inventory-upper">
90	            <div className="armor-column">
91	              {ARMOR_SLOTS.map((armorSlot) => {
92	                const equippedId = equippedArmor[armorSlot];
93	                const equippedIndex = equippedId ? inventory.findIndex((slot) => slot.id === equippedId && slot.count > 0) : -1;
94	                const equippedItem = equippedIndex >= 0 ? inventory[equippedIndex] : undefined;
95	                return (
96	                  <button
97	                    key={`armor-${armorSlot}`}
98	                    className={equippedItem ? "inv-slot armor-slot filled" : "inv-slot armor-slot"}
99	                    onClick={() => equippedIndex >= 0 && onToggleEquipArmor(equippedIndex)}
100	                    {...bind(equippedItem ? itemTooltipFor(equippedItem) : { title: `${ARMOR_SLOT_LABELS[armorSlot]} (empty)` })}
101	                    aria-label={equippedItem ? `${ARMOR_SLOT_LABELS[armorSlot]}: ${equippedItem.label}` : `${ARMOR_SLOT_LABELS[armorSlot]}: empty`}
102	                  >
103	                    {equippedItem ? (
104	                      <ItemIcon slot={equippedItem} size={32} />
105	                    ) : (
106	                      <span className="armor-ghost">
107	                        <PixelImg src={itemIconUrl(armorSlot)} alt="" size={32} aria-hidden />
108	                      </span>
109	                    )}
110	                  </button>
111	                );
112	              })}
113	            </div>
114	            <div className="inventory-hint">
115	              Click one slot, then another to move items.
116	              <br />
117	              Click armor to equip or unequip it.
118	            </div>
119	          </div>
120	          <div className="inv-grid storage" data-testid="storage-grid">
121	            {storage.map((slot, offset) => renderSlot(slot, offset + hotbarSlots))}
122	          </div>
123	          <div className="inv-grid hotbar-row" data-testid="hotbar-grid">
124	            {hotbar.map((slot, idx) => renderSlot(slot, idx, idx === selectedHotbarSlot ? "active" : ""))}
125	          </div>
126	        </div>
127	
128	        <div className="recipe-book">
129	          <div className="inventory-heading">Crafting</div>
130	          <div className="recipe-list">
131	            {recipes.map((recipe) => {
132	              const stationLocked = !!recipe.station && recipe.station !== craftingStation;
133	              return (
134	                <button
135	                  key={recipe.id}
136	                  className="recipe-entry"
137	                  onClick={() => onCraft(recipe)}
138	                  disabled={stationLocked || !canCraft(recipe)}
139	                  aria-label={recipe.label}
140	                  {...bind({ title: recipe.label, lines: stationLocked ? [`Requires ${STATION_LABELS[recipe.station!]}`] : undefined })}
141	                >
142	                  <span className="recipe-ingredients">
143	                    {recipe.cost.map((cost) => (
144	                      <ItemIcon key={`${recipe.id}-${cost.slotId}`} slot={createSlot(cost.slotId, cost.count)} size={24} />
145	                    ))}
146	                  </span>
147	                  <span className="recipe-arrow" aria-hidden>
148	                    →
149	                  </span>
150	                  <ItemIcon slot={createSlot(recipe.result.slotId, recipe.result.count)} size={24} />
151	                </button>
152	              );
153	            })}
154	          </div>
155	        </div>
156	      </div>
157	      {tooltip}
158	    </div>
159	  );
160	}
161

1	import * as THREE from "three";
2	import { BlockId, collidesAt, generateWorld, VoxelWorld, WORLD_SIZE_X, WORLD_SIZE_Y, WORLD_SIZE_Z } from "@/lib/world";
3	import {
4	  DAY_CYCLE_SECONDS,
5	  HOTBAR_SLOTS,
6	  MAX_HUNGER,
7	  MAX_HEARTS,
8	  PLAYER_HALF_WIDTH,
9	  PLAYER_HEIGHT,
10	  RENDER_RADIUS,
11	  STUCK_RESET_SECONDS,
12	  WAKE_DAY_PHASE
13	} from "@/lib/game/config";
14	import { createEmptyArmorEquipment, createInitialInventory } from "@/lib/game/items";
15	import { RECIPES } from "@/lib/game/recipes";
16	import * as inv from "@/lib/game/inventory";
17	import {
18	  inventorySlotsSnapshot,
19	  restoreDayClock,
20	  restoreEquippedArmor,
21	  restoreHearts,
22	  restoreHungerLevel,
23	  restoreInventorySlots,
24	  restoreSelectedSlot,
25	  restoreSpawnPoint
26	} from "@/lib/game/save";
27	import { createSurfaceYAt, findSpawnOnLand, randomLandPointNear, type SurfaceYAtFn } from "@/lib/game/spawn";
28	import { rollMobDrops } from "@/lib/game/mobLoot";
29	import type { SaveData } from "@/lib/game/types";
30	import { createBlockChangeTracker } from "./blockChanges";
31	import type { Command } from "./commands";
32	import { createTimers, nextCameraMode, type FrameInput, type GameEvent, type GameSnapshot, type GameState } from "./state";
33	import { daylightAt, tickDayNight } from "./systems/dayNight";
34	import { tickWeather } from "./systems/weather";
35	import { applyDamageWithArmor, tickRespawnTimer } from "./systems/playerLife";
36	import { tickPlayerMotion } from "./systems/playerMotion";
37	import { restoreHunger, tickHungerDrain, tickHealthRegen } from "./systems/playerStats";
38	import { placeSelectedBlock, resetMining, tickMining } from "./systems/mining";
39	import { tryFeedAimedMob, tryInteractBlock, tryUseHeldItem } from "./systems/interact";
40	import { tryAttackMob, weaponDamage } from "./systems/combat";
41	import { tickMobs } from "./systems/mobAI";
42	import { tickRandomBlocks } from "./systems/randomTicks";
43	import { tickBreeding } from "./systems/breeding";
44	import { spawnInitialMobs, tickHostileSpawnDirector } from "./systems/spawnDirector";
45	
46	export type GameEngineOptions = {
47	  /** A parsed save to restore, or null for a fresh world. */
48	  save?: SaveData | null;
49	  /** Seed for a fresh world; ignored when a save is provided. */
50	  seed?: number;
51	  /** Randomness source for mob spawning/AI — injectable for deterministic tests. */
52	  rng?: () => number;
53	  /** World dimensions override for fast headless tests. */
54	  worldSize?: { x: number; y: number; z: number };
55	};
56	
57	/**
58	 * The framework-agnostic game core. Owns all simulation state, advances it in
59	 * step(), and accepts player intents through dispatch(). No React, no DOM, no
60	 * rendering — the renderer reads state, the React shell subscribes to
61	 * snapshots via subscribe()/getSnapshot() (useSyncExternalStore compatible).
62	 */
63	export class GameEngine {
64	  readonly state: GameState;
65	  private readonly rng: () => number;
66	  private readonly surfaceYAt: SurfaceYAtFn;
67	  private readonly listeners = new Set<() => void>();
68	  private events: GameEvent[] = [];
69	  private snapshot: GameSnapshot;
70	
71	  constructor(options: GameEngineOptions = {}) {
72	    const save = options.save ?? null;
73	    this.rng = options.rng ?? Math.random;
74	
75	    const seed = save?.seed ?? options.seed ?? Math.floor(Math.random() * 2147483647);
76	    const size = options.worldSize ?? { x: WORLD_SIZE_X, y: WORLD_SIZE_Y, z: WORLD_SIZE_Z };
77	    const world = new VoxelWorld(size.x, size.y, size.z, seed);
78	    generateWorld(world);
79	
80	    const blockChanges = createBlockChangeTracker(world);
81	    if (save) blockChanges.applySavedChanges(save.changes);
82	
83	    this.surfaceYAt = createSurfaceYAt(world);
84	
85	    const firstSpawn = findSpawnOnLand(world, Math.floor(world.sizeX / 2), Math.floor(world.sizeZ / 2));
86	    this.state = {
87	      world,
88	      blockChanges,
89	      player: {
90	        position: new THREE.Vector3(firstSpawn.x, firstSpawn.y, firstSpawn.z),
91	        velocity: new THREE.Vector3(),
92	        yaw: 0,
93	        pitch: 0,
94	        onGround: false
95	      },
96	      inventory: createInitialInventory(),
97	      equippedArmor: createEmptyArmorEquipment(),
98	      selectedSlot: 0,
99	      hearts: MAX_HEARTS,
100	      hunger: MAX_HUNGER,
101	      isDead: false,
102	      respawnTimer: 0,
103	      inventoryOpen: false,
104	      craftingStation: null,
105	      paused: false,
106	      debugOpen: false,
107	      debugInfo: null,
108	      cameraMode: "first",
109	      capsActive: false,
110	      mobs: [],
111	      nextMobId: 1,
112	      dayClock: 0,
113	      daylight: daylightAt(0),
114	      daylightPercent: Math.round(daylightAt(0) * 100),
115	      weather: { kind: "clear", intensity: 0 },
116	      sleepTimer: 0,
117	      spawnPoint: null,
118	      mining: { targetKey: "", progress: 0 },
119	      timers: createTimers(),
120	      worldMeshDirty: true
121	    };
122	
123	    if (save) {
124	      this.state.inventory = restoreInventorySlots(save) ?? this.state.inventory;
125	      this.state.equippedArmor = restoreEquippedArmor(save) ?? this.state.equippedArmor;
126	      this.state.selectedSlot = restoreSelectedSlot(save) ?? this.state.selectedSlot;
127	      this.state.hearts = restoreHearts(save) ?? this.state.hearts;
128	      this.state.hunger = restoreHungerLevel(save) ?? this.state.hunger;
129	      this.state.spawnPoint = restoreSpawnPoint(save);
130	      const savedClock = restoreDayClock(save);
131	      if (savedClock !== null) {
132	        this.state.dayClock = savedClock;
133	        this.state.daylight = daylightAt(savedClock);
134	        this.state.daylightPercent = Math.round(this.state.daylight * 100);
135	      }
136	      if (save.player) this.state.player.position.set(save.player.x, save.player.y, save.player.z);
137	    }
138	
139	    // Safety check: if stuck after load, relocate to a plain.
140	    if (collidesAt(world, this.state.player.position, PLAYER_HALF_WIDTH, PLAYER_HEIGHT) || this.state.player.position.y < 2) {
141	      this.forceUnstuck();
142	    }
143	
144	    spawnInitialMobs(this.state, this.rng, this.surfaceYAt);
145	    // Seed weather from the (possibly restored) dayClock + player position so a
146	    // loaded save's first frame/snapshot is consistent before the first step().
147	    tickWeather(this.state);
148	    this.snapshot = this.buildSnapshot();
149	  }
150	
151	  /** Advances the simulation by dt seconds. The renderer draws the state afterwards. */
152	  step(dt: number, input: FrameInput): void {
153	    const state = this.state;
154	    if (state.paused) {
155	      // Full freeze: mobs, the day clock, mining, and stats all stop.
156	      this.refreshSnapshot();
157	      return;
158	    }
159	    state.capsActive = input.capsActive;
160	
161	    // Stuck detection / auto-unstuck.
162	    const inBadState = collidesAt(state.world, state.player.position, PLAYER_HALF_WIDTH, PLAYER_HEIGHT) || state.player.position.y < 2;
163	    state.timers.stuckTimer = inBadState ? state.timers.stuckTimer + dt : 0;
164	    if (state.timers.stuckTimer > STUCK_RESET_SECONDS) {
165	      this.forceUnstuck();
166	      state.timers.stuckTimer = 0;
167	    }
168	
169	    // Death: only mobs and the respawn countdown tick while dead.
170	    if (state.isDead) {
171	      if (tickRespawnTimer(state, dt)) this.respawn();
172	      else tickMobs(state, dt, this.mobTickDeps);
173	      this.refreshSnapshot();
174	      return;
175	    }
176	
177	    // Sleeping: a full freeze during the fade, then a jump to the next morning.
178	    if (state.sleepTimer > 0) {
179	      state.sleepTimer = Math.max(0, state.sleepTimer - dt);
180	      if (state.sleepTimer === 0) this.wakeToMorning();
181	      this.refreshSnapshot();
182	      return;
183	    }
184	
185	    const move = tickPlayerMotion(state, input, dt, this.applyDamage);
186	    if (move.didJump) this.emit({ type: "jumped" });
187	    if (move.didLand) this.emit({ type: "landed", impact: move.landImpact });
188	    tickHungerDrain(state, move);
189	    tickHealthRegen(state, dt);
190	    tickMining(state, input, dt, this.emit, this.rng);
191	    tickDayNight(state, dt);
192	    tickWeather(state);
193	    tickRandomBlocks(state, dt, this.rng);
194	    tickHostileSpawnDirector(state, dt, this.rng, this.surfaceYAt);
195	    tickMobs(state, dt, this.mobTickDeps);
196	    tickBreeding(state, dt, this.rng, this.surfaceYAt, this.emit);
197	    this.tickDebugInfo(dt);
198	
199	    this.refreshSnapshot();
200	  }
201	
202	  /** Applies a discrete player intent. */
203	  dispatch(command: Command): void {
204	    const state = this.state;
205	    switch (command.type) {
206	      case "selectSlot": {
207	        if (command.index >= 0 && command.index < Math.min(HOTBAR_SLOTS, state.inventory.length)) {
208	          state.selectedSlot = command.index;
209	        }
210	        break;
211	      }
212	      case "toggleInventory": {
213	        state.inventoryOpen = !state.inventoryOpen;
214	        if (!state.inventoryOpen) state.craftingStation = null; // leaving the panel closes the station
215	        break;
216	      }
217	      case "craft": {
218	        const recipe = RECIPES.find((entry) => entry.id === command.recipeId);
219	        if (!recipe || state.isDead) break;
220	        // Station recipes (e.g. furnace smelting) require that station to be open.
221	        // The UI gates these too, but dispatch is the spoofable surface to guard.
222	        if (recipe.station && recipe.station !== state.craftingStation) break;
223	        const next = inv.craft(state.inventory, recipe);
224	        if (!next) break;
225	        state.inventory = next;
226	        if (recipe.station) this.emit({ type: "smelted" });
227	        break;
228	      }
229	      case "swapSlots": {
230	        state.inventory = inv.swapSlots(state.inventory, command.from, command.to) ?? state.inventory;
231	        break;
232	      }
233	      case "toggleEquipArmor": {
234	        state.equippedArmor = inv.toggleEquipArmor(state.inventory, state.equippedArmor, command.index) ?? state.equippedArmor;
235	        break;
236	      }
237	      case "eatFood": {
238	        if (state.isDead || state.inventoryOpen || state.sleepTimer > 0) break;
239	        const slot = state.inventory[state.selectedSlot];
240	        if (!slot?.id || slot.kind !== "food" || !slot.hunger || slot.count <= 0) break;
241	        const next = inv.adjustSlotCount(state.inventory, slot.id, -1, state.selectedSlot);
242	        if (!next) break;
243	        state.inventory = next;
244	        state.hunger = restoreHunger(state.hunger, slot.hunger);
245	        this.emit({ type: "ateFood" });
246	        break;
247	      }
248	      case "placeBlock": {
249	        if (state.isDead || state.inventoryOpen || state.sleepTimer > 0) break;
250	        // Right-click precedence: feed an aimed animal, then interact with the
251	        // aimed block (bed, furnace), then use the held item (hoe, seeds); only
252	        // place a block if none of those consumed the click.
253	        if (tryFeedAimedMob(state, this.emit)) break;
254	        if (tryInteractBlock(state, this.emit)) break;
255	        if (tryUseHeldItem(state, this.emit, this.rng)) break;
256	        placeSelectedBlock(state, this.emit);
257	        break;
258	      }
259	      case "attack": {
260	        if (state.isDead || state.inventoryOpen || state.sleepTimer > 0) break;
261	        this.emit({ type: "attackSwung" });
262	        const hitKind = tryAttackMob(state, weaponDamage(state), this.removeMobAt);
263	        if (hitKind) {
264	          this.emit({ type: "mobHit", kind: hitKind });
265	          state.inventory = inv.consumeToolDurability(state.inventory, state.selectedSlot, 1) ?? state.inventory;
266	          resetMining(state);
267	        }
268	        break;
269	      }
270	      case "unstuck": {
271	        if (state.isDead) break;
272	        this.forceUnstuck();
273	        break;
274	      }
275	      case "pause": {
276	        // The inventory panel and the death screen own their lock-loss; only
277	        // plain gameplay lock-loss (or an explicit Escape) opens the pause menu.
278	        // Sleeping is a brief, atomic freeze — pausing mid-fade would stall the
279	        // sleep timer (step early-returns on paused before the sleep branch).
280	        if (state.inventoryOpen || state.isDead || state.sleepTimer > 0) break;
281	        state.paused = true;
282	        state.craftingStation = null;
283	        break;
284	      }
285	      case "resume": {
286	        state.paused = false;
287	        break;
288	      }
289	      case "toggleDebug": {
290	        state.debugOpen = !state.debugOpen;
291	        state.debugInfo = state.debugOpen ? this.currentDebugInfo() : null;
292	        break;
293	      }
294	      case "toggleCameraView": {
295	        // Render-only, so it works even while dead or paused (like Minecraft F5).
296	        state.cameraMode = nextCameraMode(state.cameraMode);
297	        break;
298	      }
299	      case "respawn": {
300	        // Skip the rest of the countdown; the next step performs the respawn.
301	        if (state.isDead) state.respawnTimer = 0;
302	        break;
303	      }
304	    }
305	    this.syncEquippedArmor();
306	    this.refreshSnapshot();
307	  }
308	
309	  /** Mouse-look: applied directly by the input controller (radians). */
310	  applyLook(deltaYaw: number, deltaPitch: number): void {
311	    const player = this.state.player;
312	    player.yaw += deltaYaw;
313	    player.pitch = Math.max(-Math.PI / 2 + 0.01, Math.min(Math.PI / 2 - 0.01, player.pitch + deltaPitch));
314	  }
315	
316	  /** Current world + player state as a persistable save. */
317	  serialize(): SaveData {
318	    const state = this.state;
319	    return {
320	      version: 3,
321	      seed: state.world.seed,
322	      changes: state.blockChanges.changes(),
323	      inventorySlots: inventorySlotsSnapshot(state.inventory),
324	      equippedArmor: { ...state.equippedArmor },
325	      selectedSlot: state.selectedSlot,
326	      player: {
327	        x: state.player.position.x,
328	        y: state.player.position.y,
329	        z: state.player.position.z
330	      },
331	      dayClock: state.dayClock,
332	      hearts: state.hearts,
333	      hunger: state.hunger,
334	      spawnPoint: state.spawnPoint ? { ...state.spawnPoint } : null
335	    };
336	  }
337	
338	  subscribe = (listener: () => void): (() => void) => {
339	    this.listeners.add(listener);
340	    return () => this.listeners.delete(listener);
341	  };
342	
343	  getSnapshot = (): GameSnapshot => this.snapshot;
344	
345	  /** Drains queued one-shot gameplay events for the shell (death screen, audio). */
346	  consumeEvents(): GameEvent[] {
347	    if (this.events.length === 0) return this.events;
348	    const drained = this.events;
349	    this.events = [];
350	    return drained;
351	  }
352	
353	  private emit = (event: GameEvent): void => {
354	    this.events.push(event);
355	  };
356	
357	  private applyDamage = (amount: number): void => {
358	    const heartsBefore = this.state.hearts;
359	    const died = applyDamageWithArmor(this.state, amount);
360	    this.syncEquippedArmor();
361	    if (died) {
362	      resetMining(this.state);
363	      this.emit({ type: "died" });
364	    } else if (this.state.hearts < heartsBefore) {
365	      this.emit({ type: "playerHurt" });
366	    }
367	  };
368	
369	  private removeMobAt = (index: number): void => {
370	    const state = this.state;
371	    const mob = state.mobs[index];
372	    state.mobs.splice(index, 1);
373	    // Babies drop nothing — only grown animals yield loot.
374	    const drops = mob.ageTimer <= 0 ? rollMobDrops(mob.kind, this.rng) : [];
375	    for (const drop of drops) {
376	      state.inventory = inv.adjustSlotCount(state.inventory, drop.itemId, drop.count) ?? state.inventory;
377	    }
378	    this.emit({ type: "mobDied", kind: mob.kind, x: mob.position.x, y: mob.position.y, z: mob.position.z });
379	    // Drops land straight in inventory (no ground item), so announce them.
380	    if (drops.length > 0) this.emit({ type: "pickedUp", items: drops });
381	  };
382	
383	  private get mobTickDeps() {
384	    return {
385	      surfaceYAt: this.surfaceYAt,
386	      applyDamage: this.applyDamage,
387	      removeMobAt: this.removeMobAt,
388	      rng: this.rng,
389	      emit: this.emit
390	    };
391	  }
392	
393	  private forceUnstuck(): void {
394	    const state = this.state;
395	    const safe = findSpawnOnLand(state.world, state.player.position.x, state.player.position.z, true);
396	    state.player.position.set(safe.x, safe.y, safe.z);
397	    state.player.velocity.set(0, 0, 0);
398	    state.player.onGround = false;
399	    state.worldMeshDirty = true;
400	  }
401	
402	  private respawn(): void {
403	    const state = this.state;
404	    const bed = state.spawnPoint;
405	    if (bed && state.world.get(bed.x, bed.y, bed.z) === BlockId.Bed) {
406	      // Respawn standing on the bed; the bed block is non-solid head room above it.
407	      state.player.position.set(bed.x + 0.5, bed.y + 1.05, bed.z + 0.5);
408	    } else {
409	      const spawn = randomLandPointNear(state.world, this.surfaceYAt, state.world.sizeX / 2, state.world.sizeZ / 2, RENDER_RADIUS * 0.9, this.rng);
410	      state.player.position.set(spawn.x, spawn.y + 2, spawn.z);
411	    }
412	    state.player.velocity.set(0, 0, 0);
413	    state.player.pitch = 0;
414	    resetMining(state);
415	    state.worldMeshDirty = true;
416	    this.events.push({ type: "respawned" });
417	  }
418	
419	  /** Advances the day clock to the next morning after a sleep completes. */
420	  private wakeToMorning(): void {
421	    const state = this.state;
422	    const nextDay = Math.floor(state.dayClock / DAY_CYCLE_SECONDS) + 1;
423	    state.dayClock = (nextDay + WAKE_DAY_PHASE) * DAY_CYCLE_SECONDS;
424	    state.daylight = daylightAt(state.dayClock);
425	    state.daylightPercent = Math.round(state.daylight * 100);
426	    this.emit({ type: "wokeUp" });
427	  }
428	
429	  /** Unequips armor that left the inventory (broken or dropped). */
430	  private syncEquippedArmor(): void {
431	    const state = this.state;
432	    state.equippedArmor = inv.unequipMissingArmor(state.inventory, state.equippedArmor) ?? state.equippedArmor;
433	  }
434	
435	  /** Refreshes the F3 readout at ~4 Hz so React is not re-rendered every frame. */
436	  private tickDebugInfo(dt: number): void {
437	    const state = this.state;
438	    if (!state.debugOpen) return;
439	    state.timers.debugHudTimer += dt;
440	    if (state.timers.debugHudTimer < 0.25) return;
441	    state.timers.debugHudTimer = 0;
442	    state.debugInfo = this.currentDebugInfo();
443	  }
444	
445	  private currentDebugInfo() {
446	    const { player, daylight } = this.state;
447	    return {
448	      x: Math.round(player.position.x * 10) / 10,
449	      y: Math.round(player.position.y * 10) / 10,
450	      z: Math.round(player.position.z * 10) / 10,
451	      daylight: Math.round(daylight * 100) / 100
452	    };
453	  }
454	
455	  private buildSnapshot(): GameSnapshot {
456	    const state = this.state;
457	    return {
458	      api: this,
459	      inventory: state.inventory,
460	      equippedArmor: state.equippedArmor,
461	      selectedSlot: state.selectedSlot,
462	      hearts: state.hearts,
463	      hunger: state.hunger,
464	      daylightPercent: state.daylightPercent,
465	      passiveCount: state.mobs.reduce((acc, mob) => acc + (mob.hostile ? 0 : 1), 0),
466	      hostileCount: state.mobs.reduce((acc, mob) => acc + (mob.hostile ? 1 : 0), 0),
467	      respawnSeconds: state.isDead ? Math.max(0, Math.ceil(state.respawnTimer)) : 0,
468	      inventoryOpen: state.inventoryOpen,
469	      paused: state.paused,
470	      debugOpen: state.debugOpen,
471	      debug: state.debugInfo,
472	      cameraMode: state.cameraMode,
473	      armorPoints: inv.equippedDefense(state.inventory, state.equippedArmor),
474	      capsActive: state.capsActive,
475	      sleeping: state.sleepTimer > 0,
476	      craftingStation: state.craftingStation
477	    };
478	  }
479	
480	  private refreshSnapshot(): void {
481	    const next = this.buildSnapshot();
482	    const prev = this.snapshot;
483	    const changed = (Object.keys(next) as Array<keyof GameSnapshot>).some((key) => next[key] !== prev[key]);
484	    if (!changed) return;
485	    this.snapshot = next;
486	    for (const listener of this.listeners) listener();
487	  }
488	}
489

1	/**
2	 * Discrete player intents. Every state mutation triggered by the UI or input
3	 * controller goes through GameEngine.dispatch with one of these — the single
4	 * auditable entry point into the simulation.
5	 */
6	export type Command =
7	  | { type: "selectSlot"; index: number }
8	  | { type: "toggleInventory" }
9	  | { type: "craft"; recipeId: string }
10	  | { type: "swapSlots"; from: number; to: number }

1	import * as THREE from "three";
2	import { BlockId, collidesAt, voxelRaycast } from "@/lib/world";
3	import { BARE_HAND_MINE_POWER, EYE_HEIGHT, MINE_REACH, MINING_RATE, PLAYER_HALF_WIDTH, PLAYER_HEIGHT } from "@/lib/game/config";
4	import { BREAK_HARDNESS, rollBlockDrops } from "@/lib/game/items";
5	import { adjustSlotCount, consumeToolDurability } from "@/lib/game/inventory";
6	import type { EmitGameEvent, FrameInput, GameState } from "../state";
7	import { lookDirection } from "./playerMotion";
8	import type { InventorySlot } from "@/lib/game/types";
9	
10	const scratchEye = new THREE.Vector3();
11	const scratchDir = new THREE.Vector3();
12	
13	function eyePosition(state: GameState, out: THREE.Vector3): THREE.Vector3 {
14	  const { position } = state.player;
15	  return out.set(position.x, position.y + EYE_HEIGHT, position.z);
16	}
17	
18	export function selectedTool(state: GameState): InventorySlot | null {
19	  const slot = state.inventory[state.selectedSlot];
20	  return slot?.kind === "tool" && slot.count > 0 ? slot : null;
21	}
22	
23	export function canMineBlock(block: BlockId, toolTier: number): boolean {
24	  if (block === BlockId.Stone || block === BlockId.Cobblestone || block === BlockId.Brick) return toolTier >= 1;
25	  if (block === BlockId.SliverOre) return toolTier >= 2;
26	  if (block === BlockId.RubyOre) return toolTier >= 3;
27	  if (block === BlockId.GoldOre) return toolTier >= 3;
28	  if (block === BlockId.SapphireOre) return toolTier >= 4;
29	  if (block === BlockId.DiamondOre) return toolTier >= 4;
30	  return true;
31	}
32	
33	export function miningSpeed(tool: InventorySlot | null): number {
34	  return tool?.minePower ?? BARE_HAND_MINE_POWER;
35	}
36	
37	export function resetMining(state: GameState): void {
38	  state.mining.targetKey = "";
39	  state.mining.progress = 0;
40	}
41	
42	function addBlockDrop(state: GameState, block: BlockId, rng: () => number): void {
43	  for (const drop of rollBlockDrops(block, rng)) {
44	    state.inventory = adjustSlotCount(state.inventory, drop.itemId, drop.count) ?? state.inventory;
45	  }
46	}
47	
48	/** Advances mining progress while the mouse is held; breaks the block at full progress. */
49	export function tickMining(state: GameState, input: FrameInput, dt: number, emit: EmitGameEvent, rng: () => number): void {
50	  if (!input.leftMouseHeld) {
51	    // Releasing the button abandons progress (matching the crack overlay).
52	    if (state.mining.progress > 0) resetMining(state);
53	    return;
54	  }
55	  if (state.inventoryOpen || state.isDead || !input.pointerLocked) return;
56	
57	  const { world, mining } = state;
58	  const origin = eyePosition(state, scratchEye);
59	  const direction = lookDirection(state.player.yaw, state.player.pitch, scratchDir);
60	  const result = voxelRaycast(world, origin, direction, MINE_REACH);
61	  if (!result) {
62	    resetMining(state);
63	    return;
64	  }
65	
66	  const bx = result.hit.x;
67	  const by = result.hit.y;
68	  const bz = result.hit.z;
69	  const targetBlock = world.get(bx, by, bz);
70	  const tool = selectedTool(state);
71	  const tier = tool?.mineTier ?? 0;
72	
73	  if (targetBlock === BlockId.Bedrock || targetBlock === BlockId.Air || !canMineBlock(targetBlock as BlockId, tier)) {
74	    resetMining(state);
75	    return;
76	  }
77	
78	  const key = `${bx},${by},${bz}`;
79	  if (mining.targetKey !== key) {
80	    mining.targetKey = key;
81	    mining.progress = 0;
82	  }
83	
84	  const hardness = BREAK_HARDNESS[targetBlock as BlockId] ?? 2;
85	  mining.progress += dt * miningSpeed(tool) * MINING_RATE;
86	  if (mining.progress < hardness) return;
87	
88	  state.blockChanges.set(bx, by, bz, BlockId.Air);
89	  if (tool) state.inventory = consumeToolDurability(state.inventory, state.selectedSlot, 1) ?? state.inventory;
90	  addBlockDrop(state, targetBlock as BlockId, rng);
91	  state.worldMeshDirty = true;
92	  resetMining(state);
93	  emit({ type: "blockBroken", blockId: targetBlock as BlockId, x: bx, y: by, z: bz });
94	}
95	
96	/** Places the selected block against the targeted face, refusing self-entombment. */
97	export function placeSelectedBlock(state: GameState, emit: EmitGameEvent): void {
98	  const { world } = state;
99	  const origin = eyePosition(state, scratchEye);
100	  const direction = lookDirection(state.player.yaw, state.player.pitch, scratchDir);
101	  const result = voxelRaycast(world, origin, direction, MINE_REACH);
102	  if (!result) return;
103	
104	  const tx = result.previous.x;
105	  const ty = result.previous.y;
106	  const tz = result.previous.z;
107	  if (!world.inBounds(tx, ty, tz) || world.get(tx, ty, tz) !== BlockId.Air) return;
108	
109	  const slot = state.inventory[state.selectedSlot];
110	  if (!slot || !slot.id || slot.kind !== "block" || slot.count <= 0 || slot.blockId === undefined) return;
111	  if (slot.blockId === BlockId.Bedrock) return;
112	
113	  const afterTake = adjustSlotCount(state.inventory, slot.id, -1, state.selectedSlot);
114	  if (!afterTake) return;
115	  state.inventory = afterTake;
116	  state.blockChanges.set(tx, ty, tz, slot.blockId);
117	  if (collidesAt(world, state.player.position, PLAYER_HALF_WIDTH, PLAYER_HEIGHT)) {
118	    state.blockChanges.set(tx, ty, tz, BlockId.Air);
119	    state.inventory = adjustSlotCount(state.inventory, slot.id, 1, state.selectedSlot) ?? state.inventory;
120	    return;
121	  }
122	
123	  state.worldMeshDirty = true;
124	  emit({ type: "blockPlaced", blockId: slot.blockId, x: tx, y: ty, z: tz });
125	}
126

1	import { MAX_STACK_SIZE } from "@/lib/game/config";
2	import { ARMOR_SLOTS, createEmptySlot, createSlot, ITEM_DEF_BY_ID } from "@/lib/game/items";
3	import type { EquippedArmor, InventorySlot, Recipe } from "@/lib/game/types";
4	
5	/**
6	 * Pure inventory slot algebra. Every mutation returns a NEW slots array, or
7	 * null when the operation does not apply — callers keep the previous array on
8	 * null, so reference equality doubles as a change signal for React.
9	 */
10	
11	export function cloneSlots(slots: InventorySlot[]): InventorySlot[] {
12	  return slots.map((slot) => ({ ...slot }));
13	}
14	
15	export function countsById(slots: InventorySlot[]): Map<string, number> {
16	  const byId = new Map<string, number>();
17	  for (const slot of slots) {
18	    if (!slot.id || slot.count <= 0) continue;
19	    byId.set(slot.id, (byId.get(slot.id) ?? 0) + slot.count);
20	  }
21	  return byId;
22	}
23	
24	/**
25	 * Adds (delta > 0) or removes (delta < 0) items. Removal is all-or-nothing:
26	 * if the inventory holds fewer than |delta|, nothing is removed and null is
27	 * returned. Addition fills existing stacks, then empty slots; whatever does
28	 * not fit is dropped (a full inventory loses surplus block drops).
29	 */
30	export function adjustSlotCount(slots: InventorySlot[], slotId: string, delta: number, preferredIndex?: number): InventorySlot[] | null {
31	  if (!slotId || delta === 0) return null;
32	  const next = cloneSlots(slots);
33	  let remaining = Math.abs(delta);
34	
35	  if (delta < 0) {
36	    const consumeFromIndex = (index: number) => {
37	      if (remaining <= 0) return;
38	      if (index < 0 || index >= next.length) return;
39	      const slot = next[index];
40	      if (slot.id !== slotId || slot.count <= 0) return;
41	      const take = Math.min(remaining, slot.count);
42	      slot.count -= take;
43	      remaining -= take;
44	      if (slot.count <= 0) next[index] = createEmptySlot();
45	    };
46	
47	    if (typeof preferredIndex === "number") consumeFromIndex(preferredIndex);
48	    for (let i = 0; i < next.length && remaining > 0; i += 1) consumeFromIndex(i);
49	    if (remaining > 0) return null;
50	    return next;
51	  }
52	
53	  if (!ITEM_DEF_BY_ID[slotId]) return null;
54	
55	  const fillIndex = (index: number) => {
56	    if (remaining <= 0) return;
57	    if (index < 0 || index >= next.length) return;
58	    const slot = next[index];
59	    if (slot.id !== slotId || slot.count >= MAX_STACK_SIZE) return;
60	    const add = Math.min(remaining, MAX_STACK_SIZE - slot.count);
61	    slot.count += add;
62	    remaining -= add;
63	  };
64	
65	  if (typeof preferredIndex === "number") fillIndex(preferredIndex);
66	  for (let i = 0; i < next.length && remaining > 0; i += 1) fillIndex(i);
67	  for (let i = 0; i < next.length && remaining > 0; i += 1) {
68	    if (next[i].id !== null || next[i].count !== 0) continue;
69	    const add = Math.min(remaining, MAX_STACK_SIZE);
70	    next[i] = createSlot(slotId, add);
71	    remaining -= add;
72	  }
73	  return next;
74	}
75	
76	/** Wears the tool/weapon at `index` by `amount`; the slot empties at zero durability. */
77	export function consumeToolDurability(slots: InventorySlot[], index: number, amount = 1): InventorySlot[] | null {
78	  if (amount <= 0) return null;
79	  if (index < 0 || index >= slots.length) return null;
80	  const next = cloneSlots(slots);
81	  const slot = next[index];
82	  if ((slot.kind !== "tool" && slot.kind !== "weapon") || !slot.id || slot.count <= 0 || !slot.maxDurability) return null;
83	  const nextDurability = (slot.durability ?? slot.maxDurability) - amount;
84	  if (nextDurability <= 0) {
85	    next[index] = createEmptySlot();
86	    return next;
87	  }
88	  slot.durability = nextDurability;
89	  return next;
90	}
91	
92	/** Wears every equipped armor piece by `amount`; broken pieces disappear. */
93	export function consumeEquippedArmorDurability(slots: InventorySlot[], equipped: EquippedArmor, amount = 1): InventorySlot[] | null {
94	  if (amount <= 0) return null;
95	  const next = cloneSlots(slots);
96	  let changed = false;
97	  for (const armorSlot of ARMOR_SLOTS) {
98	    const equippedId = equipped[armorSlot];
99	    if (!equippedId) continue;
100	    const idx = next.findIndex((slot) => slot.id === equippedId && slot.kind === "armor" && slot.count > 0);
101	    if (idx < 0) continue;
102	    const slot = next[idx];
103	    if (!slot.maxDurability) continue;
104	    const nextDurability = (slot.durability ?? slot.maxDurability) - amount;
105	    if (nextDurability <= 0) next[idx] = createEmptySlot();
106	    else slot.durability = nextDurability;
107	    changed = true;
108	  }
109	  return changed ? next : null;
110	}
111	
112	export function canCraft(slots: InventorySlot[], recipe: Recipe): boolean {
113	  const byId = countsById(slots);
114	  const hasCost = recipe.cost.every((cost) => (byId.get(cost.slotId) ?? 0) >= cost.count);
115	  if (!hasCost) return false;
116	
117	  let freeForResult = 0;
118	  for (const slot of slots) {
119	    if (slot.id === recipe.result.slotId) freeForResult += MAX_STACK_SIZE - slot.count;
120	    if (slot.id === null && slot.count === 0) freeForResult += MAX_STACK_SIZE;
121	  }
122	  return freeForResult >= recipe.result.count;
123	}
124	
125	/**
126	 * Consumes the recipe cost and adds the result. Returns null unless the cost
127	 * is affordable AND the result fits — crafting never silently destroys the
128	 * overflow (this previously could happen when the inventory was full).
129	 */
130	export function craft(slots: InventorySlot[], recipe: Recipe): InventorySlot[] | null {
131	  if (!ITEM_DEF_BY_ID[recipe.result.slotId]) return null;
132	  if (!canCraft(slots, recipe)) return null;
133	
134	  const next = cloneSlots(slots);
135	  for (const cost of recipe.cost) {
136	    let remaining = cost.count;
137	    for (let i = 0; i < next.length && remaining > 0; i += 1) {
138	      if (next[i].id !== cost.slotId || next[i].count <= 0) continue;
139	      const take = Math.min(remaining, next[i].count);
140	      next[i].count -= take;
141	      remaining -= take;
142	      if (next[i].count <= 0) next[i] = createEmptySlot();
143	    }
144	  }
145	
146	  let remaining = recipe.result.count;
147	  for (let i = 0; i < next.length && remaining > 0; i += 1) {
148	    if (next[i].id !== recipe.result.slotId || next[i].count >= MAX_STACK_SIZE) continue;
149	    const add = Math.min(remaining, MAX_STACK_SIZE - next[i].count);
150	    next[i].count += add;
151	    remaining -= add;
152	  }
153	  for (let i = 0; i < next.length && remaining > 0; i += 1) {
154	    if (next[i].id !== null || next[i].count !== 0) continue;
155	    const add = Math.min(remaining, MAX_STACK_SIZE);
156	    next[i] = createSlot(recipe.result.slotId, add);
157	    remaining -= add;
158	  }
159	  return next;
160	}
161	
162	export function swapSlots(slots: InventorySlot[], fromIndex: number, toIndex: number): InventorySlot[] | null {
163	  if (fromIndex === toIndex) return null;
164	  if (fromIndex < 0 || toIndex < 0 || fromIndex >= slots.length || toIndex >= slots.length) return null;
165	  const next = cloneSlots(slots);
166	  const temp = next[fromIndex];
167	  next[fromIndex] = next[toIndex];
168	  next[toIndex] = temp;
169	  return next;
170	}
171	
172	/** Toggles the armor piece at `index` in its armor slot. */
173	export function toggleEquipArmor(slots: InventorySlot[], equipped: EquippedArmor, index: number): EquippedArmor | null {
174	  if (index < 0 || index >= slots.length) return null;
175	  const slot = slots[index];
176	  if (slot.kind !== "armor" || !slot.id || !slot.armorSlot || slot.count <= 0) return null;
177	  const next = { ...equipped };
178	  next[slot.armorSlot] = equipped[slot.armorSlot] === slot.id ? null : slot.id;
179	  return next;
180	}
181	
182	/** Unequips armor pieces no longer present in the inventory (broken or dropped). */
183	export function unequipMissingArmor(slots: InventorySlot[], equipped: EquippedArmor): EquippedArmor | null {
184	  let changed = false;
185	  const next = { ...equipped };
186	  for (const armorSlot of ARMOR_SLOTS) {
187	    const equippedId = next[armorSlot];
188	    if (!equippedId) continue;
189	    const stillOwned = slots.some((slot) => slot.id === equippedId && slot.count > 0);
190	    if (stillOwned) continue;
191	    next[armorSlot] = null;
192	    changed = true;
193	  }
194	  return changed ? next : null;
195	}
196	
197	/** Total defense points from equipped, still-owned armor (drives the HUD armor bar). */
198	export function equippedDefense(slots: InventorySlot[], equipped: EquippedArmor): number {
199	  let defense = 0;
200	  for (const armorSlot of ARMOR_SLOTS) {
201	    const equippedId = equipped[armorSlot];
202	    if (!equippedId) continue;
203	    const def = ITEM_DEF_BY_ID[equippedId];
204	    if (!def || def.kind !== "armor" || def.armorSlot !== armorSlot) continue;
205	    const hasOwnedPiece = slots.some((slot) => slot.id === equippedId && slot.count > 0);
206	    if (!hasOwnedPiece) continue;
207	    defense += def.defense ?? 0;
208	  }
209	  return defense;
210	}
211	
212	/** Damage reduction from equipped, still-owned armor: 5% per defense point, capped at 75%. */
213	export function armorReduction(slots: InventorySlot[], equipped: EquippedArmor): number {
214	  return Math.min(0.75, equippedDefense(slots, equipped) * 0.05);
215	}
216	
```

          "label": "Must empty first",

          "label": "Destroy contents",
          "description": "Breaking a chest discards whatever is inside (like a careless real-Minecraft break with no hopper). Simplest, but risks unhappy accidental data loss."

{

  "content": "# Plan: Chests & persistent storage (block-entity system)\n\n## Context\n\nThe voxel game has shipped a UI pass and a juice/atmosphere pass; three roadmap directions remain (caves/block-light, exploration/loot, endgame/ranged). The user picked **persistent storage (chests)** as the next feature.\n\nThis is the most-felt foundational gap: right now *everything* a player owns rides in 36 inventory slots on their person, with nowhere to stash anything. Blocks are bare `BlockId`s — there is **no per-block metadata system** — so a chest's contents need a new, small \"block-entity\" layer. That layer is what later makes dungeon loot and boss rewards have somewhere to live, so building it now is foundational, not throwaway.\n\nScope: **single 27-slot chests only**. No worldgen change (so `SAVE_KEY` is unchanged and v1/v2/v3 saves still load) — only an **additive save `version` bump 3 → 4**. Dungeons that scatter pre-filled chests are an explicit fast-follow, not part of this slice.\n\n### Settled design decisions\n- **Capacity:** 27 slots (9×3), rendered beside the existing 27-slot player storage grid.\n- **Breaking a chest with items in it:** spill its contents into the player inventory (preserving tool/armor durability), return the `chest` item, and emit a `pickedUp` toast for what was retrieved. If there isn't room for everything, **refuse the break** (chest stays intact) and show a toast — consistent with the game's no-ground-item loot model.\n\n## Design overview\n\nA **block-entity** is a chest's contents: `InventorySlot[]` of length `CHEST_SLOTS`, keyed by the block's voxel index (`world.index(x,y,z)` — the same index space as the block-diff save). Held in `state.containers: Map<number, InventorySlot[]>`.\n\nReuses that already exist and should be leaned on:\n- Right-click interact precedence — `lib/game/engine/systems/interact.ts` (`INTERACTIVE_BLOCKS`, `tryInteractBlock`); bed & furnace are the precedent.\n- \"Open a panel from a block\" state pattern — furnace sets `inventoryOpen=true` + `craftingStation`; closing the panel clears it (`GameEngine.dispatch` `toggleInventory`/`pause`, lines 214/282). Chests mirror this with `openContainerIndex`.\n- Pure slot algebra — `lib/game/inventory.ts` (`swapSlots`, `adjustSlotCount`, `cloneSlots`, `createEmptySlot`). Cross-array move and break-spill are new pure helpers built in the same style.\n- Save restore/validation — `lib/game/save.ts` (`restoreInventorySlots`, `inventorySlotsSnapshot`); container persistence reuses the same per-slot snapshot shape `{id,count,durability}` and validation.\n- Generic block break/place particles already tint from `BLOCK_COLORS`, so chest place/break get particle bursts for free.\n\n## Changes\n\n### 1. Content (block + item + recipe + atlas)\n- `lib/world/blocks.ts`: add `BlockId.Chest = 27` (after `Furnace = 26`). Add wood-brown entries to `BLOCK_COLORS` and `HELD_BLOCK_COLORS`.\n- `lib/world/atlas.ts`: add a Chest tile — wood box with a lid seam on top and a latch on the front face, following the **furnace special-front-face** approach already in this file.\n- `lib/game/items.ts`: add `ItemDef { id:\"chest\", label:\"Chest\", kind:\"block\", blockId: BlockId.Chest }`; add `BREAK_HARDNESS[Chest]` (wood-like, ~2); add the Chest → `chest` entry to the block-drop table (`rollBlockDrops`).\n- `lib/game/recipes.ts`: `{ id:\"chest\", label:\"Chest\", cost:[{slotId:\"planks\", count:8}], result:{slotId:\"chest\", count:1} }` (basic grid, no station).\n- `config.ts`: new tunable `CHEST_SLOTS = 27`.\n\n### 2. Engine state & systems\n- `lib/game/engine/state.ts`:\n  - `GameState`: add `containers: Map<number, InventorySlot[]>` and `openContainerIndex: number | null`.\n  - `GameSnapshot`: add `container: InventorySlot[] | null` (the open chest's slots, else null).\n  - `GameEvent`: add `{ type:\"openedContainer\" }` and `{ type:\"breakBlocked\"; reason:\"containerFull\" }`.\n- `lib/game/engine/systems/interact.ts`:\n  - Extend `InteractiveKind` with `\"chest\"` and add `[BlockId.Chest]:\"chest\"` to `INTERACTIVE_BLOCKS`.\n  - `interactChest(state, emit, idx)`: if `!state.containers.has(idx)` set a fresh empty container (`Array.from({length: CHEST_SLOTS}, createEmptySlot)`); set `state.openContainerIndex = idx`, `state.inventoryOpen = true`; emit `openedContainer`. (Lazy-create handles emptied chests and any chest missing from `blockEntities`.)\n- `lib/game/engine/systems/mining.ts`:\n  - `placeSelectedBlock`: after a Chest placement commits successfully, `state.containers.set(world.index(tx,ty,tz), emptyContainer())`.\n  - `tickMining` break path: when `targetBlock === BlockId.Chest`, before setting Air, attempt to spill via the new `tryInsertSlots` helper. On success → set inventory, `state.containers.delete(idx)`, proceed with break + `chest` drop, and emit `pickedUp` for spilled items. On failure (no room) → **abort the break** (do not set Air, reset progress) and emit `breakBlocked`.\n- `lib/game/engine/commands.ts` + `GameEngine.dispatch`:\n  - New command `{ type:\"moveStack\"; from:number; to:number }`. Indices `>= CONTAINER_SLOT_BASE` (e.g. 1000) address the open container (`index - BASE`); otherwise the player inventory. Decode both refs and apply a unified pure swap; write back `state.inventory` and the container array in `state.containers`.\n  - In `toggleInventory` and `pause`, also clear `openContainerIndex` whenever the panel closes (mirror the existing `craftingStation = null`).\n- `GameEngine` constructor: initialize `containers: new Map()`, `openContainerIndex: null`; after `applySavedChanges`, call `restoreContainers(save)` to rebuild the map. `buildSnapshot`: set `container = openContainerIndex !== null ? containers.get(openContainerIndex) ?? null : null`.\n\n### 3. Pure inventory helpers (`lib/game/inventory.ts`)\n- `tryInsertSlots(inventory, slots): InventorySlot[] | null` — all-or-nothing insert of whole slot objects: merge stackables into existing/empty slots (reuse the `adjustSlotCount` add pattern) and place durability items as whole slots, preserving `durability`. Returns null if it doesn't all fit. Used by the break-spill.\n- `moveStack(invA, idxA, invB, idxB): { a, b } | null` — generalizes `swapSlots` across two arrays (same array → in-place swap). Used by the `moveStack` command.\n\n### 4. Save format (additive, version 3 → 4)\n- `lib/game/types.ts`: `SaveData.version: 4`; add optional `blockEntities?: Array<{ index:number; slots: Array<{id:string|null; count:number; durability?:number}> }>`. Add `SaveDataV3` alias for the prior shape.\n- `lib/game/save.ts`:\n  - `migrateSaveV3toV4` — pure version bump (field stays absent → restores as empty containers).\n  - `readSave`: chain v2→v3→v4; accept only `version === 4`.\n  - `serializeContainers(containers)` — emit only **non-empty** containers via `inventorySlotsSnapshot`.\n  - `restoreContainers(save)` — rebuild the map, keeping only entries whose voxel index actually holds `BlockId.Chest` (after `applySavedChanges`) and validating each slot like `restoreInventorySlots` (drop unknown ids, clamp counts/durability).\n- `GameEngine.serialize()`: `version: 4`, add `blockEntities: serializeContainers(state.containers)`.\n- `SAVE_KEY` unchanged (`minecraft_save_v5`) — no worldgen change, so the generation hash tests are **not** re-baselined.\n\n### 5. React shell & UI\n- `components/game/InventoryPanel.tsx`: accept `container: InventorySlot[] | null` and `onMoveStack(from, to)`. When `container` is non-null, render a \"Chest\" grid (27 slots) above the player storage; route slot clicks through `onMoveStack` using the `CONTAINER_SLOT_BASE` offset for container slots (covers inv↔inv, inv↔chest, chest↔chest). When null, keep the existing `onSwapSlots` path untouched.\n- `lib/game/useMinecraftGame.ts`: thread `snapshot.container` into the panel; add the `onMoveStack` dispatcher; surface `breakBlocked` (and the spill `pickedUp`) through the existing toast component.\n- `GameRenderer`/audio `handleEvent`: optional `openedContainer` creak SFX (reuse the synthesized open-station cue); particles already covered generically.\n\n### 6. Tests (ship with the change)\n- `inventory.test.ts`: `tryInsertSlots` (all-or-nothing, durability preserved, stack-merge, overflow → null) and `moveStack` across arrays.\n- `save.test.ts`: v4 round-trip with `blockEntities`; v3→v4 migration (old save loads with empty containers); `restoreContainers` drops entries whose block isn't a Chest and invalid slots.\n- `GameEngine.test.ts`: place chest → container created; open chest → `openContainerIndex` set + `inventoryOpen`; break empty chest → `chest` drop; break full chest → spills to inventory + container deleted + `pickedUp`; break full chest with full inventory → refused (block intact) + `breakBlocked`; `moveStack` inv↔container.\n- `InventoryPanel` component test: chest grid renders when `container` present; click inv slot then chest slot calls `onMoveStack`.\n- Update sprite/atlas integrity test for the new Chest tile if it enumerates tiles.\n\n### 7. Docs & changelog (the docs gate — update with the change)\n- `docs/save-format.md` — v4 schema + `blockEntities`, migration note, `SAVE_KEY` unchanged.\n- `docs/architecture.md` — block-entity/container concept, `containers` map + serialize, interact precedence now bed/furnace/**chest**.\n- `docs/adding-content.md` — a short \"adding a container / block-entity block\" worked example using the chest.\n- `docs/reference.md` — recipe table (Chest = 8 planks), block table (Chest: hardness, no tool gate), item entry.\n- `docs/tuning.md` — `CHEST_SLOTS` tunable (which system reads it).\n- `docs/manual.md` — storage section: craft (8 planks) → place → right-click to open → move items → breaking spills into your inventory.\n- `README.md` — controls: right-click a chest to open it.\n- `CHANGELOG.md` — `[Unreleased] / Added` entry, flagging the additive save bump (v3 → v4) and **no worldgen impact**.\n\n## Verification\n\n1. `bun run lint`, `bun run typecheck`, `bun run format:check`, `bun test`, `bun run build` — the full CI `verify` list, all green.\n2. `bun run test:e2e` — this touches the React shell, input, and renderer, so the e2e gate applies. Extend the smoke suite (driven via `window.__monecraft`) to craft a chest, place it, open it, move an item in, and confirm it round-trips through a save/reload.\n3. Manual gameplay pass: craft a chest from 8 planks, place it, right-click to open, move items both ways, wait for autosave (or reload), confirm contents persist; break a chest with items and confirm they spill into the inventory; fill the inventory and confirm a full chest refuses to break with a toast.\n4. Confirm a pre-feature save (v3) still loads (chests simply absent), proving the migration is additive.\n\n## Out of scope (fast-follows)\n- Dungeons / pre-filled loot chests (worldgen pass — would bump `SAVE_KEY` and re-baseline the worldgen hash).\n- Double (54-slot) chests.\n- Stack-merge on click-move (kept as plain swap to match the existing single-array UX).\n"
}

Add wood-brown entries to `BLOCK_COLORS` and `HELD_BLOCK_COLORS`.\n- `lib/world/atlas.ts`: add a Chest tile — wood box with a lid seam on top and a latch on the front face, following the **furnace special-front-face** approach already in this file.\n- `lib/game/items.ts`: add `ItemDef { id:\"chest\", label:\"Chest\", kind:\"block\", blockId: BlockId.Chest }`; add `BREAK_HARDNESS[Chest]` (wood-like, ~2); add the Chest → `chest` entry to the block-drop table (`rollBlockDrops`).\n- `lib/game/recipes.ts`: `{ id:\"chest\", label:\"Chest\", cost:[{slotId:\"planks\", count:8}], result:{slotId:\"chest\", count:1} }` (basic grid, no station).\n- `config.ts`: new tunable `CHEST_SLOTS = 27`.\n\n### 2. Engine state & systems\n- `lib/game/engine/state.ts`:\n  - `GameState`: add `containers: Map<number, InventorySlot[]>` and `openContainerIndex: number | null`.\n  - `GameSnapshot`: add `container: InventorySlot[] | null` (the open chest's slots, else null).\n  - `GameEvent`: add `{ type:\"openedContainer\" }` and `{ type:\"breakBlocked\"; reason:\"containerFull\" }`.\n- `lib/game/engine/systems/interact.ts`:\n  - Extend `InteractiveKind` with `\"chest\"` and add `[BlockId.Chest]:\"chest\"` to `INTERACTIVE_BLOCKS`.\n  - `interactChest(state, emit, idx)`: if `!state.containers.has(idx)` set a fresh empty container (`Array.from({length: CHEST_SLOTS}, createEmptySlot)`); set `state.openContainerIndex = idx`, `state.inventoryOpen = true`; emit `openedContainer`. (Lazy-create handles emptied chests and any chest missing from `blockEntities`.)\n- `lib/game/engine/systems/mining.ts`:\n  - `placeSelectedBlock`: after a Chest placement commits successfully, `state.containers.set(world.index(tx,ty,tz), emptyContainer())`.\n  - `tickMining` break path: when `targetBlock === BlockId.Chest`, before setting Air, attempt to spill via the new `tryInsertSlots` helper. On success → set inventory, `state.containers.delete(idx)`, proceed with break + `chest` drop, and emit `pickedUp` for spilled items. On failure (no room) → **abort the break** (do not set Air, reset progress) and emit `breakBlocked`.\n- `lib/game/engine/commands.ts` + `GameEngine.dispatch`:\n  - New command `{ type:\"moveStack\"; from:number; to:number }`. Indices `>= CONTAINER_SLOT_BASE` (e.g. 1000) address the open container (`index - BASE`); otherwise the player inventory. Decode both refs and apply a unified pure swap; write back `state.inventory` and the container array in `state.containers`.\n  - In `toggleInventory` and `pause`, also clear `openContainerIndex` whenever the panel closes (mirror the existing `craftingStation = null`).\n- `GameEngine` constructor: initialize `containers: new Map()`, `openContainerIndex: null`; after `applySavedChanges`, call `restoreContainers(save)` to rebuild the map. `buildSnapshot`: set `container = openContainerIndex !== null ? containers.get(openContainerIndex) ?? null : null`.\n\n### 3. Pure inventory helpers (`lib/game/inventory.ts`)\n- `tryInsertSlots(inventory, slots): InventorySlot[] | null` — all-or-nothing insert of whole slot objects: merge stackables into existing/empty slots (reuse the `adjustSlotCount` add pattern) and place durability items as whole slots, preserving `durability`. Returns null if it doesn't all fit. Used by the break-spill.\n- `moveStack(invA, idxA, invB, idxB): { a, b } | null` — generalizes `swapSlots` across two arrays (same array → in-place swap). Used by the `moveStack` command.\n\n### 4. Save format (additive, version 3 → 4)\n- `lib/game/types.ts`: `SaveData.version: 4`; add optional `blockEntities?: Array<{ index:number; slots: Array<{id:string|null; count:number; durability?:number}> }>`. Add `SaveDataV3` alias for the prior shape.\n- `lib/game/save.ts`:\n  - `migrateSaveV3toV4` — pure version bump (field stays absent → restores as empty containers).\n  - `readSave`: chain v2→v3→v4; accept only `version === 4`.\n  - `serializeContainers(containers)` — emit only **non-empty** containers via `inventorySlotsSnapshot`.\n  - `restoreContainers(save)` — rebuild the map, keeping only entries whose voxel index actually holds `BlockId.Chest` (after `applySavedChanges`) and validating each slot like `restoreInventorySlots` (drop unknown ids, clamp counts/durability).\n- `GameEngine.serialize()`: `version: 4`, add `blockEntities: serializeContainers(state.containers)`.\n- `SAVE_KEY` unchanged (`minecraft_save_v5`) — no worldgen change, so the generation hash tests are **not** re-baselined.\n\n### 5. React shell & UI\n- `components/game/InventoryPanel.tsx`: accept `container: InventorySlot[] | null` and `onMoveStack(from, to)`. When `container` is non-null, render a \"Chest\" grid (27 slots) above the player storage; route slot clicks through `onMoveStack` using the `CONTAINER_SLOT_BASE` offset for container slots (covers inv↔inv, inv↔chest, chest↔chest). When null, keep the existing `onSwapSlots` path untouched.\n- `lib/game/useMinecraftGame.ts`: thread `snapshot.container` into the panel; add the `onMoveStack` dispatcher; surface `breakBlocked` (and the spill `pickedUp`) through the existing toast component.\n- `GameRenderer`/audio `handleEvent`: optional `openedContainer` creak SFX (reuse the synthesized open-station cue); particles already covered generically.\n\n### 6. Tests (ship with the change)\n- `inventory.test.ts`: `tryInsertSlots` (all-or-nothing, durability preserved, stack-merge, overflow → null) and `moveStack` across arrays.\n- `save.test.ts`: v4 round-trip with `blockEntities`; v3→v4 migration (old save loads with empty containers); `restoreContainers` drops entries whose block isn't a Chest and invalid slots.\n- `GameEngine.test.ts`: place chest → container created; open chest → `openContainerIndex` set + `inventoryOpen`; break empty chest → `chest` drop; break full chest → spills to inventory + container deleted + `pickedUp`; break full chest with full inventory → refused (block intact) + `breakBlocked`; `moveStack` inv↔container.\n- `InventoryPanel` component test: chest grid renders when `container` present; click inv slot then chest slot calls `onMoveStack`.\n- Update sprite/atlas integrity test for the new Chest tile if it enumerates tiles.\n\n### 7. Docs & changelog (the docs gate — update with the change)\n- `docs/save-format.md` — v4 schema + `blockEntities`, migration note, `SAVE_KEY` unchanged.\n- `docs/architecture.md` — block-entity/container concept, `containers` map + serialize, interact precedence now bed/furnace/**chest**.\n- `docs/adding-content.md` — a short \"adding a container / block-entity block\" worked example using the chest.\n- `docs/reference.md` — recipe table (Chest = 8 planks), block table (Chest: hardness, no tool gate), item entry.\n- `docs/tuning.md` — `CHEST_SLOTS` tunable (which system reads it).\n- `docs/manual.md` — storage section: craft (8 planks) → place → right-click to open → move items → breaking spills into your inventory.\n- `README.md` — controls: right-click a chest to open it.\n- `CHANGELOG.md` — `[Unreleased] / Added` entry, flagging the additive save bump (v3 → v4) and **no worldgen impact**.\n\n## Verification\n\n1. `bun run lint`, `bun run typecheck`, `bun run format:check`, `bun test`, `bun run build` — the full CI `verify` list, all green.\n2. `bun run test:e2e` — this touches the React shell, input, and renderer, so the e2e gate applies. ## Approved Plan:
# Plan: Chests & persistent storage (block-entity system)

## Context

The voxel game has shipped a UI pass and a juice/atmosphere pass; three roadmap directions remain (caves/block-light, exploration/loot, endgame/ranged). The user picked **persistent storage (chests)** as the next feature.

This is the most-felt foundational gap: right now *everything* a player owns rides in 36 inventory slots on their person, with nowhere to stash anything. Blocks are bare `BlockId`s — there is **no per-block metadata system** — so a chest's contents need a new, small "block-entity" layer. That layer is what later makes dungeon loot and boss rewards have somewhere to live, so building it now is foundational, not throwaway.

Scope: **single 27-slot chests only**. No worldgen change (so `SAVE_KEY` is unchanged and v1/v2/v3 saves still load) — only an **additive save `version` bump 3 → 4**. Dungeons that scatter pre-filled chests are an explicit fast-follow, not part of this slice.

### Settled design decisions
- **Capacity:** 27 slots (9×3), rendered beside the existing 27-slot player storage grid.
- **Breaking a chest with items in it:** spill its contents into the player inventory (preserving tool/armor durability), return the `chest` item, and emit a `pickedUp` toast for what was retrieved. If there isn't room for everything, **refuse the break** (chest stays intact) and show a toast — consistent with the game's no-ground-item loot model.

## Design overview

A **block-entity** is a chest's contents: `InventorySlot[]` of length `CHEST_SLOTS`, keyed by the block's voxel index (`world.index(x,y,z)` — the same index space as the block-diff save). Held in `state.containers: Map<number, InventorySlot[]>`.

Reuses that already exist and should be leaned on:
- Right-click interact precedence — `lib/game/engine/systems/interact.ts` (`INTERACTIVE_BLOCKS`, `tryInteractBlock`); bed & furnace are the precedent.
- "Open a panel from a block" state pattern — furnace sets `inventoryOpen=true` + `craftingStation`; closing the panel clears it (`GameEngine.dispatch` `toggleInventory`/`pause`, lines 214/282). Chests mirror this with `openContainerIndex`.
- Pure slot algebra — `lib/game/inventory.ts` (`swapSlots`, `adjustSlotCount`, `cloneSlots`, `createEmptySlot`). Cross-array move and break-spill are new pure helpers built in the same style.
- Save restore/validation — `lib/game/save.ts` (`restoreInventorySlots`, `inventorySlotsSnapshot`); container persistence reuses the same per-slot snapshot shape `{id,count,durability}` and validation.
- Generic block break/place particles already tint from `BLOCK_COLORS`, so chest place/break get particle bursts for free.

## Changes

### 1. Content (block + item + recipe + atlas)
- `lib/world/blocks.ts`: add `BlockId.Chest = 27` (after `Furnace = 26`). Add wood-brown entries to `BLOCK_COLORS` and `HELD_BLOCK_COLORS`.
- `lib/world/atlas.ts`: add a Chest tile — wood box with a lid seam on top and a latch on the front face, following the **furnace special-front-face** approach already in this file.
- `lib/game/items.ts`: add `ItemDef { id:"chest", label:"Chest", kind:"block", blockId: BlockId.Chest }`; add `BREAK_HARDNESS[Chest]` (wood-like, ~2); add the Chest → `chest` entry to the block-drop table (`rollBlockDrops`).
- `lib/game/recipes.ts`: `{ id:"chest", label:"Chest", cost:[{slotId:"planks", count:8}], result:{slotId:"chest", count:1} }` (basic grid, no station).
- `config.ts`: new tunable `CHEST_SLOTS = 27`.

### 2. Engine state & systems
- `lib/game/engine/state.ts`:
  - `GameState`: add `containers: Map<number, InventorySlot[]>` and `openContainerIndex: number | null`.
  - `GameSnapshot`: add `container: InventorySlot[] | null` (the open chest's slots, else null).
  - `GameEvent`: add `{ type:"openedContainer" }` and `{ type:"breakBlocked"; reason:"containerFull" }`.
- `lib/game/engine/systems/interact.ts`:
  - Extend `InteractiveKind` with `"chest"` and add `[BlockId.Chest]:"chest"` to `INTERACTIVE_BLOCKS`.
  - `interactChest(state, emit, idx)`: if `!state.containers.has(idx)` set a fresh empty container (`Array.from({length: CHEST_SLOTS}, createEmptySlot)`); set `state.openContainerIndex = idx`, `state.inventoryOpen = true`; emit `openedContainer`. (Lazy-create handles emptied chests and any chest missing from `blockEntities`.)
- `lib/game/engine/systems/mining.ts`:
  - `placeSelectedBlock`: after a Chest placement commits successfully, `state.containers.set(world.index(tx,ty,tz), emptyContainer())`.
  - `tickMining` break path: when `targetBlock === BlockId.Chest`, before setting Air, attempt to spill via the new `tryInsertSlots` helper. On success → set inventory, `state.containers.delete(idx)`, proceed with break + `chest` drop, and emit `pickedUp` for spilled items. On failure (no room) → **abort the break** (do not set Air, reset progress) and emit `breakBlocked`.
- `lib/game/engine/commands.ts` + `GameEngine.dispatch`:
  - New command `{ type:"moveStack"; from:number; to:number }`. Indices `>= CONTAINER_SLOT_BASE` (e.g. 1000) address the open container (`index - BASE`); otherwise the player inventory. Decode both refs and apply a unified pure swap; write back `state.inventory` and the container array in `state.containers`.
  - In `toggleInventory` and `pause`, also clear `openContainerIndex` whenever the panel closes (mirror the existing `craftingStation = null`).
- `GameEngine` constructor: initialize `containers: new Map()`, `openContainerIndex: null`; after `applySavedChanges`, call `restoreContainers(save)` to rebuild the map. `buildSnapshot`: set `container = openContainerIndex !== null ? containers.get(openContainerIndex) ?? null : null`.

### 3. Pure inventory helpers (`lib/game/inventory.ts`)
- `tryInsertSlots(inventory, slots): InventorySlot[] | null` — all-or-nothing insert of whole slot objects: merge stackables into existing/empty slots (reuse the `adjustSlotCount` add pattern) and place durability items as whole slots, preserving `durability`. Returns null if it doesn't all fit. Used by the break-spill.
- `moveStack(invA, idxA, invB, idxB): { a, b } | null` — generalizes `swapSlots` across two arrays (same array → in-place swap). Used by the `moveStack` command.

### 4. Save format (additive, version 3 → 4)
- `lib/game/types.ts`: `SaveData.version: 4`; add optional `blockEntities?: Array<{ index:number; slots: Array<{id:string|null; count:number; durability?:number}> }>`. Add `SaveDataV3` alias for the prior shape.
- `lib/game/save.ts`:
  - `migrateSaveV3toV4` — pure version bump (field stays absent → restores as empty containers).
  - `readSave`: chain v2→v3→v4; accept only `version === 4`.
  - `serializeContainers(containers)` — emit only **non-empty** containers via `inventorySlotsSnapshot`.
  - `restoreContainers(save)` — rebuild the map, keeping only entries whose voxel index actually holds `BlockId.Chest` (after `applySavedChanges`) and validating each slot like `restoreInventorySlots` (drop unknown ids, clamp counts/durability).
- `GameEngine.serialize()`: `version: 4`, add `blockEntities: serializeContainers(state.containers)`.
- `SAVE_KEY` unchanged (`minecraft_save_v5`) — no worldgen change, so the generation hash tests are **not** re-baselined.

### 5. React shell & UI
- `components/game/InventoryPanel.tsx`: accept `container: InventorySlot[] | null` and `onMoveStack(from, to)`. When `container` is non-null, render a "Chest" grid (27 slots) above the player storage; route slot clicks through `onMoveStack` using the `CONTAINER_SLOT_BASE` offset for container slots (covers inv↔inv, inv↔chest, chest↔chest). When null, keep the existing `onSwapSlots` path untouched.
- `lib/game/useMinecraftGame.ts`: thread `snapshot.container` into the panel; add the `onMoveStack` dispatcher; surface `breakBlocked` (and the spill `pickedUp`) through the existing toast component.
- `GameRenderer`/audio `handleEvent`: optional `openedContainer` creak SFX (reuse the synthesized open-station cue); particles already covered generically.

### 6. Tests (ship with the change)
- `inventory.test.ts`: `tryInsertSlots` (all-or-nothing, durability preserved, stack-merge, overflow → null) and `moveStack` across arrays.
- `save.test.ts`: v4 round-trip with `blockEntities`; v3→v4 migration (old save loads with empty containers); `restoreContainers` drops entries whose block isn't a Chest and invalid slots.
- `GameEngine.test.ts`: place chest → container created; open chest → `openContainerIndex` set + `inventoryOpen`; break empty chest → `chest` drop; break full chest → spills to inventory + container deleted + `pickedUp`; break full chest with full inventory → refused (block intact) + `breakBlocked`; `moveStack` inv↔container.
- `InventoryPanel` component test: chest grid renders when `container` present; click inv slot then chest slot calls `onMoveStack`.
- Update sprite/atlas integrity test for the new Chest tile if it enumerates tiles.

### 7. Docs & changelog (the docs gate — update with the change)
- `docs/save-format.md` — v4 schema + `blockEntities`, migration note, `SAVE_KEY` unchanged.
- `docs/architecture.md` — block-entity/container concept, `containers` map + serialize, interact precedence now bed/furnace/**chest**.
- `docs/adding-content.md` — a short "adding a container / block-entity block" worked example using the chest.
- `docs/reference.md` — recipe table (Chest = 8 planks), block table (Chest: hardness, no tool gate), item entry.
- `docs/tuning.md` — `CHEST_SLOTS` tunable (which system reads it).
- `docs/manual.md` — storage section: craft (8 planks) → place → right-click to open → move items → breaking spills into your inventory.
- `README.md` — controls: right-click a chest to open it.
- `CHANGELOG.md` — `[Unreleased] / Added` entry, flagging the additive save bump (v3 → v4) and **no worldgen impact**.

## Branch, commits & PR

Per AGENTS.md (multi-file change + save bump): work on a `feat/chests-storage` branch off `main`, Conventional Commits, each commit independently lint/build-green so the branch stays bisectable, no `Co-Authored-By`/AI-attribution trailers, a body on each commit explaining the *why*. Proposed slices:

1. `feat(world): add Chest block, item, recipe, and atlas tile` — registry/content only (`BlockId.Chest`, colors, atlas tile, item def + drop + hardness, recipe, `CHEST_SLOTS`); inert until wired.
2. `feat(inventory): add tryInsertSlots and cross-array moveStack helpers` — pure helpers + unit tests.
3. `feat(save): persist chest block-entities (save v4)` — `SaveData` v4, migration, pure serialize/restore + save tests.
4. `feat(engine): chests — place, open, move, break-spill` — state fields, interact/mining hooks, `moveStack` command, engine wiring + engine tests (the behavioral core).
5. `feat(ui): chest container grid in the inventory panel` — panel grid, shell wiring, toasts, component test.
6. `docs: chests, block-entities, save v4 + e2e smoke` — docs/CHANGELOG + the e2e extension (directly-coupled doc edits may instead fold into their slice).

Docs and tests ship **in this same PR**, not a later one. After all slices are in and the full verify suite is green, push the branch and open the PR with `gh pr create --repo hutusi/monecraft --base main` (no AI-attribution in the body) — confirming the branch/commits first since it's outward-facing.

## Verification

1. `bun run lint`, `bun run typecheck`, `bun run format:check`, `bun test`, `bun run build` — the full CI `verify` list, all green.
2. `bun run test:e2e` — this touches the React shell, input, and renderer, so the e2e gate applies. Extend the smoke suite (driven via `window.__monecraft`) to craft a chest, place it, open it, move an item in, and confirm it round-trips through a save/reload.
3. Manual gameplay pass: craft a chest from 8 planks, place it, right-click to open, move items both ways, wait for autosave (or reload), confirm contents persist; break a chest with items and confirm they spill into the inventory; fill the inventory and confirm a full chest refuses to break with a toast.
4. Confirm a pre-feature save (v3) still loads (chests simply absent), proving the migration is additive.

## Out of scope (fast-follows)
- Dungeons / pre-filled loot chests (worldgen pass — would bump `SAVE_KEY` and re-baseline the worldgen hash).
- Double (54-slot) chests.
- Stack-merge on click-move (kept as plain swap to match the existing single-array UX).
