111	  { id: "boots", label: "Boots", kind: "armor", armorSlot: "boots", defense: 2, maxDurability: 250 }
112	];
113	
114	export const ITEM_DEF_BY_ID: Record<string, ItemDef> = Object.fromEntries(ITEM_DEFS.map((item) => [item.id, item]));
115	
116	export function createEmptySlot(): InventorySlot {
117	  return { id: null, label: "Empty", kind: null, count: 0 };
118	}
119	
120	export function createSlot(itemId: string, count: number): InventorySlot {
121	  const def = ITEM_DEF_BY_ID[itemId];
122	  if (!def) return createEmptySlot();
123	  const slot: InventorySlot = { ...def, count };
124	  if ((def.kind === "tool" || def.kind === "weapon" || def.kind === "armor") && def.maxDurability) {
125	    slot.maxDurability = def.maxDurability;
126	    slot.durability = def.maxDurability;
127	  }
128	  return slot;
129	}
130	
131	export function createInitialInventory(): InventorySlot[] {
132	  const slots: InventorySlot[] = Array.from({ length: INVENTORY_SLOTS }, () => createEmptySlot());
133	  const starter: Array<{ id: string; count: number }> = [
134	    { id: "grass", count: 64 },
135	    { id: "dirt", count: 64 },
136	    { id: "stone", count: 64 },
137	    { id: "wood", count: 64 },
138	    { id: "planks", count: 20 },
139	    { id: "cobble", count: 20 },
140	    { id: "sand", count: 20 },
141	    { id: "wood_pickaxe", count: 1 },
142	    { id: "knife", count: 1 }
143	  ];
144	  for (let i = 0; i < starter.length && i < slots.length; i += 1) slots[i] = createSlot(starter[i].id, starter[i].count);
145	  return slots;
146	}
147	
148	export const BLOCK_TO_SLOT: Partial<Record<BlockId, string>> = {
149	  [BlockId.Grass]: "grass",
150	  [BlockId.Dirt]: "dirt",
151	  [BlockId.Stone]: "stone",
152	  [BlockId.Wood]: "wood",
153	  [BlockId.Leaves]: "dirt",
154	  [BlockId.Planks]: "planks",
155	  [BlockId.Cobblestone]: "cobble",
156	  [BlockId.Sand]: "sand",
157	  [BlockId.Brick]: "brick",
158	  [BlockId.Glass]: "glass",
159	  [BlockId.SliverOre]: "sliver_ore",
160	  [BlockId.RubyOre]: "ruby_ore",
161	  [BlockId.GoldOre]: "gold_ore",
162	  [BlockId.SapphireOre]: "sapphire_ore",
163	  [BlockId.DiamondOre]: "diamond_ore",
164	  [BlockId.Snow]: "snow",
165	  [BlockId.Cactus]: "cactus",
166	  [BlockId.Bed]: "bed",
167	  [BlockId.Furnace]: "furnace",
168	  // Tilled soil reverts to dirt; immature wheat returns its seed.
169	  [BlockId.Farmland]: "dirt",
170	  [BlockId.WheatStage0]: "seeds",
171	  [BlockId.WheatStage1]: "seeds",
172	  [BlockId.WheatStage2]: "seeds"
173	};
174	
175	/**
176	 * Items a broken block yields. The default is its single `BLOCK_TO_SLOT` entry;
177	 * grass occasionally also drops a seed (the natural seed source), and mature
178	 * wheat drops wheat plus 1–2 seeds. `rng` is injectable for deterministic tests.
179	 */
180	export function rollBlockDrops(block: BlockId, rng: () => number): Array<{ itemId: string; count: number }> {
181	  const drops: Array<{ itemId: string; count: number }> = [];
182	  const base = BLOCK_TO_SLOT[block];
183	  if (base) drops.push({ itemId: base, count: 1 });
184	
185	  if (block === BlockId.Grass && rng() < GRASS_SEED_DROP_CHANCE) {
186	    drops.push({ itemId: "seeds", count: 1 });
187	  }
188	  if (block === BlockId.WheatStage3) {
189	    drops.push({ itemId: "wheat", count: 1 });
190	    drops.push({ itemId: "seeds", count: 1 + Math.floor(rng() * 2) });
191	  }
192	  return drops;
193	}
194

1	import * as THREE from "three";
2	import { BLOCK_COLORS, BlockId } from "./blocks";
3	
4	// Procedural texture atlas: one 16x16 tile per block face variant, painted on a
5	// canvas at startup. This is the only world module that touches the DOM.
6	export const ATLAS_TILE_SIZE = 16;
7	export const ATLAS_FACE_VARIANTS = 3; // top, side, bottom
8	export const ATLAS_COLUMNS = 16;
9	
10	// Tile range derived from the palette so new blocks get tiles automatically —
11	// a hardcoded last-block bound here once left new blocks sampling garbage UVs.
12	export const ATLAS_BLOCK_COUNT = Math.max(...Object.keys(BLOCK_COLORS).map(Number)) + 1;
13	export const ATLAS_ROWS = Math.ceil((ATLAS_BLOCK_COUNT * ATLAS_FACE_VARIANTS) / ATLAS_COLUMNS);
14	
15	let atlasTextureCache: THREE.CanvasTexture | null = null;
16	
17	function clamp01(v: number): number {
18	  return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, v));
19	}
20	
21	function tone(c: [number, number, number], mul: number, add = 0): [number, number, number] {
22	  return [clamp01(c[0] * mul + add), clamp01(c[1] * mul + add), clamp01(c[2] * mul + add)];
23	}
24	
25	function rgb(c: [number, number, number]): string {
26	  return `rgb(${Math.floor(clamp01(c[0]) * 255)}, ${Math.floor(clamp01(c[1]) * 255)}, ${Math.floor(clamp01(c[2]) * 255)})`;
27	}
28	
29	export function tileIndexFor(block: number, face: "top" | "side" | "bottom"): number {
30	  const faceId = face === "top" ? 0 : face === "side" ? 1 : 2;
31	  return block * ATLAS_FACE_VARIANTS + faceId;
32	}
33	
34	export function createBlockAtlasTexture(): THREE.CanvasTexture {
35	  if (atlasTextureCache) return atlasTextureCache;
36	
37	  const width = ATLAS_COLUMNS * ATLAS_TILE_SIZE;
38	  const height = ATLAS_ROWS * ATLAS_TILE_SIZE;
39	  const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
40	  canvas.width = width;
41	  canvas.height = height;
42	  const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
43	  if (!ctx) throw new Error("Failed to create atlas context");
44	  ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
45	
46	  const drawTile = (block: number, face: "top" | "side" | "bottom") => {
47	    const tile = tileIndexFor(block, face);
48	    const col = tile % ATLAS_COLUMNS;
49	    const row = Math.floor(tile / ATLAS_COLUMNS);
50	    const ox = col * ATLAS_TILE_SIZE;
51	    const oy = row * ATLAS_TILE_SIZE;
52	
53	    const baseBlockColor = BLOCK_COLORS[block] ?? [1, 0, 1];
54	    let base = baseBlockColor;
55	    if (face === "top") base = tone(base, 1.08);
56	    if (face === "bottom") base = tone(base, 0.96);
57	    if (block === BlockId.Grass && face === "bottom") base = BLOCK_COLORS[BlockId.Dirt];
58	
59	    for (let y = 0; y < ATLAS_TILE_SIZE; y += 1) {
60	      for (let x = 0; x < ATLAS_TILE_SIZE; x += 1) {
61	        const h = Math.sin((x + block * 13 + (face === "top" ? 7 : face === "side" ? 17 : 29)) * 12.1 + (y + block * 19) * 7.7) * 43758.5453;
62	        const n = h - Math.floor(h);
63	        let c = tone(base, 0.92 + n * 0.22);
64	
65	        if (block === BlockId.Grass && face === "side" && y < 4) c = tone(BLOCK_COLORS[BlockId.Grass], 0.95 + n * 0.15);
66	        if ((block === BlockId.Stone || block === BlockId.Cobblestone || block === BlockId.Bedrock) && n > 0.8) c = tone(base, 1.18);
67	        if ((block === BlockId.Wood || block === BlockId.Planks) && (x + y) % 4 === 0) c = tone(base, 0.82);
68	        if (block === BlockId.SliverOre && n > 0.86) c = tone([0.93, 0.93, 0.95], 1);
69	        if (block === BlockId.RubyOre && n > 0.88) c = tone([0.86, 0.24, 0.24], 1);
70	        if (block === BlockId.GoldOre && n > 0.84) c = tone([0.96, 0.8, 0.25], 1);
71	        if (block === BlockId.SapphireOre && n > 0.86) c = tone([0.2, 0.62, 0.9], 1);
72	        if (block === BlockId.DiamondOre && n > 0.9) c = tone([0.7, 0.94, 0.98], 1);
73	        if (block === BlockId.Water) c = tone([0.22, 0.48, 0.85], 0.95 + n * 0.12, face === "top" ? 0.02 : 0);
74	        if (block === BlockId.Sand && n > 0.84) c = tone(base, 1.12);
75	        if (block === BlockId.Cactus && face === "side" && x % 4 === 0) c = tone(base, 0.72);
76	        if (block === BlockId.Snow) c = tone(base, 0.97 + n * 0.06);
77	        if (block === BlockId.Bed && face === "top" && y < 5) c = tone([0.95, 0.95, 0.97], 0.95 + n * 0.1); // pillow band
78	        if (block >= BlockId.WheatStage0 && block <= BlockId.WheatStage3 && face === "side" && x % 3 === 1) c = tone(base, 0.66); // gaps read as stalks
79	        if (block === BlockId.Furnace && face === "side" && x >= 5 && x <= 10 && y >= 8 && y <= 12) c = tone([0.95, 0.45, 0.12], 0.85 + n * 0.3); // glowing mouth
80	
81	        ctx.fillStyle = rgb(c);
82	        ctx.fillRect(ox + x, oy + y, 1, 1);
83	      }
84	    }
85	  };
86	
87	  for (let block = BlockId.Grass; block < ATLAS_BLOCK_COUNT; block += 1) {
88	    drawTile(block, "top");
89	    drawTile(block, "side");
90	    drawTile(block, "bottom");
91	  }
92	
93	  const texture = new THREE.CanvasTexture(canvas);
94	  texture.magFilter = THREE.NearestFilter;
95	  texture.minFilter = THREE.NearestMipmapNearestFilter;
96	  texture.wrapS = THREE.ClampToEdgeWrapping;
97	  texture.wrapT = THREE.ClampToEdgeWrapping;
98	  texture.flipY = false;
99	  texture.needsUpdate = true;
100	  atlasTextureCache = texture;
101	  return texture;
102	}
103

1	// Gameplay tunables. Item/block data lives in items.ts, recipes in recipes.ts.
2	
3	// Player physics
4	export const PLAYER_HEIGHT = 1.8;
5	export const PLAYER_HALF_WIDTH = 0.3;
6	export const EYE_HEIGHT = 1.62;
7	export const GRAVITY = 26;
8	export const JUMP_VELOCITY = 8.2;
9	export const WALK_SPEED = 4.8;
10	export const SPRINT_SPEED = 12.8;
11	export const CROUCH_SPEED = 2.1;
12	export const WORLD_BORDER_PADDING = 1.2;
13	
14	// Player stats — Minecraft ranges: 20 HP shown as 10 hearts, 20 hunger as 10 drumsticks.
15	export const MAX_HEARTS = 20;
16	export const MAX_HUNGER = 20;
17	export const RESPAWN_SECONDS = 3;
18	export const HEALTH_REGEN_INTERVAL_SECONDS = 3;
19	// Health regen only runs at or above this hunger level; sprint needs more than SPRINT_MIN_HUNGER.
20	export const REGEN_MIN_HUNGER = 12;
21	export const SPRINT_MIN_HUNGER = 6;
22	// Hunger drain: one point per N blocks sprinted/walked, or per N jumps.
23	export const SPRINT_BLOCKS_PER_HUNGER = 100;
24	export const WALK_BLOCKS_PER_HUNGER = 300;
25	export const JUMPS_PER_HUNGER = 50;
26	
27	// Inventory
28	export const HOTBAR_SLOTS = 9;
29	export const INVENTORY_SLOTS = 36;
30	export const MAX_STACK_SIZE = 99;
31	
32	// Mining & combat
33	export const MINE_REACH = 7;
34	export const MINING_RATE = 2.1; // progress per second per minePower
35	export const BARE_HAND_MINE_POWER = 0.8;
36	export const FIST_DAMAGE = 6;
37	export const ATTACK_REACH = 4.5;
38	export const ATTACK_AIM_DOT = 0.89; // how precisely the camera must face a mob
39	
40	// Day-night cycle (daylight ranges 0.04–1.0)
41	export const DAY_CYCLE_SECONDS = 240;
42	export const HOSTILE_SPAWN_BELOW_DAYLIGHT = 0.28;
43	export const SPIDER_AGGRO_BELOW_DAYLIGHT = 0.42;
44	export const HOSTILE_BURN_ABOVE_DAYLIGHT = 0.72;
45	
46	// Weather (cosmetic, transient — never persisted, never touches spawn balance).
47	// Time is split into fixed windows; a seeded hash of the window index decides
48	// whether it precipitates, and a triangular envelope ramps intensity in/out.
49	export const WEATHER_CYCLE_SECONDS = 180;
50	export const WEATHER_RAIN_FRACTION = 0.35;
51	
52	// Beds & sleep
53	// Sleeping is only allowed once it is night by the game's own definition (the
54	// hostile-spawn threshold). The fade is the frozen window before time skips;
55	// waking lands at this fraction of the cycle (a rising ~0.45 daylight morning).
56	export const SLEEP_ALLOWED_BELOW_DAYLIGHT = 0.28;
57	export const SLEEP_HOSTILE_RADIUS = 12;
58	export const SLEEP_FADE_SECONDS = 1.5;
59	export const WAKE_DAY_PHASE = 0.07;
60	
61	// Mob director
62	export const HOSTILE_SPAWN_INTERVAL_SECONDS = 10;
63	export const HOSTILE_CAP = 16;
64	
65	// Animal breeding. Feeding a passive animal puts it "in love" for a window; two
66	// in-love adults of the same kind within range spawn a baby that grows up after
67	// a timer. The passive cap and the wheat/seed cost bound the population.
68	export const BREED_FED_WINDOW_SECONDS = 30;
69	export const BREED_PARTNER_RADIUS = 3;
70	export const BREED_CHECK_INTERVAL_SECONDS = 0.5;
71	export const BABY_GROW_SECONDS = 90;
72	export const BABY_SCALE = 0.55;
73	export const PASSIVE_CAP = 24;
74	
75	// Random block ticks (crop growth; the system is extensible to other blocks).
76	// Each interval samples N columns within RADIUS of the player and runs the
77	// block's handler. ~128 samples/s over a 64x64 area ≈ 50 s/stage (~2.5 min to
78	// mature). GRASS_SEED_DROP_CHANCE is the per-break odds a grass block drops a seed.
79	export const RANDOM_TICK_INTERVAL_SECONDS = 0.5;
80	export const RANDOM_TICK_SAMPLES = 64;
81	export const RANDOM_TICK_RADIUS = 32;
82	export const CROP_GROWTH_CHANCE = 0.65;
83	export const GRASS_SEED_DROP_CHANCE = 0.2;
84	
85	// Safety & persistence
86	export const STUCK_RESET_SECONDS = 0.8;
87	export const AUTOSAVE_INTERVAL_MS = 15000;
88	export const SAVE_KEY = "minecraft_save_v5";
89	
90	// Rendering
91	export const RENDER_RADIUS = 90;
92	export const RENDER_GRID = 20;
93	// Third-person camera boom, clamped against walls with a margin that keeps
94	// the near plane (0.1) out of the blocking block.
95	export const THIRD_PERSON_DISTANCE = 4;
96	export const THIRD_PERSON_MARGIN = 0.2;
97

20	function makeSlots(...items: Array<[string, number] | null>): InventorySlot[] {
21	  const slots = Array.from({ length: INVENTORY_SLOTS }, () => createEmptySlot());
22	  items.forEach((item, i) => {
23	    if (item) slots[i] = createSlot(item[0], item[1]);
24	  });
25	  return slots;
26	}

29	const brickRecipe = RECIPES.find((recipe) => recipe.id === "brick")!; // 2 dirt + 2 stone -> 2 brick
30	
31	describe("countsById", () => {
32	  test("sums across stacks and skips empties", () => {
33	    const slots = makeSlots(["dirt", 30], ["stone", 5], null, ["dirt", 12]);
34	    const byId = countsById(slots);
35	    expect(byId.get("dirt")).toBe(42);
36	    expect(byId.get("stone")).toBe(5);
37	    expect(byId.has("")).toBe(false);
38	  });

41	describe("adjustSlotCount", () => {
42	  test("removal drains the preferred slot first, then scans", () => {
43	    const slots = makeSlots(["dirt", 10], ["dirt", 10]);
44	    const next = adjustSlotCount(slots, "dirt", -12, 1)!;
45	    expect(next[1].count).toBe(0);
46	    expect(next[1].id).toBeNull();
47	    expect(next[0].count).toBe(8);
48	  });
49	
50	  test("removal is all-or-nothing when short", () => {
51	    const slots = makeSlots(["dirt", 5]);

53	    expect(slots[0].count).toBe(5); // input untouched
54	  });
55	
56	  test("addition fills existing stacks then empty slots", () => {
57	    const slots = makeSlots(["dirt", MAX_STACK_SIZE - 2]);
58	    const next = adjustSlotCount(slots, "dirt", 5)!;

60	    expect(next[1].id).toBe("dirt");
61	    expect(next[1].count).toBe(3);

66	    const next = adjustSlotCount(slots, "dirt", 10)!;
67	    expect(countsById(next).get("dirt")).toBeUndefined();
68	  });
69	
70	  test("unknown items and zero deltas are no-ops", () => {

72	    expect(adjustSlotCount(slots, "no_such_item", 3)).toBeNull();
73	    expect(adjustSlotCount(slots, "dirt", 0)).toBeNull();
74	  });

76	  test("never mutates the input array", () => {

84	describe("durability", () => {

92	  });

95	    const slots = makeSlots(["dirt", 5]);
96	    expect(consumeToolDurability(slots, 0, 1)).toBeNull();
97	  });

99	  test("equipped armor wears together; broken pieces vanish", () => {
100	    let slots = makeSlots(["helmet", 1], ["boots", 1]);
101	    const equipped = { ...createEmptyArmorEquipment(), helmet: "helmet", boots: "boots" };
102	    slots = consumeEquippedArmorDurability(slots, equipped, slots[1].maxDurability! - 1)!;
103	    expect(slots[0].durability).toBe(slots[0].maxDurability! - (slots[1].maxDurability! - 1));
104	    expect(slots[1].durability).toBe(1);

106	    expect(slots[1].id).toBeNull(); // boots broke, helmet survives
107	    expect(slots[0].id).toBe("helmet");
108	  });

143	    const pick = next.find((slot) => slot.id === "wood_pickaxe")!;

175	  });

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/lib/game/inventory.ts",
  "old_string": "export function swapSlots(slots: InventorySlot[], fromIndex: number, toIndex: number): InventorySlot[] | null {\n  if (fromIndex === toIndex) return null;\n  if (fromIndex < 0 || toIndex < 0 || fromIndex >= slots.length || toIndex >= slots.length) return null;\n  const next = cloneSlots(slots);\n  const temp = next[fromIndex];\n  next[fromIndex] = next[toIndex];\n  next[toIndex] = temp;\n  return next;\n}",
  "new_string": "export function swapSlots(slots: InventorySlot[], fromIndex: number, toIndex: number): InventorySlot[] | null {\n  if (fromIndex === toIndex) return null;\n  if (fromIndex < 0 || toIndex < 0 || fromIndex >= slots.length || toIndex >= slots.length) return null;\n  const next = cloneSlots(slots);\n  const temp = next[fromIndex];\n  next[fromIndex] = next[toIndex];\n  next[toIndex] = temp;\n  return next;\n}\n\n/**\n * Swaps a slot between two (possibly identical) arrays — the cross-container\n * generalization of swapSlots, used to move items between the player inventory\n * and an open chest. When both refs point at the same array it degrades to a\n * plain swap and returns that one array as both `a` and `b`. Returns null on a\n * no-op or out-of-range index.\n */\nexport function moveStack(\n  a: InventorySlot[],\n  indexA: number,\n  b: InventorySlot[],\n  indexB: number\n): { a: InventorySlot[]; b: InventorySlot[] } | null {\n  if (a === b) {\n    const swapped = swapSlots(a, indexA, indexB);\n    return swapped ? { a: swapped, b: swapped } : null;\n  }\n  if (indexA < 0 || indexA >= a.length || indexB < 0 || indexB >= b.length) return null;\n  const nextA = cloneSlots(a);\n  const nextB = cloneSlots(b);\n  const temp = nextA[indexA];\n  nextA[indexA] = nextB[indexB];\n  nextB[indexB] = temp;\n  return { a: nextA, b: nextB };\n}\n\n/**\n * All-or-nothing insert of whole slot objects into `inventory` (used when a\n * broken chest spills its contents). Stackable items merge into existing stacks\n * then empty slots; durability items (tools/weapons/armor) take a whole empty\n * slot each so each piece keeps its own `durability`. Returns a new array, or\n * null if everything does not fit — the caller then leaves the inventory (and\n * the chest) untouched.\n */\nexport function tryInsertSlots(inventory: InventorySlot[], incoming: InventorySlot[]): InventorySlot[] | null {\n  const next = cloneSlots(inventory);\n\n  const insertStackable = (id: string, count: number): boolean => {\n    let remaining = count;\n    for (let i = 0; i < next.length && remaining > 0; i += 1) {\n      const slot = next[i];\n      if (slot.id !== id || slot.count >= MAX_STACK_SIZE) continue;\n      const add = Math.min(remaining, MAX_STACK_SIZE - slot.count);\n      slot.count += add;\n      remaining -= add;\n    }\n    for (let i = 0; i < next.length && remaining > 0; i += 1) {\n      if (next[i].id !== null || next[i].count !== 0) continue;\n      const add = Math.min(remaining, MAX_STACK_SIZE);\n      next[i] = createSlot(id, add);\n      remaining -= add;\n    }\n    return remaining === 0;\n  };\n\n  const insertDurable = (slot: InventorySlot): boolean => {\n    let remaining = slot.count;\n    for (let i = 0; i < next.length && remaining > 0; i += 1) {\n      if (next[i].id !== null || next[i].count !== 0) continue;\n      next[i] = { ...slot, count: 1 };\n      remaining -= 1;\n    }\n    return remaining === 0;\n  };\n\n  for (const slot of incoming) {\n    if (!slot.id || slot.count <= 0) continue;\n    const def = ITEM_DEF_BY_ID[slot.id];\n    if (!def) continue; // unknown ids carry no data to preserve\n    const placed = def.maxDurability ? insertDurable(slot) : insertStackable(slot.id, slot.count);\n    if (!placed) return null;\n  }\n\n  return next;\n}"
}

  "old_string": "/** Legacy save shape (40 inventory slots, 10-slot hotbar) — accepted and migrated on load. */\nexport type SaveDataV1 = {\n  version: 1;\n  seed: number;\n  changes: Array<[number, number]>;\n  inventoryCounts?: Record<string, number>;\n  inventorySlots?: Array<{ id: string | null; count: number; durability?: number }>;\n  equippedArmor?: Partial<EquippedArmor>;\n  selectedSlot: number;\n  player: { x: number; y: number; z: number };\n};\n\n/** v2 save shape: same fields as v1, reinterpreted for 36 slots / 9-slot hotbar. */\nexport type SaveDataV2 = Omit<SaveDataV1, \"version\"> & { version: 2 };\n\n/**\n * Current save shape (v3): v2 plus persisted time-of-day, player stats, and the\n * bed respawn point. All new fields are optional so the v2→v3 migration is a\n * pure version bump and pre-v3 saves load with sensible defaults.\n */\nexport type SaveData = Omit<SaveDataV2, \"version\"> & {\n  version: 3;\n  dayClock?: number;\n  hearts?: number;\n  hunger?: number;\n  spawnPoint?: { x: number; y: number; z: number } | null;\n};",
  "new_string": "/** A persisted inventory/container slot: just enough to rebuild it on load. */\nexport type SavedSlot = { id: string | null; count: number; durability?: number };\n\n/** A block-entity: a placed chest's contents, keyed by its voxel index. */\nexport type SavedContainer = { index: number; slots: SavedSlot[] };\n\n/** Legacy save shape (40 inventory slots, 10-slot hotbar) — accepted and migrated on load. */\nexport type SaveDataV1 = {\n  version: 1;\n  seed: number;\n  changes: Array<[number, number]>;\n  inventoryCounts?: Record<string, number>;\n  inventorySlots?: SavedSlot[];\n  equippedArmor?: Partial<EquippedArmor>;\n  selectedSlot: number;\n  player: { x: number; y: number; z: number };\n};\n\n/** v2 save shape: same fields as v1, reinterpreted for 36 slots / 9-slot hotbar. */\nexport type SaveDataV2 = Omit<SaveDataV1, \"version\"> & { version: 2 };\n\n/**\n * v3 save shape: v2 plus persisted time-of-day, player stats, and the bed\n * respawn point. All added fields are optional so the v2→v3 migration is a pure\n * version bump and pre-v3 saves load with sensible defaults.\n */\nexport type SaveDataV3 = Omit<SaveDataV2, \"version\"> & {\n  version: 3;\n  dayClock?: number;\n  hearts?: number;\n  hunger?: number;\n  spawnPoint?: { x: number; y: number; z: number } | null;\n};\n\n/**\n * Current save shape (v4): v3 plus chest contents as block-entities. The new\n * field is optional so the v3→v4 migration is a pure version bump and pre-v4\n * saves load with no containers. `SAVE_KEY` is unchanged — chests are never\n * generated, so worldgen and the block-diff index space are untouched.\n */\nexport type SaveData = Omit<SaveDataV3, \"version\"> & {\n  version: 4;\n  blockEntities?: SavedContainer[];\n};"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/lib/game/save.ts",
  "old_string": "/**\n * Migrates a v2 save to v3 — a pure version bump. The new persisted fields\n * (dayClock, hearts, hunger, spawnPoint) are optional, so an older save simply\n * loads with the engine's defaults for them.\n */\nexport function migrateSaveV2toV3(save: SaveDataV2): SaveData {\n  return { ...save, version: 3 };\n}\n\n// Storage is injectable so save logic can be tested without a browser.\nexport function readSave(saveKey: string, storage: Storage = localStorage): SaveData | null {\n  try {\n    const raw = storage.getItem(saveKey);\n    if (!raw) return null;\n    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as SaveData | SaveDataV2 | SaveDataV1;\n    if (!parsed || !Number.isFinite(parsed.seed) || !Array.isArray(parsed.changes)) return null;\n    let migrated: SaveDataV2 | SaveData = parsed.version === 1 ? migrateSaveV1toV2(parsed) : parsed;\n    if (migrated.version === 2) migrated = migrateSaveV2toV3(migrated);\n    if (migrated.version !== 3) return null;\n    return migrated;\n  } catch {\n    return null;\n  }\n}\n\nexport function writeSave(saveKey: string, data: SaveData, storage: Storage = localStorage): void {\n  storage.setItem(saveKey, JSON.stringify(data));\n}\n\nexport function inventorySlotsSnapshot(inventory: InventorySlot[]): Array<{ id: string | null; count: number; durability?: number }> {\n  return inventory.map((slot) => ({ id: slot.id, count: slot.count, durability: slot.durability }));\n}",
  "new_string": "/**\n * Migrates a v2 save to v3 — a pure version bump. The new persisted fields\n * (dayClock, hearts, hunger, spawnPoint) are optional, so an older save simply\n * loads with the engine's defaults for them.\n */\nexport function migrateSaveV2toV3(save: SaveDataV2): SaveDataV3 {\n  return { ...save, version: 3 };\n}\n\n/**\n * Migrates a v3 save to v4 — a pure version bump. `blockEntities` (chest\n * contents) is optional, so a pre-chest save simply loads with no containers.\n */\nexport function migrateSaveV3toV4(save: SaveDataV3): SaveData {\n  return { ...save, version: 4 };\n}\n\n// Storage is injectable so save logic can be tested without a browser.\nexport function readSave(saveKey: string, storage: Storage = localStorage): SaveData | null {\n  try {\n    const raw = storage.getItem(saveKey);\n    if (!raw) return null;\n    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as SaveData | SaveDataV3 | SaveDataV2 | SaveDataV1;\n    if (!parsed || !Number.isFinite(parsed.seed) || !Array.isArray(parsed.changes)) return null;\n    let migrated: SaveDataV2 | SaveDataV3 | SaveData = parsed.version === 1 ? migrateSaveV1toV2(parsed) : parsed;\n    if (migrated.version === 2) migrated = migrateSaveV2toV3(migrated);\n    if (migrated.version === 3) migrated = migrateSaveV3toV4(migrated);\n    if (migrated.version !== 4) return null;\n    return migrated;\n  } catch {\n    return null;\n  }\n}\n\nexport function writeSave(saveKey: string, data: SaveData, storage: Storage = localStorage): void {\n  storage.setItem(saveKey, JSON.stringify(data));\n}\n\nexport function inventorySlotsSnapshot(inventory: InventorySlot[]): SavedSlot[] {\n  return inventory.map((slot) => ({ id: slot.id, count: slot.count, durability: slot.durability }));\n}\n\n/**\n * Rebuilds one slot from its saved snapshot: drops unknown ids, clamps the\n * count, and clamps/defaults durability for gear (a non-positive saved\n * durability means the piece broke and is dropped). Returns an empty slot for\n * anything invalid. Shared by inventory and container restore.\n */\nfunction restoreSlot(saved: SavedSlot | undefined): InventorySlot {\n  if (!saved?.id || saved.count <= 0 || !ITEM_DEF_BY_ID[saved.id]) return createEmptySlot();\n  const slot = createSlot(saved.id, Math.min(MAX_STACK_SIZE, Math.max(0, Math.floor(saved.count))));\n  if ((slot.kind === \"tool\" || slot.kind === \"weapon\" || slot.kind === \"armor\") && slot.maxDurability) {\n    if (typeof saved.durability === \"number\") {\n      const loaded = Math.floor(saved.durability);\n      if (loaded <= 0) return createEmptySlot();\n      slot.durability = Math.max(1, Math.min(slot.maxDurability, loaded));\n    } else {\n      slot.durability = slot.maxDurability;\n    }\n  }\n  return slot;\n}\n\n/** Snapshots non-empty chest containers for persistence (empty chests carry no data). */\nexport function serializeContainers(containers: Map<number, InventorySlot[]>): SavedContainer[] {\n  const out: SavedContainer[] = [];\n  for (const [index, slots] of containers) {\n    if (!slots.some((slot) => slot.id && slot.count > 0)) continue;\n    out.push({ index, slots: inventorySlotsSnapshot(slots) });\n  }\n  return out;\n}\n\n/**\n * Reads chest containers from a save into validated, CHEST_SLOTS-length slot\n * arrays keyed by voxel index. Per-slot validation only — the engine still\n * confirms each index actually holds a Chest block before using it.\n */\nexport function readContainers(save: SaveData): Array<{ index: number; slots: InventorySlot[] }> {\n  if (!Array.isArray(save.blockEntities)) return [];\n  const out: Array<{ index: number; slots: InventorySlot[] }> = [];\n  for (const entry of save.blockEntities) {\n    if (!entry || !Number.isFinite(entry.index) || !Array.isArray(entry.slots)) continue;\n    const slots = Array.from({ length: CHEST_SLOTS }, (_, i) => restoreSlot(entry.slots[i]));\n    out.push({ index: entry.index, slots });\n  }\n  return out;\n}"
}

18	function memoryStorage(initial: Record<string, string> = {}): Storage {
19	  const data = new Map(Object.entries(initial));
20	  return {
21	    get length() {
22	      return data.size;
23	    },
24	    clear: () => data.clear(),
25	    getItem: (key: string) => data.get(key) ?? null,
26	    key: (index: number) => [...data.keys()][index] ?? null,
27	    removeItem: (key: string) => void data.delete(key),
28	    setItem: (key: string, value: string) => void data.set(key, value)
29	  };
30	}

Introduce a small block-entity\nlayer: a chest's contents are persisted under a new optional blockEntities\nfield, keyed by the block's voxel index (the same index space as the diff).\n\n- SaveData bumped to version 4; SaveDataV3 alias retained. blockEntities\n  is optional, so migrateSaveV3toV4 is a pure version bump and pre-chest\n  saves load with no containers. readSave now chains v1->v2->v3->v4.\n- serializeContainers snapshots only non-empty chests (an empty chest\n  carries no data and is lazily recreated on open).\n- readContainers rebuilds CHEST_SLOTS-length, per-slot-validated arrays\n  keyed by index; the engine still confirms each index holds a Chest.\n- Extracted a shared restoreSlot helper (drops unknown ids, clamps count,\n  clamps/defaults gear durability) now used by both inventory and\n  container restore.\n- serialize() emits version 4 (contents are wired from live state in the\n  next commit).\n\nSAVE_KEY is unchanged — chests are never generated, so worldgen and the\nvoxel index formula are untouched and v1/v2/v3 saves still load.\nEOF\ngit log --oneline -1",
  "description": "Commit slice 3"
}

1	import { BiomeId, BlockId, WORLD_SIZE_X, WORLD_SIZE_Y, WORLD_SIZE_Z } from "./blocks";
2	
3	/**
4	 * Voxel data store plus cheap world queries. Terrain generation lives in
5	 * generation.ts, meshing in meshing.ts, raycast/collision in queries.ts.
6	 *
7	 * ⚠ Save-format invariants: saves address voxels through index(), and getBiome()
8	 * feeds terrain generation. Changing either breaks every existing save — see
9	 * generation.test.ts.
10	 */
11	export class VoxelWorld {
12	  readonly sizeX: number;
13	  readonly sizeY: number;
14	  readonly sizeZ: number;
15	  readonly seed: number;
16	  readonly blocks: Uint8Array;
17	
18	  constructor(sizeX = WORLD_SIZE_X, sizeY = WORLD_SIZE_Y, sizeZ = WORLD_SIZE_Z, seed = 1337) {
19	    this.sizeX = sizeX;
20	    this.sizeY = sizeY;
21	    this.sizeZ = sizeZ;
22	    this.seed = seed;
23	    this.blocks = new Uint8Array(sizeX * sizeY * sizeZ);
24	  }
25	
26	  index(x: number, y: number, z: number): number {
27	    return x + z * this.sizeX + y * this.sizeX * this.sizeZ;
28	  }
29	
30	  inBounds(x: number, y: number, z: number): boolean {
31	    return x >= 0 && y >= 0 && z >= 0 && x < this.sizeX && y < this.sizeY && z < this.sizeZ;
32	  }
33	
34	  get(x: number, y: number, z: number): number {
35	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y, z)) return BlockId.Air;
36	    return this.blocks[this.index(x, y, z)];
37	  }
38	
39	  set(x: number, y: number, z: number, block: BlockId): void {
40	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y, z)) return;
41	    this.blocks[this.index(x, y, z)] = block;
42	  }
43	
44	  isSolid(x: number, y: number, z: number): boolean {
45	    const block = this.get(x, y, z);
46	    return block !== BlockId.Air && block !== BlockId.Water;
47	  }
48	
49	  highestSolidY(x: number, z: number): number {
50	    for (let y = this.sizeY - 1; y >= 0; y -= 1) {
51	      if (this.isSolid(x, y, z)) return y;
52	    }
53	    return 0;
54	  }
55	
56	  getBiome(x: number, z: number): BiomeId {
57	    // Two incommensurate, direction-rotated sine octaves per field, with
58	    // dominant wavelengths of ~250-900 blocks: each field traverses several
59	    // cycles across the 512-block map, so every world gets coherent patches
60	    // of all five biomes (~60-150 block scale).
61	    const s = this.seed * 0.007;
62	    const temp = Math.sin(x * 0.013 + z * 0.006 + s * 1.3) * 0.6 + Math.sin(x * 0.029 - z * 0.017 + s * 2.4) * 0.4;
63	    const moisture = Math.sin(x * 0.007 - z * 0.012 + s * 0.8) * 0.6 + Math.sin(x * 0.019 + z * 0.023 - s * 1.6) * 0.4;
64	    const continental = Math.sin(x * 0.008 + z * 0.01 + s * 2.1) * 0.55 + Math.sin(x * 0.014 - z * 0.009 - s * 1.7) * 0.45;
65	    const ridge = Math.sin(x * 0.024 + z * 0.02 + s) * 0.5 + Math.sin(x * 0.016 - z * 0.024 - s) * 0.5;
66	
67	    if (continental < -0.52) return BiomeId.Ocean;
68	    if ((continental > 0.5 && ridge > 0) || ridge > 0.8) return BiomeId.Mountains;
69	    if (temp > 0.2 && moisture < -0.12) return BiomeId.Desert;
70	    if (moisture > 0.18) return BiomeId.Forest;
71	    return BiomeId.Plains;
72	  }
73	}
74

2	import * as THREE from "three";
3	import { BlockId, collidesAt } from "@/lib/world";
4	import {
5	  DAY_CYCLE_SECONDS,
6	  EYE_HEIGHT,
7	  MAX_HUNGER,
8	  MAX_HEARTS,

10	  PLAYER_HEIGHT,
11	  REGEN_MIN_HUNGER,
12	  SPRINT_BLOCKS_PER_HUNGER,
13	  SPRINT_MIN_HUNGER
14	} from "@/lib/game/config";
15	import { countsById } from "@/lib/game/inventory";
16	import { createSlot } from "@/lib/game/items";
17	import { GameEngine } from "@/lib/game/engine/GameEngine";
18	import { daylightAt } from "@/lib/game/engine/systems/dayNight";
19	import type { FrameInput } from "@/lib/game/engine/state";
20	import type { MobKind } from "@/lib/game/types";
21	
22	/**
23	 * Headless simulation tests: the engine boots a real generated world and runs
24	 * real frames without React, Three.js rendering, or a DOM.
25	 */
26	
27	function mulberry32(seed: number): () => number {
28	  let t = seed >>> 0;
29	  return () => {
30	    t += 0x6d2b79f5;
31	    let r = Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 15), 1 | t);
32	    r ^= r + Math.imul(r ^ (r >>> 7), 61 | r);
33	    return ((r ^ (r >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
34	  };
35	}
36	
37	function makeEngine(save: ReturnType<GameEngine["serialize"]> | null = null): GameEngine {
38	  return new GameEngine({ save, seed: 1337, rng: mulberry32(42), worldSize: { x: 64, y: 150, z: 64 } });
39	}
40	
41	function input(overrides: Partial<{ keys: string[]; capsActive: boolean; leftMouseHeld: boolean; pointerLocked: boolean }> = {}): FrameInput {
42	  return {
43	    keys: new Set(overrides.keys ?? []),
44	    capsActive: overrides.capsActive ?? false,
45	    leftMouseHeld: overrides.leftMouseHeld ?? false,
46	    pointerLocked: overrides.pointerLocked ?? false
47	  };
48	}
49	
50	function run(engine: GameEngine, seconds: number, frame: FrameInput = input()): void {
51	  const dt = 1 / 60;
52	  for (let t = 0; t < seconds; t += dt) engine.step(dt, frame);
53	}
54	
55	/**
56	 * The world boots at dawn (daylight 0.05), so the initial hostiles aggro
57	 * immediately. Tests about other mechanics clear them and move to midday.
58	 */
59	function calmDaytime(engine: GameEngine): void {
60	  engine.state.mobs = engine.state.mobs.filter((mob) => !mob.hostile);
61	  engine.state.dayClock = 60;
62	}

}

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/lib/game/engine/systems/mining.ts",
  "old_string": "function addBlockDrop(state: GameState, block: BlockId, rng: () => number): void {\n  for (const drop of rollBlockDrops(block, rng)) {\n    state.inventory = adjustSlotCount(state.inventory, drop.itemId, drop.count) ?? state.inventory;\n  }\n}",
  "new_string": "function addBlockDrop(state: GameState, block: BlockId, rng: () => number): void {\n  for (const drop of rollBlockDrops(block, rng)) {\n    state.inventory = adjustSlotCount(state.inventory, drop.itemId, drop.count) ?? state.inventory;\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Empties a broken chest into the inventory. Returns true if the break may\n * proceed (the chest was empty, or its contents all fit), false to refuse it\n * (no room — the chest is left intact). On success the container entry is\n * removed and a pickedUp toast announces what was retrieved.\n */\nfunction spillChestOnBreak(state: GameState, idx: number, emit: EmitGameEvent): boolean {\n  const container = state.containers.get(idx);\n  const items = container?.filter((slot) => slot.id && slot.count > 0) ?? [];\n  if (items.length > 0) {\n    const merged = tryInsertSlots(state.inventory, items);\n    if (!merged) {\n      emit({ type: \"breakBlocked\", reason: \"containerFull\" });\n      return false;\n    }\n    state.inventory = merged;\n    const picked = new Map<string, number>();\n    for (const slot of items) picked.set(slot.id!, (picked.get(slot.id!) ?? 0) + slot.count);\n    emit({ type: \"pickedUp\", items: [...picked].map(([itemId, count]) => ({ itemId, count })) });\n  }\n  state.containers.delete(idx);\n  return true;\n}"
}

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01QKjQarFupeDzZ73Rg6S6Cv
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/lib/game/engine/GameEngine.ts",
  "old_string": "  private emit = (event: GameEvent): void => {\n    this.events.push(event);\n  };",
  "new_string": "  private emit = (event: GameEvent): void => {\n    this.events.push(event);\n  };\n\n  /**\n   * Moves a slot between the player inventory and the open chest. Indices at or\n   * above CONTAINER_SLOT_BASE address the container; below it, the inventory.\n   * Writes back whichever of the two arrays the move touched.\n   */\n  private applyMoveStack(from: number, to: number): void {\n    const state = this.state;\n    const container = state.openContainerIndex !== null ? state.containers.get(state.openContainerIndex) : undefined;\n    const resolve = (index: number): { arr: InventorySlot[]; local: number } | null => {\n      if (index >= CONTAINER_SLOT_BASE) return container ? { arr: container, local: index - CONTAINER_SLOT_BASE } : null;\n      return { arr: state.inventory, local: index };\n    };\n    const a = resolve(from);\n    const b = resolve(to);\n    if (!a || !b) return;\n    const moved = inv.moveStack(a.arr, a.local, b.arr, b.local);\n    if (!moved) return;\n\n    const writeBack = (arr: InventorySlot[], next: InventorySlot[]): void => {\n      if (arr === state.inventory) state.inventory = next;\n      else if (state.openContainerIndex !== null) state.containers.set(state.openContainerIndex, next);\n    };\n    writeBack(a.arr, moved.a);\n    writeBack(b.arr, moved.b);\n  }"
}
```

3	import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState, useSyncExternalStore } from "react";

6	import { AUTOSAVE_INTERVAL_MS, HOTBAR_SLOTS, MAX_HUNGER, MAX_HEARTS, SAVE_KEY } from "@/lib/game/config";
7	import { GameEngine } from "@/lib/game/engine/GameEngine";
8	import type { GameApi, GameSnapshot } from "@/lib/game/engine/state";
9	import { createInputController, type InputController } from "@/lib/game/input/inputController";
10	import * as inv from "@/lib/game/inventory";
11	import { DEFAULT_SKIN_ID, getSkinPreset, type SkinId } from "@/lib/game/playerSkins";
12	import { readSkinSettings, writeSkinSettings } from "@/lib/game/skinSettings";
13	import { createEmptyArmorEquipment, createInitialInventory, ITEM_DEF_BY_ID } from "@/lib/game/items";

18	import type { Recipe } from "@/lib/game/types";
19	
20	/**
21	 * Thin React shell around the headless GameEngine and the GameRenderer.
22	 *
23	 * The engine is created in the canvas mount's callback ref (commit phase) and
24	 * held in React state; the UI reads it through useSyncExternalStore snapshots
25	 * and sends intents back as engine commands. The effect below owns everything
26	 * with a lifecycle: renderer, input listeners, the rAF loop, and autosave.
27	 */
28	
29	// Pre-mount snapshot (also the SSR snapshot): the starter loadout at full stats.
30	const PRE_MOUNT_SNAPSHOT: GameSnapshot = {
31	  api: null,
32	  inventory: createInitialInventory(),
33	  equippedArmor: createEmptyArmorEquipment(),
34	  selectedSlot: 0,
35	  hearts: MAX_HEARTS,
36	  hunger: MAX_HUNGER,
37	  daylightPercent: 100,
38	  passiveCount: 0,
39	  hostileCount: 0,
40	  respawnSeconds: 0,
41	  inventoryOpen: false,
42	  paused: false,
43	  debugOpen: false,
44	  debug: null,
45	  cameraMode: "first",
46	  armorPoints: 0,
47	  capsActive: false,
48	  sleeping: false,
49	  craftingStation: null
50	};
51	
52	const noopSubscribe = () => () => {};
53	
54	type GameContext = { engine: GameEngine; node: HTMLDivElement };
55	
56	// Debug/test handle: lets the browser console and the Playwright E2E suite
57	// inspect the live simulation (single-player client game — nothing to protect).
58	declare global {
59	  interface Window {
60	    __monecraft?: { engine: GameEngine; renderer: GameRenderer; input: InputController; audio: AudioDirector };
61	  }
62	}
63	
64	function persistGame(api: GameApi, onMessage: (text: string) => void): void {
65	  try {
66	    writeSave(SAVE_KEY, api.serialize());
67	    onMessage("Saved");
68	  } catch {
69	    onMessage("Save failed");
70	  }
71	}
72	
73	export function useMinecraftGame() {
74	  const [ctx, setCtx] = useState<GameContext | null>(null);
75	  const [locked, setLocked] = useState(false);
76	  const [saveMessage, setSaveMessage] = useState("");
77	  const [rendererError, setRendererError] = useState<string | null>(null);
78	  const [audioSettings, setAudioSettings] = useState<AudioSettings>(DEFAULT_AUDIO_SETTINGS);
79	  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement | null>(null);
80	  const minimapNodeRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
81	  const audioRef = useRef<AudioDirector | null>(null);
82	  // The rAF effect must not re-run on volume tweaks — it reads through a ref.
83	  const audioSettingsRef = useRef(audioSettings);
84	  const [skinId, setSkinId] = useState<SkinId>(DEFAULT_SKIN_ID);
85	  const skinIdRef = useRef(skinId);
86	  const rendererRef = useRef<GameRenderer | null>(null);
87	
88	  // Persisted preferences load after mount: render never touches localStorage
89	  // (SSR), and the setState hops a microtask like the renderer-error report.
90	  // This effect runs before the renderer effect (ctx is set by a callback ref
91	  // in a later commit), so the refs are populated by the time either exists.
92	  useEffect(() => {
93	    const stored = readAudioSettings();
94	    audioSettingsRef.current = stored;
95	    audioRef.current?.setSettings(stored);
96	    const { skinId: storedSkin } = readSkinSettings();
97	    skinIdRef.current = storedSkin;
98	    queueMicrotask(() => {
99	      setAudioSettings(stored);
100	      setSkinId(storedSkin);
101	    });
102	  }, []);
103	
104	  const updateAudioSettings = useCallback((partial: Partial<AudioSettings>) => {
105	    const next = { ...audioSettingsRef.current, ...partial };
106	    audioSettingsRef.current = next;
107	    setAudioSettings(next);
108	    writeAudioSettings(next);
109	    audioRef.current?.setSettings(next);
110	  }, []);
111	
112	  const updateSkin = useCallback((id: SkinId) => {
113	    skinIdRef.current = id;
114	    setSkinId(id);
115	    writeSkinSettings({ skinId: id });
116	    rendererRef.current?.setPlayerSkin(getSkinPreset(id).palette);
117	  }, []);
118	
119	  // Callback ref: the engine boots as soon as the canvas mount exists. A ref
120	  // callback runs during commit, where side effects and setState are legal.
121	  const attachMount = useCallback((node: HTMLDivElement | null) => {
122	    if (!node) {
123	      setCtx(null);
124	      return;
125	    }
126	    setCtx({ engine: new GameEngine({ save: readSave(SAVE_KEY) }), node });
127	  }, []);
128	
129	  // The minimap container mounts independently of the canvas; the rAF loop
130	  // below picks it up lazily once both exist.
131	  const attachMinimap = useCallback((node: HTMLDivElement | null) => {
132	    minimapNodeRef.current = node;
133	  }, []);
134	
135	  const engine = ctx?.engine ?? null;
136	  const subscribe = useMemo(() => engine?.subscribe ?? noopSubscribe, [engine]);
137	  const getSnapshot = useMemo(() => engine?.getSnapshot ?? (() => PRE_MOUNT_SNAPSHOT), [engine]);
138	  const getServerSnapshot = useCallback(() => PRE_MOUNT_SNAPSHOT, []);
139	  const snapshot = useSyncExternalStore(subscribe, getSnapshot, getServerSnapshot);
140	
141	  const flashMessage = useCallback((text: string, durationMs = 1200) => {
142	    setSaveMessage(text);
143	    window.setTimeout(() => setSaveMessage(""), durationMs);
144	  }, []);
145	
146	  useEffect(() => {
147	    if (!ctx) return;
148	    const { engine: gameEngine, node } = ctx;
149	
150	    const created = GameRenderer.create(node);
151	    if (!created.ok) {
152	      // Microtask: reporting an init failure from inside the effect body
153	      // would count as a cascading synchronous setState.
154	      queueMicrotask(() => setRendererError(created.error));
155	      return;
156	    }
157	    const renderer = created.renderer;
158	    canvasRef.current = renderer.domElement;
159	    rendererRef.current = renderer;
160	    // Before the first rAF, so no frame can ever show the default palette.
161	    renderer.setPlayerSkin(getSkinPreset(skinIdRef.current).palette);
162	
163	    const audio = createAudioDirector();
164	    audio.setSettings(audioSettingsRef.current);
165	    audioRef.current = audio;
166	    const input = createInputController({
167	      canvas: renderer.domElement,
168	      engine: gameEngine,
169	      onResize: () => renderer.handleResize(),
170	      onLockChange: (isLocked) => {
171	        // Pointer-lock acquisition is itself a user gesture — a safe unlock
172	        // point, and it re-resumes a context suspended by the browser.
173	        if (isLocked) audio.unlock();
174	        setLocked(isLocked);
175	      }
176	    });
177	
178	    // Autoplay policy: the AudioContext may only start inside a user gesture.
179	    const unlockAudio = () => audio.unlock();
180	    document.addEventListener("mousedown", unlockAudio);
181	    document.addEventListener("keydown", unlockAudio);
182	
183	    const autoSave = () => persistGame(gameEngine, flashMessage);
184	    const autoSaveId = window.setInterval(autoSave, AUTOSAVE_INTERVAL_MS);
185	    window.addEventListener("beforeunload", autoSave);
186	
187	    window.__monecraft = { engine: gameEngine, renderer, input, audio };
188	
189	    let minimap: MinimapRenderer | null = null;
190	    let last = performance.now();
191	    let animationFrame = 0;
192	    // Catch-up stepping: a slow frame (software GL, busy machine) can take far
193	    // longer than one 50ms step, and a single clamped step would run the
194	    // simulation in slow motion. Bounded substeps keep sim time tracking wall
195	    // time; the cap bounds work per frame and quietly drops time beyond it
196	    // (e.g. after a background-tab stall).
197	    const MAX_STEP_SECONDS = 0.05;
198	    const MAX_SUBSTEPS = 5;
199	    let pendingSeconds = 0;
200	    const clock = () => {
201	      const now = performance.now();
202	      pendingSeconds = Math.min(pendingSeconds + (now - last) / 1000, MAX_STEP_SECONDS * MAX_SUBSTEPS);
203	      last = now;
204	
205	      let frameSeconds = 0;
206	      while (pendingSeconds > 0) {
207	        const dt = Math.min(pendingSeconds, MAX_STEP_SECONDS);
208	        gameEngine.step(dt, input.input);
209	        pendingSeconds -= dt;
210	        frameSeconds += dt;
211	      }
212	
213	      for (const event of gameEngine.consumeEvents()) {
214	        if (event.type === "died") {
215	          input.clearKeys();
216	          if (document.pointerLockElement === renderer.domElement) document.exitPointerLock();
217	        }
218	        if (event.type === "respawned") input.clearKeys();
219	        if (event.type === "attackSwung") renderer.triggerSwing();
220	        if (event.type === "openedStation") {
221	          // A furnace opened the inventory from a mouse click — release the keys
222	          // and pointer lock the same way KeyI does on the DOM side.
223	          input.clearKeys();
224	          if (document.pointerLockElement === renderer.domElement) document.exitPointerLock();
225	        }
226	        if (event.type === "sleepDenied") {
227	          flashMessage(event.reason === "daylight" ? "You can only sleep at night" : "Monsters are nearby");
228	        }
229	        if (event.type === "pickedUp") {
230	          flashMessage(event.items.map((drop) => `+${drop.count} ${ITEM_DEF_BY_ID[drop.itemId]?.label ?? drop.itemId}`).join(", "));
231	        }
232	        renderer.handleEvent(event, gameEngine.state);
233	        audio.handleEvent(event);
234	      }
235	
236	      if (!minimap && minimapNodeRef.current) minimap = createMinimapRenderer(minimapNodeRef.current);
237	      // The minimap must read worldMeshDirty before renderer.sync clears it.
238	      minimap?.sync(gameEngine.state, now);
239	      renderer.sync(gameEngine.state, now);
240	      audio.sync(gameEngine.state, frameSeconds);
241	      renderer.render();
242	      animationFrame = requestAnimationFrame(clock);
243	    };
244	    animationFrame = requestAnimationFrame(clock);
245	
246	    return () => {
247	      delete window.__monecraft;
248	      canvasRef.current = null;
249	      rendererRef.current = null;
250	      minimap?.dispose();
251	      cancelAnimationFrame(animationFrame);
252	      window.clearInterval(autoSaveId);
253	      window.removeEventListener("beforeunload", autoSave);
254	      document.removeEventListener("mousedown", unlockAudio);
255	      document.removeEventListener("keydown", unlockAudio);
256	      audioRef.current = null;
257	      audio.dispose();
258	      input.dispose();
259	      document.exitPointerLock();
260	      renderer.dispose();
261	    };
262	  }, [ctx, flashMessage]);
263	
264	  // Re-locking can legitimately reject (e.g. Chrome's cooldown right after
265	  // Escape); the player just clicks the canvas to lock again.
266	  const requestPointerLock = useCallback(() => {
267	    const canvas = canvasRef.current;
268	    if (canvas) Promise.resolve(canvas.requestPointerLock()).catch(() => {});
269	  }, []);
270	
271	  return {
272	    attachMount,
273	    attachMinimap,
274	    locked,
275	    rendererError,
276	    selectedSlot: snapshot.selectedSlot,
277	    setSelectedSlot: (index: number) => engine?.dispatch({ type: "selectSlot", index }),
278	    capsActive: snapshot.capsActive,
279	    inventoryOpen: snapshot.inventoryOpen,
280	    inventory: snapshot.inventory,
281	    equippedArmor: snapshot.equippedArmor,
282	    armorPoints: snapshot.armorPoints,
283	    hearts: snapshot.hearts,
284	    hunger: snapshot.hunger,
285	    daylightPercent: snapshot.daylightPercent,
286	    passiveCount: snapshot.passiveCount,
287	    hostileCount: snapshot.hostileCount,
288	    respawnSeconds: snapshot.respawnSeconds,
289	    paused: snapshot.paused,
290	    sleeping: snapshot.sleeping,
291	    craftingStation: snapshot.craftingStation,
292	    debugOpen: snapshot.debugOpen,
293	    debug: snapshot.debug,
294	    saveMessage,
295	    audioSettings,
296	    updateAudioSettings,
297	    skinId,
298	    updateSkin,
299	    hotbarSlots: HOTBAR_SLOTS,
300	    recipes: RECIPES,
301	    maxHearts: MAX_HEARTS,
302	    maxHunger: MAX_HUNGER,
303	    canCraft: (recipe: Recipe) => inv.canCraft(snapshot.inventory, recipe),
304	    craft: (recipe: Recipe) => engine?.dispatch({ type: "craft", recipeId: recipe.id }),
305	    swapInventorySlots: (from: number, to: number) => engine?.dispatch({ type: "swapSlots", from, to }),
306	    toggleEquipArmor: (index: number) => engine?.dispatch({ type: "toggleEquipArmor", index }),
307	    resumeNow: () => {
308	      engine?.dispatch({ type: "resume" });
309	      requestPointerLock();
310	    },
311	    respawnNow: () => engine?.dispatch({ type: "respawn" }),
312	    saveNow: () => {
313	      if (engine) persistGame(engine, flashMessage);
314	    },
315	    loadNow: () => {
316	      if (!readSave(SAVE_KEY)) {
317	        flashMessage("No save found", 1400);
318	        return;
319	      }
320	      flashMessage("Loaded");
321	      window.setTimeout(() => window.location.reload(), 120);
322	    },
323	    resetNow: () => {
324	      try {
325	        localStorage.removeItem(SAVE_KEY);
326	        setSaveMessage("Resetting...");
327	        window.setTimeout(() => window.location.reload(), 500);
328	      } catch {
329	        flashMessage("Reset failed");
330	      }
331	    }
332	  };
333	}

   engine.dispatch({ type: \"moveStack\", from: 0, to: CONTAINER_SLOT_BASE + 0 });\n    expect(engine.state.inventory[0].id).toBeNull();\n    expect(engine.state.containers.get(idx)![0].id).toBe(\"dirt\");\n    expect(engine.state.containers.get(idx)![0].count).toBe(10);\n\n    engine.dispatch({ type: \"moveStack\", from: CONTAINER_SLOT_BASE + 0, to: 0 });\n    expect(engine.state.inventory[0].id).toBe(\"dirt\");\n    expect(engine.state.containers.get(idx)![0].id).toBeNull();\n  });\n\n  test(\"closing the inventory engine.state.openContainerIndex = 5000;\n    engine.state.inventoryOpen = true;\n    engine.dispatch({ type: \"toggleInventory\" }); // closes\n    expect(engine.state.inventoryOpen).toBe(false);\n    expect(engine.state.openContainerIndex).toBeNull();\n    expect(engine.getSnapshot().container).toBeNull();\n  });\n\n  test(\"breaking an empty chest drops the chest item and removes its container\", () => {\n    const engine = makeEngine();\n    const { idx } = aimDownAtFloor(engine);\n    const { state } = engine;\n    state.blockChanges.set(...indexToXYZ(state, idx), BlockId.Chest);\n    state.containers.set(\n      idx,\n      Array.from({ length: CHEST_SLOTS }, () => createEmptySlot())\n    );\n\n    run(engine, 4, input({ leftMouseHeld: true, pointerLocked: true }));\n\n    expect(state.world.blocks[idx]).toBe(BlockId.Air);\n    expect(state.containers.has(idx)).toBe(false);\n    expect(countsById(state.inventory).get(\"chest\")).toBe(1);\n  });\n\n  test(\"breaking a full chest spills its contents into the inventory\", () => {\n    const engine = makeEngine();\n    const { idx } = aimDownAtFloor(engine);\n    const { state } = engine;\n    state.blockChanges.set(...indexToXYZ(state, idx), BlockId.Chest);\n    const slots = Array.from({ length: CHEST_SLOTS }, () => createEmptySlot());\n    slots[0] = createSlot(\"diamond_ore\", 7);\n    state.containers.set(idx, slots);\n    engine.consumeEvents();\n\n    run(engine, 4, input({ leftMouseHeld: true, pointerLocked: true }));\n\n    expect(state.world.blocks[idx]).toBe(BlockId.Air);\n    expect(state.containers.has(idx)).toBe(false);\n    expect(countsById(state.inventory).get(\"diamond_ore\")).toBe(7);\n  });\n\n  test(\"a full chest refuses to break into a full inventory, staying intact\", () => {\n    const engine = makeEngine();\n    const { idx } = aimDownAtFloor(engine);\n    const { state } = engine;\n    state.blockChanges.set(...indexToXYZ(state, idx), BlockId.Chest);\n    const slots = Array.from({ length: CHEST_SLOTS }, () => createEmptySlot());\n    slots[0] = createSlot(\"dirt\", 5);\n    state.containers.set(idx, slots);\n    // Fill every inventory slot so the spill cannot land anywhere.\n    state.inventory = Array.from({ length: state.inventory.length }, () => createSlot(\"stone\", 99));\n    engine.consumeEvents();\n\n    run(engine, 4, input({ leftMouseHeld: true, pointerLocked: true }));\n    const events = engine.consumeEvents();\n\n    expect(state.world.blocks[idx]).toBe(BlockId.Chest); // not broken\n    expect(state.containers.get(idx)![0].id).toBe(\"dirt\"); // contents kept\n    expect(events.some((event) => event.type === \"breakBlocked\")).toBe(true);\n  });\n\n  test(\"chest contents survive a save round-trip\", () => {\n    const engine = makeEngine();\n    const idx = engine.state.world.index(20, 40, 20);\n    engine.state.blockChanges.set(20, 40, 20, BlockId.Chest);\n    const slots = Array.from({ length: CHEST_SLOTS }, () => createEmptySlot());\n    slots[0] = createSlot(\"gold_ore\", 3);\n    slots[1] = { ...createSlot(\"diamond_sword\", 1), durability: 200 };\n    engine.state.containers.set(idx, slots);\n\n    const restored = makeEngine(engine.serialize());\n    const container = restored.state.containers.get(idx);\n    expect(container).toBeDefined();\n    expect(container![0].id).toBe(\"gold_ore\");\n    expect(container![0].count).toBe(3);\n    expect(container![1].durability).toBe(200);\n  });\n});\n\n/** Decodes a voxel index back to [x, y, z] for the active world. */\nfunction indexToXYZ(state: GameEngine[\"state\"], idx: number): [number, number, number] {\n  const layer = state.world.sizeX * state.world.sizeZ;\n  const y = Math.floor(idx / layer);\n  const rem = idx - y * layer;\n  const z = Math.floor(rem / state.world.sizeX);\n  const x = rem - z * state.world.sizeX;\n  return [x, y, z];\n}"
}

  "command": "git add -A && git commit -q -F - <<'EOF'\nfeat(engine): chests — place, open, move, break-spill\n\nWire the chest block-entity behavior into the simulation, joining the\nexisting right-click interact precedence and the mining/placement paths.\n\n- GameState gains a `containers` map (voxel index -> 27 slots) and an\n  `openContainerIndex`; GameSnapshot exposes the open chest's `container`.\n- Right-clicking a chest opens it in the inventory panel (interact wins\n  over placement, like bed/furnace), lazily creating its empty store.\n- Placing a chest creates a fresh empty container; serialize() now\n  persists all chest contents and the boot path restores them for indices\n  that still hold a Chest block.\n- Breaking a chest empties it into the inventory first (durability\n  preserved via tryInsertSlots) and announces the haul with a pickedUp\n  toast; if it will not all fit the break is refused and the chest stays\n  intact (new breakBlocked event).\n- New moveStack command moves a slot across the inventory/chest boundary\n  (container indices offset by CONTAINER_SLOT_BASE); closing the panel or\n  pausing clears the open chest.\n\nEngine tests cover place, open, move both ways, empty/full break, the\nrefuse-into-full-inventory case, and a chest save round-trip. No worldgen\nimpact; the save bump is the additive v4 from the previous commit.\nEOF\ngit log --oneline -1",
  "description": "Commit slice 4"
}

64	      <div className="game-root">
65	        <div className="renderer-error">
66	          <h2>Could not start the 3D renderer</h2>
67	          <p>WebGL appears to be unavailable in this browser ({rendererError}).</p>
68	          <p>Try enabling hardware acceleration or switching browsers, then reload.</p>
69	        </div>
70	      </div>
71	    );
72	  }
73	
74	  const showClickHint = !locked && !paused && !inventoryOpen && respawnSeconds === 0;
75	
76	  return (
77	    <div className="game-root">
78	      <div ref={attachMount} className="game-canvas-wrap" />
79	      <div className="vignette" aria-hidden="true" />
80	
81	      {debugOpen ? <DebugOverlay debug={debug} passiveCount={passiveCount} hostileCount={hostileCount} daylightPercent={daylightPercent} /> : null}
82	
83	      {showClickHint ? <div className="click-hint">Click to play</div> : null}
84	
85	      {saveMessage && !paused ? (
86	        <div className="hud-toast" role="status">
87	          {saveMessage}
88	        </div>
89	      ) : null}
90	
91	      <div ref={attachMinimap} className="minimap" data-testid="minimap" />
92	
93	      <div className="hud-bottom">
94	        <StatusBars hearts={hearts} maxHearts={maxHearts} hunger={hunger} maxHunger={maxHunger} armorPoints={armorPoints} />
95	        <Hotbar inventory={inventory} selectedSlot={selectedSlot} hotbarSlots={hotbarSlots} onSelectSlot={setSelectedSlot} />
96	      </div>
97	
98	      {inventoryOpen || paused ? <div className="menu-backdrop" /> : null}
99	
100	      {inventoryOpen ? (
101	        <InventoryPanel
102	          inventory={inventory}
103	          equippedArmor={equippedArmor}
104	          selectedHotbarSlot={selectedSlot}
105	          hotbarSlots={hotbarSlots}
106	          recipes={recipes}
107	          craftingStation={craftingStation}
108	          canCraft={canCraft}
109	          onSwapSlots={swapInventorySlots}
110	          onToggleEquipArmor={toggleEquipArmor}
111	          onCraft={craft}
112	        />
113	      ) : null}
114	
115	      {paused ? (
116	        <PauseMenu
117	          saveMessage={saveMessage}
```

container?.[index - CONTAINER_SLOT_BASE] : inventory[index];\n\n  const onSlotClick = (index: number) => {\n    const slot = slotAt(index);\n    if (!slot) return;\n    // Clicking armor in the player inventory equips it; chest slots only move.\n    if (index < CONTAINER_SLOT_BASE && slot.kind === \"armor\" && slot.count > 0) {\n      onToggleEquipArmor(index);\n      setPendingIndex(null);\n      return;\n    }\n\n    if (pendingIndex === null) {\n      setPendingIndex(index);\n      return;\n    }\n    if (pendingIndex === index) {\n      setPendingIndex(null);\n      return;\n    }\n    (container ? 178	    handleEvent(event) {
179	      const backend = graph?.backend;
180	      if (!backend) return;
181	      switch (event.type) {
182	        case "blockBroken":
183	          backend.play(BREAK_SOUNDS[materialGroupFor(event.blockId)]);
184	          break;
185	        case "blockPlaced":
186	          backend.play(PLACE_SOUNDS[materialGroupFor(event.blockId)]);
187	          break;
188	        case "playerHurt":
189	          backend.play(HURT_SOUND);
190	          break;
191	        case "ateFood":
192	          backend.play(EAT_SOUND);
193	          break;
194	        case "jumped":
195	          backend.play(JUMP_SOUND);
196	          break;
197	        case "landed":
198	          backend.play(LAND_SOUND, { gain: Math.min(1.2, Math.max(0.3, event.impact / FULL_LANDING_IMPACT)) });
199	          break;
200	        case "mobAttacked":
201	          backend.play(MOB_ATTACK_SOUNDS[event.kind]);
202	          break;
203	        case "mobHit":
204	          backend.play(MOB_HIT_SOUND);
205	          break;
206	        case "mobDied":
207	          backend.play(MOB_DEATH_SOUND);
208	          break;
209	        case "died":
210	          backend.play(DEATH_SOUND);
211	          break;
212	        case "respawned":
213	          backend.play(RESPAWN_SOUND);
214	          break;
215	        case "sleepStarted":
216	          backend.play(SLEEP_SOUND);
217	          break;
218	        case "wokeUp":
219	          backend.play(WAKE_SOUND);
220	          break;
221	        case "tilledSoil":
222	          backend.play(TILL_SOUND);
223	          break;
224	        case "plantedSeed":
225	          backend.play(PLANT_SOUND);
226	          break;
227	        case "smelted":
228	          backend.play(SMELT_SOUND);
229	          break;
230	        case "mobFed":
231	          backend.play(MOB_FED_SOUND);
232	          break;
233	        case "mobBred":
234	          backend.play(MOB_BRED_SOUND);
235	          break;
236	      }
237	    },

1	import { describe, expect, mock, test } from "bun:test";
2	import { render, screen } from "@testing-library/react";
3	import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
4	import InventoryPanel from "@/components/game/InventoryPanel";
5	import { HOTBAR_SLOTS, INVENTORY_SLOTS } from "@/lib/game/config";
6	import { createEmptyArmorEquipment, createEmptySlot, createSlot } from "@/lib/game/items";
7	import { RECIPES } from "@/lib/game/recipes";
8	import type { InventorySlot } from "@/lib/game/types";
9	
10	function makeInventory(...items: Array<[string, number] | null>): InventorySlot[] {
11	  const slots = Array.from({ length: INVENTORY_SLOTS }, () => createEmptySlot());
12	  items.forEach((item, i) => {
13	    if (item) slots[i] = createSlot(item[0], item[1]);
14	  });
15	  return slots;
16	}
17	
18	function renderPanel(overrides: Partial<Parameters<typeof InventoryPanel>[0]> = {}) {
19	  const props = {
20	    inventory: makeInventory(["dirt", 10], ["stone", 5], ["helmet", 1]),
21	    equippedArmor: createEmptyArmorEquipment(),
22	    selectedHotbarSlot: 0,
23	    hotbarSlots: HOTBAR_SLOTS,
24	    recipes: RECIPES,
25	    craftingStation: null as "furnace" | null,
26	    canCraft: () => true,
27	    onSwapSlots: mock(),
28	    onToggleEquipArmor: mock(),
29	    onCraft: mock(),
30	    ...overrides
31	  };
32	  render(<InventoryPanel {...props} />);
33	  return props;
34	}
35	
36	function slotButtons() {
37	  // Inventory slot wells, excluding the armor column and recipe entries.
38	  return screen.getAllByRole("button").filter((button) => button.className.includes("inv-slot") && !button.className.includes("armor-slot"));
39	}
40	
41	describe("InventoryPanel", () => {
42	  test("renders all inventory slots plus recipe buttons", () => {
43	    renderPanel();
44	    expect(slotButtons()).toHaveLength(INVENTORY_SLOTS);
45	    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "2 Wood -> 4 Planks" })).toBeTruthy();
46	  });
47	
48	  test("slots are ordered storage first, hotbar row last, with icons and counts", () => {
49	    renderPanel();
50	    const slots = slotButtons();
51	    // DOM order: the 27 storage slots (indices 9..35) render above the hotbar row (0..8).
52	    expect(slots[27].getAttribute("aria-label")).toBe("Slot 1: Dirt");
53	    // Dirt appears as the slot icon and again inside recipe ingredients.
54	    expect(screen.getAllByAltText("Dirt").length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
55	    expect(screen.getByText("10")).toBeTruthy(); // the dirt stack count
56	  });
57	
58	  test("clicking two slots swaps them via onSwapSlots", async () => {
59	    const user = userEvent.setup();
60	    const props = renderPanel();
61	    const slots = slotButtons();
62	
63	    await user.click(slots[0]); // pending — first storage slot, inventory index 9
64	    expect(slots[0].className).toContain("pending");
65	    await user.click(slots[1]);
66	    expect(props.onSwapSlots).toHaveBeenCalledWith(9, 10);
67	  });
68	
69	  test("clicking the same slot twice cancels the pending selection", async () => {
70	    const user = userEvent.setup();
71	    const props = renderPanel();
72	    const slots = slotButtons();
73	
74	    await user.click(slots[0]);
75	    await user.click(slots[0]);
76	    expect(slots[0].className).not.toContain("pending");
77	    expect(props.onSwapSlots).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
78	
79	    await user.click(slots[1]); // a fresh click starts a new pending selection
80	    expect(props.onSwapSlots).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
81	  });
82	
83	  test("clicking an armor item toggles equipment instead of swapping", async () => {
84	    const user = userEvent.setup();
85	    const props = renderPanel();
86	    const slots = slotButtons();
87	
88	    await user.click(slots[27 + 2]); // the helmet sits in hotbar slot 2
89	    expect(props.onToggleEquipArmor).toHaveBeenCalledWith(2);
90	    expect(props.onSwapSlots).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
91	  });
92	
93	  test("equipped armor is shown in its armor slot with durability and unequips on click", async () => {
94	    const user = userEvent.setup();
95	    const props = renderPanel({ equippedArmor: { ...createEmptyArmorEquipment(), helmet: "helmet" } });
96	    const helmetSlot = screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Helmet: Helmet" });
97	    expect(helmetSlot.className).toContain("filled");
98	    await user.hover(helmetSlot);
99	    expect(screen.getByText("Durability 260 / 260")).toBeTruthy(); // surfaced in the hover tooltip
100	    await user.click(helmetSlot);
101	    expect(props.onToggleEquipArmor).toHaveBeenCalledWith(2); // the helmet's inventory index
102	  });
103	
104	  test("empty armor slots show a ghost icon and ignore clicks", async () => {
105	    const user = userEvent.setup();

108	    await user.click(bootsSlot);

111	
112	  test("craft buttons are disabled per canCraft and click through onCraft", async () => {
113	    const user = userEvent.setup();

}

1	# Player Manual
2	
3	A complete guide to playing Monecraft — a single-player, browser-based voxel
4	sandbox. Mine, build, craft, farm, fight off the night, and survive.
5	
6	This page is the prose guide; for the exact numbers (every recipe, block, mob, and
7	item stat) see the [reference tables](reference.md). Just want the keys? They're in
8	the [README](../README.md#controls).
9	
10	> Everything you see and hear is generated from code at runtime — there are no
11	> image or audio files anywhere in the game.
12	
13	## Getting started: your first day
14	
15	You spawn at dawn on solid ground with a small starter kit: stacks of grass, dirt,
16	stone, wood, planks, cobble, and sand, plus a **wood pickaxe** and a **knife**.
17	
18	The day-night cycle is short — **four minutes per full day** — so the first night
19	comes fast. A good opening:
20	
21	1. **Look around.** Click the game once to lock the mouse (the first click only
22	   grabs the pointer; it doesn't mine). Move with `W A S D`, look with the mouse.
23	2. **Gear up from your kit.** Open the inventory with **`I`**. You already have
24	   cobble and wood, so craft a **stone pickaxe** (2 cobble + 1 wood) and a **stone
25	   sword** (2 cobble + 1 wood) — both upgrades over your starting wood pickaxe and
26	   knife.
27	3. **Mine and gather.** Hold **left-click** to break trees, stone, and dirt for
28	   more materials; craft **2 wood → 4 planks** whenever you need them.
29	4. **Shelter before dark.** Wall yourself in with blocks (**right-click** or `E`
30	   to place the selected hotbar item), or craft a **bed** and skip the night
31	   entirely. Hostile mobs spawn once it gets dark, so don't be caught in the open.
32	
33	From there it's a sandbox: dig for ores, build, farm, breed animals, and gear up.
34	
35	## Controls
36	
37	| Key / input        | Action                                                              |
38	| ------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------- |
39	| `W` `A` `S` `D`    | Move (walk / strafe)                                                |
40	| `Space`            | Jump                                                                |
41	| `C`                | Crouch (slower, careful movement)                                   |
42	| `W` + `CapsLock`   | Sprint — faster, but drains hunger                                  |
43	| Mouse              | Look around (click the game first to lock the pointer)              |
44	| Left-click (hold)  | Break the targeted block / attack a mob                             |
45	| Right-click or `E` | Place the selected block / interact (bed, furnace, hoe, seeds)      |
46	| `1`–`9`            | Select a hotbar slot                                                |
47	| `I`                | Open / close inventory & crafting                                   |
48	| `F`                | Eat the selected food                                               |
49	| `V`                | Cycle camera: first-person → third-person rear → third-person front |
50	| `U`                | Emergency unstuck (teleport to safe ground if wedged)               |
51	| `Esc`              | Pause menu (save / load / reset, volume sliders, skin picker)       |
52	| `F3`               | Debug overlay (FPS, position, daylight, mob counts)                 |
53	
54	Gameplay is always **eye-relative**: even in third person, your reach, aim, and
55	audio follow where your eyes point, and the crosshair stays centered.
56	
57	## Survival
58	
59	### Health and death
60	
61	You have **20 health**, shown as **10 hearts** (each heart = 2 HP). Mobs, falling
62	into the void, and starvation-adjacent danger chip it away. At zero health you die,
63	freeze for **3 seconds**, then respawn — at your bed if you've slept in one,
64	otherwise at a random land point. Death doesn't wipe your world or inventory.
65	
66	If you ever get wedged inside terrain, press **`U`** to teleport free. (Standing in
67	water is _not_ "stuck" — you're allowed to swim.)
68	
69	### Hunger
70	
71	You have **20 hunger**, shown as **10 drumsticks**. Activity burns it:
72	
73	- ~1 hunger per **100 blocks sprinted**
74	- ~1 hunger per **300 blocks walked**
75	- ~1 hunger per **50 jumps**
76	
77	Hunger gates two things:
78	
79	- **Sprinting** needs hunger above **6** — below that you slow down.
80	- **Health regeneration** only runs while hunger is **12 or higher**: you heal
81	  about **+0.5 HP every 3 seconds** when you're fed and hurt.
82	
83	So eat before a fight. Press **`F`** to eat the selected food. Different foods
84	restore different amounts — cooked meat fills far more than raw (see the
85	[food table](reference.md#food)).
86	
87	### Combat
88	
89	Hold **left-click** to swing. You hit a mob if it's within **~4.5 blocks** and
90	close to your crosshair (a fairly tight aim cone). Bare fists do **6 damage**;
91	weapons do much more and have durability that ticks down with each hit. A landed
92	blow knocks the mob back.
93	
94	**Armor** reduces incoming damage. Equip a helmet, face mask, neck protection,
95	chestplate, leggings, and boots from the inventory's armor slots. Each piece adds
96	defense and absorbs durability when you're struck — but a hit always deals at
97	least **1 damage**, even in a full set.
98	
99	## Day, night, and danger
100	
101	A full day lasts **240 seconds (4 minutes)**. Daylight rises and falls on a smooth
102	curve from ~0.04 at midnight to ~1.0 at noon. Three thresholds matter:
103	
104	| Daylight       | What happens                                                              |
105	| -------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
106	| below **0.28** | Night: hostile mobs spawn, and you may sleep                              |
107	| below **0.42** | Spiders turn hostile (they're passive in twilight and day)                |
108	| above **0.72** | Hostile mobs caught in open sunlight catch fire and burn (caves are safe) |
109	
110	The game boots at a dim dawn, so the very first mobs can already be active —
111	build or sleep quickly.
112	
113	**Weather and sky.** It periodically **rains** — or **snows** up in the mountains —
114	with clouds rolling in, the sky greying over, and the light dimming; deserts and
115	oceans stay clear. At night the sky fills with **stars and a moon**, while clouds
116	drift overhead by day. Weather is atmospheric only: it doesn't change how mobs
117	spawn or behave, and it isn't saved.
118	
119	## Mining and building
120	
121	**Break** a block by holding left-click; a crack overlay shows progress, and harder
122	blocks take longer. **Place** the selected hotbar item with right-click (or `E`)
123	against a surface. Your reach is about **7 blocks**.
124	
125	**Tools gate what you can mine.** A pickaxe's tier determines which blocks it can
126	harvest at all — mining with too weak a tool yields nothing:
127	
128	| To mine…                  | You need at least… |
129	| ------------------------- | ------------------ |
130	| Stone, cobblestone, brick | Wood pickaxe       |
131	| Sliver ore                | Stone pickaxe      |
132	| Ruby & gold ore           | Sliver pickaxe     |
133	| Sapphire & diamond ore    | Ruby pickaxe       |
134	
135	Higher-tier pickaxes (sapphire, gold, diamond) also mine **faster**. Dirt, sand,
136	wood, leaves, and crops need no special tool. Ores hide underground and in caves;
137	the rarer the ore, the deeper it tends to sit.
138	
139	## Crafting
140	
141	Open the inventory (`I`) to reach the crafting panel. Pick a recipe whose
142	ingredients you have and craft it — the result drops into your inventory (crafting
143	is refused if there's no room rather than destroying the overflow). Stacks hold up
144	to **99** items; tools, weapons, and armor take one slot each and show durability.
145	
146	The basic recipe grid is always available. **Smelting** recipes (cooking raw meat)
147	require a **furnace**: craft one from 8 cobble, place it, and right-click it to open
148	the panel in furnace mode — the cooking recipes unlock while a furnace is open and
149	show as "Requires Furnace" otherwise.
150	
151	See the full list of **31 recipes** in the [reference](reference.md#recipes).
152	
153	## Mobs and breeding
154	
155	Six creatures roam the world:
156	
157	- **Passive** — **sheep**, **chicken**, **horse**. They wander, flee when you get
158	  close, and never attack. They drop materials and raw meat when killed.
159	- **Hostile** — **zombie**, **skeleton**, **spider**. They hunt you at night,
160	  chase within their detection range, and attack when they have line of sight.
161	  Spiders are only hostile in the dark.
162	
163	Full stats and drop tables are in the [bestiary](reference.md#mobs).
164	
165	**Breeding** makes loot renewable. Right-click an adult **sheep or horse with
166	wheat**, or a **chicken with seeds**, to put it "in love" for **30 seconds**. Two
167	in-love adults of the same kind standing within **3 blocks** produce a **baby**,
168	which follows its parents, drops nothing while young, and grows to full size after
169	**~90 seconds**. The passive population is capped (and feeding costs crops), so it
170	stays under control.
171	
172	## Farming
173	
174	Grow your own food:
175	
176	1. Craft a **wood hoe** (2 planks + 1 wood).
177	2. Right-click **grass or dirt** with the hoe to till it into **farmland**.
178	3. Right-click farmland with **seeds** to plant wheat. (Seeds come from breaking
179	   grass — about a 1-in-5 chance per block.)
180	4. Wait. Crops grow through four stages over **~2.5 minutes** (growth ticks happen
181	   near you). Breaking an **immature** crop just returns its seed; a **mature**
182	   crop harvests into **wheat plus 1–2 seeds**.
183	5. Craft **3 wheat → bread**, or use wheat to breed sheep and horses.
184	
185	## Beds and sleeping
186	
187	Craft a **bed** (3 wool + 3 planks) and place it. Right-click it **at night** to
188	sleep: the screen fades to black, time skips to a fresh morning, and the bed
189	becomes your **respawn point**. Sleeping is refused during the day or when a
190	hostile mob is within **12 blocks**, with an on-screen reason. (Wool comes from
191	sheep, or from crafting 4 string — a spider drop — into 1 wool.)
192	
193	## Sound and music
194	
195	All audio is synthesized live — there are no sound files. Block breaks, footsteps,
196	mob calls, and combat all have procedural effects keyed to the material or
197	creature. The ambient music is generative: it shifts between brighter major-key
198	moods by day and darker minor-key moods at night, and changes flavor by biome
199	(desert, ocean, forest, mountains). Adjust **master** and **music** volume — and
200	pick your **player skin** — from the pause menu (`Esc`).
201	
202	## Saving
203	
204	Your world autosaves to the browser's `localStorage` every **15 seconds**, and you
205	can **Save**, **Load**, or **Reset** manually from the pause menu. A save stores the
206	world seed plus your edits, inventory, armor, stats, time of day, and bed spawn —
207	so the world regenerates identically and picks up where you left off. Because saves
208	live in the browser, they're tied to that browser on that device; "Reset" starts a
209	fresh world.
210	
```
