1	# Gameplay Reference
2	
3	Scannable tables for everything in the game: recipes, blocks, mobs, and item
4	stats. For how it all fits together, read the [player manual](manual.md).
5	
6	> **The code is the source of truth.** These tables are a convenience snapshot of
7	> `lib/game/recipes.ts`, `lib/game/items.ts`, `lib/world/blocks.ts`,
8	> `lib/game/mobs.ts`, and `lib/game/mobLoot.ts`. If a number here disagrees with
9	> the game, the code wins — and this page needs an update.
10	
11	## Recipes
12	
13	**31 recipes.** All use the always-available crafting grid except the two
14	**furnace** smelting recipes, which need an open furnace.
15	
16	### Building & materials
17	
18	| Result    | Ingredients       |
19	| --------- | ----------------- |
20	| 4 Planks  | 2 Wood            |
21	| 2 Glass   | 4 Sand            |
22	| 2 Brick   | 2 Dirt + 2 Stone  |
23	| 1 Furnace | 8 Cobble          |
24	| 1 Bed     | 3 Wool + 3 Planks |
25	| 1 Wool    | 4 String          |
26	
27	### Tools
28	
29	| Result           | Ingredients             |
30	| ---------------- | ----------------------- |
31	| Wood Hoe         | 2 Planks + 1 Wood       |
32	| Wood Pickaxe     | 2 Planks + 2 Wood       |
33	| Stone Pickaxe    | 2 Cobble + 1 Wood       |
34	| Sliver Pickaxe   | 2 Sliver Ore + 1 Wood   |
35	| Ruby Pickaxe     | 2 Ruby Ore + 1 Wood     |
36	| Sapphire Pickaxe | 2 Sapphire Ore + 1 Wood |
37	| Gold Pickaxe     | 2 Gold Ore + 1 Wood     |
38	| Diamond Pickaxe  | 2 Diamond Ore + 1 Wood  |
39	
40	### Weapons
41	
42	| Result         | Ingredients             |
43	| -------------- | ----------------------- |
44	| Knife          | 1 Stone + 1 Wood        |
45	| Wood Sword     | 2 Planks + 1 Wood       |
46	| Stone Sword    | 2 Cobble + 1 Wood       |
47	| Sliver Sword   | 2 Sliver Ore + 1 Wood   |
48	| Ruby Sword     | 2 Ruby Ore + 1 Wood     |
49	| Sapphire Sword | 2 Sapphire Ore + 1 Wood |
50	| Gold Sword     | 2 Gold Ore + 1 Wood     |
51	| Diamond Sword  | 2 Diamond Ore + 1 Wood  |
52	
53	### Armor
54	
55	| Result          | Ingredients                 |
56	| --------------- | --------------------------- |
57	| Helmet          | 4 Sapphire Ore + 1 Ruby Ore |
58	| Face Mask       | 2 Ruby Ore + 2 Sapphire Ore |
59	| Neck Protection | 2 Gold Ore + 1 Sapphire Ore |
60	| Chestplate      | 5 Gold Ore + 2 Sapphire Ore |
61	| Leggings        | 4 Gold Ore + 2 Ruby Ore     |
62	| Boots           | 2 Sapphire Ore + 2 Gold Ore |
63	
64	### Food & smelting
65	
66	| Result         | Ingredients            | Station |
67	| -------------- | ---------------------- | ------- |
68	| Bread          | 3 Wheat                | —       |
69	| Cooked Chicken | Raw Chicken + 1 Planks | Furnace |
70	| Cooked Mutton  | Raw Mutton + 1 Planks  | Furnace |
71	
72	## Blocks
73	
74	**26 block types** (plus air). Hardness is relative break time — higher is slower.
75	"Mine with" is the minimum tool needed; blocks with no requirement break with bare
76	hands or any tool. Bedrock and water cannot be broken.
77	
78	| Block             | Hardness | Mine with      | Notes                                       |
79	| ----------------- | -------- | -------------- | ------------------------------------------- |
80	| Grass             | 2        | any            | Drops dirt; ~20% chance to also drop a seed |
81	| Dirt              | 2        | any            | —                                           |
82	| Sand              | 2        | any            | Common on beaches and in deserts            |
83	| Snow              | 2        | any            | Mountain peaks                              |
84	| Leaves            | 2        | any            | From trees; drops dirt                      |
85	| Cactus            | 2        | any            | Desert decoration                           |
86	| Glass             | 2        | any            | Crafted from sand                           |
87	| Wood              | 3        | any            | Tree trunks                                 |
88	| Planks            | 3        | any            | Crafted from wood                           |
89	| Stone             | 5        | Wood Pickaxe   | Drops the stone item                        |
90	| Cobblestone       | 5        | Wood Pickaxe   | Drops cobble (crafting staple)              |
91	| Brick             | 5        | Wood Pickaxe   | Crafted; also found in houses               |
92	| Furnace           | 5        | Wood Pickaxe   | **Interactive** — opens smelting recipes    |
93	| Sliver Ore        | 7        | Stone Pickaxe  | —                                           |
94	| Ruby Ore          | 9        | Sliver Pickaxe | —                                           |
95	| Gold Ore          | 11       | Sliver Pickaxe | —                                           |
96	| Sapphire Ore      | 12       | Ruby Pickaxe   | —                                           |
97	| Diamond Ore       | 14       | Ruby Pickaxe   | Deepest, rarest ore                         |
98	| Bed               | 2        | any            | **Interactive** — sleep & set spawn         |
99	| Farmland          | 1        | any            | Tilled soil; reverts to dirt when broken    |
100	| Wheat (stage 0–2) | 1        | any            | Immature crop; drops its seed               |
101	| Wheat (stage 3)   | 1        | any            | Mature crop; drops wheat + 1–2 seeds        |
102	| Bedrock           | —        | unbreakable    | World floor and border                      |
103	| Water             | —        | —              | Liquid; sea level fills below y≈43          |
104

104	
105	## Mobs
106	
107	**6 mob kinds.** Passive animals flee but never attack and can be bred; hostiles
108	hunt at night. Drop counts are inclusive ranges rolled per kill.
109	
110	| Mob      | Type    | HP  | Speed | Detect range | Attack | Cooldown | Drops                      |
111	| -------- | ------- | --- | ----- | ------------ | ------ | -------- | -------------------------- |
112	| Sheep    | passive | 10  | 0.9   | —            | —      | —        | 1–2 Wool, 1 Raw Mutton     |
113	| Chicken  | passive | 7   | 1.2   | —            | —      | —        | 0–2 Feather, 1 Raw Chicken |
114	| Horse    | passive | 14  | 1.4   | —            | —      | —        | 1–2 Leather                |
115	| Zombie   | hostile | 10  | 1.05  | 11           | 3      | 1.35 s   | 1–2 Rotten Flesh           |
116	| Skeleton | hostile | 9   | 1.08  | 12           | 3      | 1.4 s    | 1–2 Bone                   |
117	| Spider   | hostile | 8   | 1.2   | 10           | 2      | 1.1 s    | 0–2 String                 |
118	
119	Spiders are hostile only in the dark (daylight below 0.42); in twilight and day
120	they behave like passives. **Breeding:** feed wheat to a sheep or horse, or seeds
121	to a chicken; babies grow up in ~90 s. Passive population is capped at 24, hostiles
122	at 16.
123	
124	## Items
125	
126	### Tools
127	
128	`minePower` scales break speed; `tier` gates which ore a pickaxe can harvest
129	(stone/cobble/brick ≥ 1, sliver ≥ 2, ruby & gold ≥ 3, sapphire & diamond ≥ 4).
130	
131	| Item             | Mine power | Tier | Durability |
132	| ---------------- | ---------- | ---- | ---------- |
133	| Wood Hoe         | 1.0        | 0    | 90         |
134	| Wood Pickaxe     | 1.05       | 1    | 70         |
135	| Stone Pickaxe    | 1.55       | 2    | 140        |
136	| Sliver Pickaxe   | 2.2        | 3    | 240        |
137	| Ruby Pickaxe     | 2.8        | 4    | 340        |
138	| Sapphire Pickaxe | 3.3        | 5    | 430        |
139	| Gold Pickaxe     | 3.8        | 6    | 520        |
140	| Diamond Pickaxe  | 4.4        | 7    | 700        |
141	
142	### Weapons
143	
144	A bare fist deals 6 damage for comparison.
145	
146	| Item           | Attack | Durability |
147	| -------------- | ------ | ---------- |
148	| Knife          | 9      | 50         |
149	| Wood Sword     | 13     | 80         |
150	| Stone Sword    | 18     | 160        |
151	| Sliver Sword   | 24     | 260        |
152	| Ruby Sword     | 31     | 360        |
153	| Sapphire Sword | 35     | 450        |
154	| Gold Sword     | 40     | 540        |
155	| Diamond Sword  | 47     | 720        |
156	
157	### Armor
158	
159	Each piece occupies its own equip slot. Defense reduces incoming damage (a hit
160	always deals at least 1).
161	
162	| Item            | Slot            | Defense | Durability |
163	| --------------- | --------------- | ------- | ---------- |
164	| Helmet          | helmet          | 2       | 260        |
165	| Face Mask       | face_mask       | 2       | 220        |
166	| Neck Protection | neck_protection | 2       | 230        |
167	| Chestplate      | chestplate      | 4       | 420        |
168	| Leggings        | leggings        | 3       | 340        |
169	| Boots           | boots           | 2       | 250        |
170	
171	### Food
172	
173	`Eat` (`F`) restores the listed hunger.
174	
175	| Item           | Hunger restored | Source             |
176	| -------------- | --------------- | ------------------ |
177	| Cooked Chicken | 8               | Smelt raw chicken  |
178	| Cooked Mutton  | 8               | Smelt raw mutton   |
179	| Food           | 7               | Generic food item  |
180	| Bread          | 6               | Craft from 3 wheat |
181	| Raw Chicken    | 3               | Chicken drop       |
182	| Raw Mutton     | 3               | Sheep drop         |
183	| Rotten Flesh   | 2               | Zombie drop        |
184	
185	### Materials
186	
187	Crafting ingredients with no direct use on their own: **Wool**, **Feather**,
188	**Bone**, **Leather**, **String** (mob drops), and **Wheat Seeds** / **Wheat**
189	(from farming and grass).
190

3	test("boots without errors and renders the world", async ({ gamePage: page }) => {
4	  await expect(page.locator(".game-canvas-wrap canvas")).toBeVisible();
5	  await expect(page.getByTestId("hotbar")).toBeVisible();
6	  const triangles = await page.evaluate(() => window.__monecraft!.renderer.renderedTriangles());
7	  expect(triangles).toBeGreaterThan(0);
8	
9	  // The audio director is wired up (still locked pre-gesture — the shared
10	  // console-error fixture proves boot stays clean without any unlock).
11	  expect(await page.evaluate(() => Boolean(window.__monecraft!.audio))).toBe(true);
12	
13	  // The engine is alive: the day clock advances between frames.
14	  const clock1 = await page.evaluate(() => window.__monecraft!.engine.state.dayClock);
15	  await page.waitForTimeout(200);
16	  const clock2 = await page.evaluate(() => window.__monecraft!.engine.state.dayClock);
17	  expect(clock2).toBeGreaterThan(clock1);
18	});

20	test("pointer-lock flow enables WASD movement", async ({ gamePage: page }) => {
21	  await calmDaytime(page);
22	  await acquirePointerLock(page);
23	
24	  await page.waitForTimeout(500); // settle onto the ground
25	  const before = await playerPosition(page);
26	  await page.keyboard.down("w");
27	  await page.waitForTimeout(700);
28	  await page.keyboard.up("w");

30	
31	  const moved = Math.hypot(after.x - before.x, after.z - before.z);
32	  expect(moved).toBeGreaterThan(0.5);
33	});

57	  await acquirePointerLock(page);
58	  await page.waitForTimeout(1000); // settle (slow CI renderers need the margin)
59	
60	  // Aim straight down from the center of the cell (a ray origin exactly on a
61	  // cell boundary may target the diagonal neighbor — see docs/testing.md).
62	  await page.evaluate(() => {

64	    player.pitch = -Math.PI / 2 + 0.02;

67	  });
68	
69	  await page.mouse.down();

71	  await expect.poll(async () => page.evaluate(() => window.__monecraft!.engine.state.blockChanges.changes().length), { timeout: 30000 }).toBeGreaterThan(0);
72	  await page.mouse.up();

75	test("right-click still places a block when not aimed at an interactive one", async ({ gamePage: page }) => {
76	  // The bed/furnace interact dispatcher sits in front of placement; this guards
77	  // that a non-interactive target still falls through to placing a block.
78	  await calmDaytime(page);

91	    const ey = Math.floor(state.player.position.y + 1.62);

93	    state.blockChanges.set(ex, ey, ez - 1, 0);
94	    state.blockChanges.set(ex, ey, ez - 2, 0);
95	    state.blockChanges.set(ex, ey, ez - 3, 3);
96	    state.selectedSlot = 0; // starter grass blocks
97	    const before = state.world.get(ex, ey, ez - 2);
98	    window.__monecraft!.engine.dispatch({ type: "placeBlock" });
99	    const after = state.world.get(ex, ey, ez - 2);
100	    return { before, after };
101	  });

103	  expect(placed.after).toBe(1); // grass placed
104	});

1	import { test as base, expect, type Page } from "@playwright/test";
2	
3	/**
4	 * Shared E2E plumbing. Tests assert against the live simulation through the
5	 * window.__monecraft debug handle rather than pixels — see docs/testing.md.
6	 */
7	
8	export const test = base.extend<{ gamePage: Page }>({
9	  // A page that has booted the game, with console errors treated as failures.
10	  // (The fixture continuation is named `runTest`, not Playwright's
11	  // conventional `use`, to avoid colliding with React's rules-of-hooks lint.)
12	  gamePage: async ({ page }, runTest) => {
13	    const errors: string[] = [];
14	    page.on("console", (message) => {
15	      if (message.type() !== "error") return;
16	      // The app ships no favicon; Chrome's automatic /favicon.ico 404 is noise.
17	      if (message.text().includes("Failed to load resource") && message.location().url.endsWith("/favicon.ico")) return;
18	      errors.push(`${message.text()} (${message.location().url})`);
19	    });
20	    page.on("pageerror", (error) => errors.push(String(error)));
21	
22	    await page.goto("/");
23	    await page.waitForFunction(() => window.__monecraft !== undefined, undefined, { timeout: 30000 });
24	    // Wait for the first frames so the engine has stepped and the scene drew.
25	    await page.waitForFunction(() => window.__monecraft!.renderer.renderedTriangles() > 0, undefined, { timeout: 30000 });
26	
27	    await runTest(page);
28	
29	    expect(errors, "no console/page errors during the test").toEqual([]);
30	  }
31	});
32	
33	export { expect };
34	
35	/**
36	 * The game boots at dawn with aggro hostiles near the spawn; interactive
37	 * tests clear them and move to midday so knockback can't disrupt input.
38	 */
39	export async function calmDaytime(page: Page): Promise<void> {
40	  await page.evaluate(() => {
41	    const state = window.__monecraft!.engine.state;
42	    state.mobs = state.mobs.filter((mob) => !mob.hostile);
43	    state.dayClock = 60;
44	  });
45	}
46	
47	export async function playerPosition(page: Page): Promise<{ x: number; y: number; z: number }> {
48	  return page.evaluate(() => {
49	    const { position } = window.__monecraft!.engine.state.player;
50	    return { x: position.x, y: position.y, z: position.z };
51	  });
52	}
53	
54	/**
55	 * Clicks the canvas to request pointer lock; if the browser refuses (headless
56	 * Chromium cannot engage pointer lock at all), forces the input controller's
57	 * lock flag so everything downstream — keys → engine, held mouse → mining —
58	 * is still exercised for real. The acquisition UX itself stays in the manual
59	 * gameplay pass.
60	 */
61	export async function acquirePointerLock(page: Page): Promise<void> {
62	  const canvas = page.locator(".game-canvas-wrap canvas");
63	  await canvas.click(); // first click only acquires the lock
64	  try {
65	    await page.waitForFunction(() => document.pointerLockElement !== null, undefined, { timeout: 2000 });
66	  } catch {
67	    await page.evaluate(() => {
68	      window.__monecraft!.input.input.pointerLocked = true;
69	    });
70	  }
71	}
72	
73	export async function itemCount(page: Page, itemId: string): Promise<number> {
74	  return page.evaluate((id) => window.__monecraft!.engine.state.inventory.filter((slot) => slot.id === id).reduce((sum, slot) => sum + slot.count, 0), itemId);
75	}
76

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/docs/save-format.md",
  "old_string": "`SaveData` (version 3) in `lib/game/types.ts`:\n\n- world `seed`\n- a block **diff** list — `changes: [blockIndex, blockId][]`; edits that revert to the generated baseline are pruned (player-placed crops, beds, and furnaces ride this list — they are ordinary block edits)\n- inventory slots with durability (36 slots; the first 9 are the hotbar)\n- equipped armor\n- selected hotbar slot (0–8)\n- player position\n- `dayClock`, `hearts`, `hunger` (all optional) — time of day and player stats, so sleeping through the night and a hurt/hungry player survive a reload\n- `spawnPoint` (optional, `{x,y,z}` or null) — the bed respawn point; respawn falls back to a random land point when it is null or the block there is no longer a bed",
  "new_string": "`SaveData` (version 4) in `lib/game/types.ts`:\n\n- world `seed`\n- a block **diff** list — `changes: [blockIndex, blockId][]`; edits that revert to the generated baseline are pruned (player-placed crops, beds, furnaces, and chests ride this list — they are ordinary block edits)\n- inventory slots with durability (36 slots; the first 9 are the hotbar)\n- equipped armor\n- selected hotbar slot (0–8)\n- player position\n- `dayClock`, `hearts`, `hunger` (all optional) — time of day and player stats, so sleeping through the night and a hurt/hungry player survive a reload\n- `spawnPoint` (optional, `{x,y,z}` or null) — the bed respawn point; respawn falls back to a random land point when it is null or the block there is no longer a bed\n- `blockEntities` (optional) — chest contents as `{ index, slots }[]`, keyed by the block's voxel index (the same index space as `changes`). Only **non-empty** chests are stored; an empty one carries no data and is recreated empty when opened. On load each entry is kept only if that index still holds a Chest block. This is the first per-block metadata in the format — `lib/game/save.ts` (`serializeContainers` / `readContainers`) handles it"
}
```

18:## React shell (`lib/game/useMinecraftGame.ts`, `components/`)
20:- `useMinecraftGame` creates the `GameEngine` in the canvas mount's callback ref, then an effect builds the `GameRenderer`, the `AudioDirector`, and `inputController` and drives the `requestAnimationFrame` loop: `engine.step(dt, input)` (in bounded catch-up substeps of ≤50 ms, so slow frames — e.g. software GL — don't run the simulation in slow motion) → drain engine events (death/respawn handling, `attackSwung` → `renderer.triggerSwing()`, + `renderer.handleEvent` for particle bursts, + `audio.handleEvent`) → `minimap.sync(state)` → `renderer.sync(state)` → `audio.sync(state, dt)` → `renderer.render()`. The minimap must sync **before** the renderer because it reads `state.worldMeshDirty`, which `renderer.sync` clears.
30:## Game engine (`lib/game/engine/`)
38:### Per-frame step order (in `GameEngine.step`)
53:Combat (`systems/combat.ts`) runs on the `attack` command rather than per frame. The `placeBlock` command runs a fixed right-click precedence (`systems/interact.ts`) — feed an aimed animal (`tryFeedAimedMob`) → interact with the aimed block (`tryInteractBlock`, e.g. sleep in a bed, open a furnace) → use the held item (`tryUseHeldItem`, e.g. a hoe tills soil, seeds plant a crop) → `placeSelectedBlock` — so each interaction consumes the click instead of getting a block placed against it. New mechanics get a new system module and a slot in this sequence — don't grow the engine class with inline logic.
55:## Renderer (`lib/game/render/`)
59:- **World mesh**: one mesh covers the visible region (not chunked), rebuilt when the player crosses a `RENDER_GRID` (20-block) boundary or when the engine sets `state.worldMeshDirty` (block edits, respawn, unstuck). Old geometry is disposed on rebuild.
65:- `minimap.ts` / `minimapColors.ts` — the top-right minimap: pure column sampling (top non-air block, height-shaded `BLOCK_COLORS`) feeding a 2D canvas that rebuilds its 128×128 base only when the player crosses a 16-block grid boundary or `worldMeshDirty` is set (read-only — the renderer owns clearing it), then blits with a yaw-rotated player arrow at ~10 Hz.
67:## Audio (`lib/game/audio/`)
71:- `audioDirector.ts` — the shell-side observer, exactly parallel to the renderer: `handleEvent(GameEvent)` plays one-shots (block break/place by material, hurt/eat/jump/land, mob attacks, death/respawn stingers) and `sync(state, dt)` drives everything continuous (footsteps, mining hit ticks, mob ambience, music mood, and the weather rain bed). Music follows `state.daylight` immediately and the biome with 5 s hysteresis, and ducks while paused. The rain bed (`rainLoop.ts`) is a looping filtered-noise source on the graph, off the SFX voice budget, whose gain tracks `state.weather` rain intensity (silent for snow / while paused). The engine never imports this module.
78:## Input (`lib/game/input/inputController.ts`)
84:## Inventory and items (`lib/game/`)
87:- `items.ts` — `ITEM_DEFS`, `BLOCK_TO_SLOT`, `BREAK_HARDNESS`, armor slots, slot factories. `recipes.ts` — `RECIPES` (each with an optional `station`, e.g. `"furnace"` for smelting).
92:## World layer (`lib/world/`)
103:## Engine invariants & gotchas
111:- **Pointer lock**: the first left-click only acquires pointer lock (no mining); `KeyI` explicitly exits lock; right-click places without requiring lock.
```

5:## A new block
7:1. Add to the `BlockId` enum and `BLOCK_COLORS` in `lib/world/blocks.ts` — the atlas auto-generates its tile. Add a `HELD_BLOCK_COLORS` entry for the first-person model tint.
12:6. Map it to a sound family in `lib/game/audio/materials.ts` — the `BlockId → MaterialGroup` record is exhaustive, so typecheck fails until the entry exists.
15:## A new item or recipe
20:- Recipes are `{ id, label, cost: [{slotId, count}], result: {slotId, count} }` in `lib/game/recipes.ts`. An optional `station` (e.g. `"furnace"`) makes a recipe a smelting recipe: it only crafts while that station's panel is open, enforced in the `craft` command and shown locked in the recipe book.
23:## A new mob drop
28:## A new mob
35:- To make a passive animal **breedable**, add it to `FEED_ITEMS` in `lib/game/engine/systems/interact.ts` (which food puts it "in love"). Breeding itself is generic: `lib/game/engine/systems/breeding.ts` pairs two fed adults of the same kind into a baby (scaled down via `ageTimer`/`BABY_SCALE`, no drops until grown), bounded by `PASSIVE_CAP`. `MobState.fedTimer`/`ageTimer` and the tunables in `config.ts` drive it; mobs are never persisted, so this is session-only by design.
37:## A new player skin preset
43:## A new sound
49:## An interactive block (right-click behavior)
51:- Right-click (and KeyE) dispatch `placeBlock`, which runs a fixed precedence in `GameEngine.dispatch` before falling through to placement: feed an aimed mob → `tryInteractBlock` → use the held item → place. To make a block do something on right-click, register it in `INTERACTIVE_BLOCKS` and add a branch in `tryInteractBlock` (`lib/game/engine/systems/interact.ts`).
52:- The handler returns `true` to consume the click (no block is placed) — return `true` even when the action is refused (e.g. a bed during the day) so the player doesn't place a block into the bed by accident.
53:- Two reference implementations: the **bed** sets `state.spawnPoint` and starts the sleep fade (`state.sleepTimer`); the **furnace** opens the inventory and sets `state.craftingStation`, which unlocks its `station` recipes (see "A new item or recipe"). A station-opening block also needs an `openedStation` handler in `useMinecraftGame` to release pointer lock. See [architecture.md](architecture.md) for the step order.
55:## A random-tick behavior (growth / spread)
57:- Block updates that happen "over time" run through `lib/game/engine/systems/randomTicks.ts`: every `RANDOM_TICK_INTERVAL_SECONDS` it samples `RANDOM_TICK_SAMPLES` columns within `RANDOM_TICK_RADIUS` of the player and runs a handler on each column's top block. Register a `BlockId → handler` in `RANDOM_TICK_HANDLERS`; the handler edits via `state.blockChanges` and sets `state.worldMeshDirty`.

38	### Per-frame step order (in `GameEngine.step`)
39	
40	0. Pause gate: while `state.paused`, `step` refreshes the snapshot and returns — mobs, the day clock, mining, and stats all freeze (autosave still serializes fine)
41	1. Stuck detection / auto-unstuck (`STUCK_RESET_SECONDS`)
42	2. Death check + respawn countdown (while dead, only mobs tick)
43	3. Sleep gate: while `state.sleepTimer > 0`, `step` decrements the fade and returns (full freeze, like pause); at zero it skips the clock to the next morning
44	4. Player movement & physics (`systems/playerMotion.ts` — derives direction from `yaw`, scratch vectors, no per-frame allocations)
45	5. Hunger drain from sprint/walk/jump budgets + health regen (`systems/playerStats.ts`)
46	6. Mining progress and block breaking (`systems/mining.ts`; placement also lives here)
47	7. Day-night clock (`systems/dayNight.ts` — `daylightAt()` is the single daylight formula); immediately after, `systems/weather.ts` sets the **transient** `state.weather` (rain/snow/clear) as a pure function of `dayClock` + seed — cosmetic only: never serialized, never touches spawn/daylight balance
48	8. Random block ticks (`systems/randomTicks.ts` — each interval samples columns near the player and runs per-block handlers; drives crop growth and is the extension point for future saplings / grass spread)
49	9. Night hostile spawning (`systems/spawnDirector.ts`, interval/cap in config)
50	10. Mob AI: wander/aggro/flee, attacks with line-of-sight, daylight burn (`systems/mobAI.ts`)
51	11. Animal breeding (`systems/breeding.ts` — feed-to-love timers, baby maturity, pairing in-love adults within range; the passive cap bounds the population)
52	
53	Combat (`systems/combat.ts`) runs on the `attack` command rather than per frame. The `placeBlock` command runs a fixed right-click precedence (`systems/interact.ts`) — feed an aimed animal (`tryFeedAimedMob`) → interact with the aimed block (`tryInteractBlock`, e.g. sleep in a bed, open a furnace) → use the held item (`tryUseHeldItem`, e.g. a hoe tills soil, seeds plant a crop) → `placeSelectedBlock` — so each interaction consumes the click instead of getting a block placed against it. New mechanics get a new system module and a slot in this sequence — don't grow the engine class with inline logic.
54	
55	## Renderer (`lib/game/render/`)
```

49	## An interactive block (right-click behavior)
50	
51	- Right-click (and KeyE) dispatch `placeBlock`, which runs a fixed precedence in `GameEngine.dispatch` before falling through to placement: feed an aimed mob → `tryInteractBlock` → use the held item → place. To make a block do something on right-click, register it in `INTERACTIVE_BLOCKS` and add a branch in `tryInteractBlock` (`lib/game/engine/systems/interact.ts`).
52	- The handler returns `true` to consume the click (no block is placed) — return `true` even when the action is refused (e.g. a bed during the day) so the player doesn't place a block into the bed by accident.
53	- Two reference implementations: the **bed** sets `state.spawnPoint` and starts the sleep fade (`state.sleepTimer`); the **furnace** opens the inventory and sets `state.craftingStation`, which unlocks its `station` recipes (see "A new item or recipe"). A station-opening block also needs an `openedStation` handler in `useMinecraftGame` to release pointer lock. See [architecture.md](architecture.md) for the step order.
54	
55	## A random-tick behavior (growth / spread)
56	
57	- Block updates that happen "over time" run through `lib/game/engine/systems/randomTicks.ts`: every `RANDOM_TICK_INTERVAL_SECONDS` it samples `RANDOM_TICK_SAMPLES` columns within `RANDOM_TICK_RADIUS` of the player and runs a handler on each column's top block. Register a `BlockId → handler` in `RANDOM_TICK_HANDLERS`; the handler edits via `state.blockChanges` and sets `state.worldMeshDirty`.
58	- The crop handler is the reference: wheat stage ids are consecutive, so growth is `block + 1`, and the mature stage has no handler so it stops. Because crops are ordinary block edits, they persist for free via the save's block diff — no new save fields.
59	- Tunables live in `config.ts`; the headless test pattern (a minimal `GameState`, a scripted rng that maps a sample onto a known column) is in `randomTicks.test.ts`.
60

A station-opening block also needs an `openedStation` handler in `useMinecraftGame` to release pointer lock. See [architecture.md](architecture.md) for the step order.\n\n## A random-tick behavior (growth / spread)",
  "new_string": "- Three reference implementations: the **bed** sets `state.spawnPoint` and starts the sleep fade (`state.sleepTimer`); the **furnace** opens the inventory and sets `state.craftingStation`, which unlocks its `station` recipes (see \"A new item or recipe\"); the **chest** opens the inventory and sets `state.openContainerIndex` (see below). A block that opens the inventory from a mouse click also needs an `openedStation`/`openedContainer` handler in `useMinecraftGame` to release pointer lock. See [architecture.md](architecture.md) for the step order.\n\n## A container block (block-entity storage)\n\n- Blocks are bare `BlockId`s, so any block that needs **attached data** (a chest's items) stores it in `state.containers: Map<voxelIndex, InventorySlot[]>`, keyed by `world.index(x,y,z)`. The chest is the reference:\n  - **Open**: `interactChest` (an `INTERACTIVE_BLOCKS` branch) lazily creates the slot array, sets `state.openContainerIndex`, opens the inventory, and emits `openedContainer`. The snapshot exposes `container`, and `InventoryPanel` renders a second grid; `toggleInventory`/`pause` clear `openContainerIndex`.\n  - **Move items**: the `moveStack` command swaps a slot across the inventory/chest boundary using the pure `inventory.moveStack` (chest indices offset by `CONTAINER_SLOT_BASE` in `commands.ts`).\n  - **Place / break**: placing the block (`mining.placeSelectedBlock`) seeds an empty container; breaking it spills the contents into the inventory via `inventory.tryInsertSlots` (all-or-nothing — a break is refused with a `breakBlocked` event when they don't fit).\n  - **Persist**: `serialize()` writes non-empty containers to `blockEntities` (`save.serializeContainers`); boot restores them (`save.readContainers`) only for indices that still hold the block. This is an **additive save bump** — see [save-format.md](save-format.md).\n\n## A random-tick behavior (growth / spread)"
}

New `chest` block/item, an 8-planks recipe, a `CHEST_SLOTS` tunable, a generated chest atlas tile, and a synthesized open-creak sound\n- **Juice & atmosphere pass**: the world now reacts and breathes,"

Six focused, bisectable commits on `feat/chests-storage` implement **persistent chest storage** with a reusable block-entity layer:

| Commit | What |

| `feat(world)` | Chest block, item, 8-planks recipe, atlas tile, `CHEST_SLOTS` |
| `feat(inventory)` | pure `tryInsertSlots` + cross-array `moveStack` helpers |
| `feat(save)` | save v4 + `blockEntities` (additive, `SAVE_KEY` unchanged) |
| `feat(engine)` | place / open / move / break-spill, the `containers` map |
| `feat(ui)` | 27-slot chest grid in the inventory panel + open-creak sound |
| `docs` | save-format, architecture, adding-content, reference, manual, tuning, README, CHANGELOG + e2e |

- `lint` (0 errors; the 2 warnings are pre-existing in `Hotbar.tsx`/`StatusBars.tsx`), `typecheck`, `format:check`

- `bun run build` — ✓
- `bun run test:e2e` — **10/10 pass**, including the new chest open→store→reload round-trip

**Settled behaviors:** 27-slot chests; breaking a chest spills contents into your inventory (durability preserved) and refuses if there's no room. No worldgen impact; v1/v2/v3 saves still load.

{
  "command": "gh pr create --repo hutusi/monecraft --base main --head feat/chests-storage --title \"feat: chests & persistent storage (block-entity system)\" --body \"$(cat <<'EOF'\n## What & why\n\nCloses the most-felt foundational gap: until now *everything* a player owned rode in 36 inventory slots on their person, with nowhere to stash anything. This adds **chests** — craft one from 8 planks, place it, right-click to open a **27-slot** storage grid.\n\nIt also introduces the game's first **block-entity** layer (per-block attached data), which is the foundation later features (dungeon loot, boss rewards) can build on.\n\n## Design decisions\n\n- **27-slot single chests** (9×3), rendered above the player inventory in the existing panel.\n- **Breaking a chest spills its contents into your inventory** (tool/armor durability preserved) and returns the chest item. If there isn't room for everything, the break is **refused** (chest stays intact, toast shown) — consistent with the game's no-ground-item loot model.\n\n## How it works\n\n- `state.containers: Map<voxelIndex, InventorySlot[]>` holds each placed chest's slots (keyed by `world.index`, the same space as the block diff).\n- Right-click opens a chest via the existing interact precedence (after bed/furnace); the new `moveStack` command shuttles slots across the inventory/chest boundary (chest indices offset by `CONTAINER_SLOT_BASE`). Pure `moveStack`/`tryInsertSlots` helpers join `lib/game/inventory.ts`.\n- **Save format v4** — additive: a new optional `blockEntities` field persists non-empty chests; `migrateSaveV3toV4` is a pure version bump and `readSave` chains v1→v2→v3→v4. **`SAVE_KEY` is unchanged and there is no worldgen impact** (chests are never generated), so v1/v2/v3 saves still load.\n\n## Commits (each builds & lints green)\n\n1. `feat(world)` — Chest block, item, recipe, atlas tile, `CHEST_SLOTS`\n2. `feat(inventory)` — pure `tryInsertSlots` + cross-array `moveStack` helpers\n3. `feat(save)` — persist chest block-entities (save v4)\n4. `feat(engine)` — place, open, move, break-spill\n5. `feat(ui)` — chest container grid in the inventory panel\n6. `docs` — save-format/architecture/adding-content/reference/manual/tuning/README/CHANGELOG + e2e\n\n## Verification\n\n`bun run lint`, `typecheck`, `format:check`, `bun test` (352 pass), `bun run build`, and `bun run test:e2e` (10/10, including a new chest open→store→reload round-trip) all green.\n\n## Save/worldgen impact\n\nAdditive save bump **v3 → v4** (`SAVE_KEY` unchanged). **No worldgen impact** — worldgen hashes are not re-baselined.\nEOF\n)\" 2>&1 | tail -5",
  "description": "Open PR against hutusi/monecraft main"
}

## Recap

Starting from the roadmap in memory, you chose **persistent storage (chests)** as the next feature. It's now implemented, fully verified, and up for review.

**What shipped** — chests with a reusable block-entity layer:
- Craft a chest (8 planks), place it, right-click to open a 27-slot grid above your inventory; move items across both grids with click-to-swap.
- Breaking a chest spills its contents back into your inventory (durability preserved) and returns the chest item; refused if there's no room.
- Save format **v4** (additive — `SAVE_KEY` unchanged, v1/v2/v3 still load, no worldgen impact).

**Delivered as 6 bisectable commits** on `feat/chests-storage` (world → inventory helpers → save → engine → UI → docs/e2e), each lint/build-green, Conventional Commits with no AI-attribution trailers.

**Verification, all green:** `lint` (0 errors), `typecheck`, `format:check`, `bun test` (352 pass), `build`, and `test:e2e` (10/10, including a new chest open→store→reload round-trip).

One forward pointer for whenever you want it: the roadmap memory still lists **dungeons / pre-filled loot chests** as the natural fast-follow — it rides on this block-entity layer but would touch worldgen (a `SAVE_KEY` bump + hash re-baseline), so it's deliberately out of this PR.

coderabbitai[bot] lib/game/engine/GameEngine.ts:399
_⚠️ Potential issue_ | _🔴 Critical_ | _⚡ Quick win_

**Moving items within the player inventory can overwrite the open chest.**

On Line 395, the first `writeBack()` can replace `state.inventory` before the second call runs. If `from` and `to` both resolve to the player inventory while a chest is open, `b.arr === state.inventory` becomes false on the second pass, so the `else` branch writes `moved.b` into `state.containers`. That corrupts the chest contents in memory and the next save will persist the wrong `blockEntities`.  
  

<details>
<summary>Suggested fix</summary>

```diff
-    const writeBack = (arr: InventorySlot[], next: InventorySlot[]): void => {
-      if (arr === state.inventory) state.inventory = next;
+    const aTarget = a.arr === state.inventory ? "inventory" : "container";
+    const bTarget = b.arr === state.inventory ? "inventory" : "container";
+
+    const writeBack = (target: "inventory" | "container", next: InventorySlot[]): void => {
+      if (target === "inventory") state.inventory = next;
       else if (state.openContainerIndex !== null) state.containers.set(state.openContainerIndex, next);
     };
-    writeBack(a.arr, moved.a);
```

119	`RENDER_GRID`, `THIRD_PERSON_DISTANCE`, `THIRD_PERSON_MARGIN`.
120	
121	`RENDER_RADIUS` is the biggest **performance** lever: the renderer meshes one region
122	of this radius around the player, so larger values draw more terrain at higher cost;
123	`RENDER_GRID` is how far the player moves before that mesh rebuilds (smaller = more
124	frequent rebuilds, fresher view). `STUCK_RESET_SECONDS` is how long an overlap is
125	tolerated before the auto-unstuck teleport fires.
126	
127	## Save- and worldgen-sensitive tunables
128	
129	Change these only with care:
130	
131	- **`SAVE_KEY`** (`"minecraft_save_v5"`) is the localStorage key name. Note it's
132	  versioned independently of the save **schema** (currently v3, per
133	  [save-format.md](save-format.md)) — renaming `SAVE_KEY` orphans every existing
134	  player's save. Don't bump it to express a schema change; add a migration instead.
135	- **`INVENTORY_SLOTS` / `HOTBAR_SLOTS` / `MAX_STACK_SIZE`** affect the saved
136	  inventory layout. `save.ts` already migrates the v1 (40-slot) → v2 (36-slot)
137	  change; altering these again needs a matching migration or old saves break.
138	- **Daylight thresholds and `WAKE_DAY_PHASE`** shape the persisted `dayClock`'s
139	  meaning but are not save-format-breaking on their own.
140	- **Worldgen is not tuned here.** Terrain/ore/structure constants live in the
```

> AGENT

The failure is at the chest-*open* step (line 126), before any of my review-fix changes to this test — it's pre-existing flakiness I should harden. The browser game generates a **random seed each load**, so terrain varies; my test places the chest based on a position snapshot and then opens via a *separate* `evaluate`, so a physics frame between them can drift the player off the chest (the mining test avoids this by re-raycasting every frame). Let me make placement + open atomic in one `evaluate` so the raycast can't miss:

> TOOL

Interact wins over placement, so the chest opens.\n  const chestIndex = await page.evaluate(() => {\n    const engine = window.__monecraft!.engine;\n    const state = engine.state;\n    const x = Math.floor(state.player.position.x);\n    const y = Math.floor(state.player.position.y) - 1;\n    const z = Math.floor(state.player.position.z);\n    state.player.position.x = x + 0.5;\n    state.player.position.z = z + 0.5;\n    state.player.velocity.set(0, 0, 0);\n    state.player.pitch = -Math.PI / 2 + 0.02;\n    state.blockChanges.set(x, y, z, 27); // BlockId.Chest\n    engine.dispatch({ type: \"placeBlock\" });\n    return state.world.index(x, y, z);\n  });\n\n  // The chest grid appears once the snapshot propagates to React.\n  await expect(page.getByTestId(\"chest-grid\")).toBeVisible();"

> AGENT

Found it. The diagnostic shows `py: 54.9995` — the player rests a hair *below* the integer, so `floor(py) - 1` computes one block too low: the chest gets buried under the real ground block, and the downward ray hits natural terrain (non-interactive) instead of the chest. The mining test gets away with this because it breaks whatever it hits; mine needs the ray to hit the *specific* chest.

The fix: snap to the nearest integer with `round()`, place the chest as the floor, and carve headroom so the down-ray can only hit the chest — fully deterministic regardless of the random terrain:

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_015xWhkxjNVDFKMtRBJMoBkg
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/e2e/smoke.e2e.ts",
  "old_string": "  // Place a chest at the block underfoot, aim straight down, and right-click —\n  // all in one step so no physics frame can drift the player off the chest\n  // before the raycast. Interact wins over placement, so the chest opens.\n  const chestIndex = await page.evaluate(() => {\n    const engine = window.__monecraft!.engine;\n    const state = engine.state;\n    const x = Math.floor(state.player.position.x);\n    const y = Math.floor(state.player.position.y) - 1;\n    const z = Math.floor(state.player.position.z);\n    state.player.position.x = x + 0.5;\n    state.player.position.z = z + 0.5;\n    state.player.velocity.set(0, 0, 0);\n    state.player.pitch = -Math.PI / 2 + 0.02;\n    state.blockChanges.set(x, y, z, 27); // BlockId.Chest\n    engine.dispatch({ type: \"placeBlock\" });\n    return state.world.index(x, y, z);\n  });\n\n  const diag = await page.evaluate((idx) => {\n    const s = window.__monecraft!.engine.state;\n    return { openIndex: s.openContainerIndex, invOpen: s.inventoryOpen, hasContainer: s.containers.has(idx), py: s.player.position.y, pitch: s.player.pitch };\n  }, chestIndex);\n  console.log(\"CHEST_DIAG\", JSON.stringify(diag));\n\n  // The chest grid appears once the snapshot propagates to React.\n  await expect(page.getByTestId(\"chest-grid\")).toBeVisible();",
  "new_string": "  // Build a deterministic spot: a chest as the floor with cleared headroom, the\n  // player standing on it looking straight down, then right-click — all in one\n  // step so the down-ray can only hit the chest (interact wins over placement,\n  // so it opens). `round` (not `floor`) tolerates the player resting a hair\n  // below the integer after collision resolution.\n  const chestIndex = await page.evaluate(() => {\n    const engine = window.__monecraft!.engine;\n    const state = engine.state;\n    const x = Math.floor(state.player.position.x);\n    const z = Math.floor(state.player.position.z);\n    const groundY = Math.round(state.player.position.y) - 1;\n    state.blockChanges.set(x, groundY, z, 27); // BlockId.Chest, the floor\n    state.blockChanges.set(x, groundY + 1, z, 0); // Air headroom (feet/body/head)\n    state.blockChanges.set(x, groundY + 2, z, 0);\n    state.blockChanges.set(x, groundY + 3, z, 0);\n    state.player.position.set(x + 0.5, groundY + 1, z + 0.5);\n    state.player.velocity.set(0, 0, 0);\n    state.player.pitch = -Math.PI / 2 + 0.02;\n    engine.dispatch({ type: \"placeBlock\" });\n    return state.world.index(x, groundY, z);\n  });\n\n  // The chest grid appears once the snapshot propagates to React.\n  await expect(page.getByTestId(\"chest-grid\")).toBeVisible();"
}

> AGENT

Done. Here's the full picture of the review and what I did.

## CodeRabbit review on PR #16

It posted **3 actionable + 1 nitpick**. My triage and resolution:

| Finding | Verdict | Action |
|---|---|---|
| 🔴 **Critical**: `applyMoveStack` could overwrite the open chest when you rearrange the **player inventory while a chest is open** | **Real bug** — my tests missed the inv↔inv-while-open case | Fixed: resolve each side's destination *before* the first write (which reassigns `state.inventory` and made the second check stale). Added a regression test. |
| 🟡 `tuning.md` said schema is "v3" | Valid (my PR bumped it) | Fixed → v4 |
| 🟡 `reference.md` Chest note omitted the full-inventory refusal | Valid | Note tightened. (The "27 vs 25 rows" count is the pre-existing wheat-stage-collapse convention — count itself is correct, left as-is.) |
| ⚡ e2e asserted via `containers.values()[0]` (order-dependent) | Valid | Now asserts by the chest's voxel index. |

The critical bug was worth catching — rearranging your hotbar with a chest open would have silently corrupted the chest and then **persisted the wrong contents to the save**.

While fixing the e2e I also uncovered (and fixed) **flakiness the first green run had masked**: the browser game uses a random seed per load, and the player can rest a hair below the integer Y after collision resolution, so `floor(py)-1` buried the chest under the real ground and the down-ray missed it. The test now rounds to the nearest block, lays the chest as the floor, and carves headroom — **6/6 repeated runs pass**.

## Verification (all green)
- `bun test` — 353 pass (incl. the new regression test) · `build` ✓ · `lint` 0 errors · `format:check` ✓
- `bun run test:e2e` — **10/10**, chest test confirmed non-flaky across repeats

The fix is committed locally as `07df94f` on `feat/chests-storage` but **not pushed** — per your usual workflow I hold review fixes for you to check first.

Want me to push it to the PR?

🎉 **Merged and cleaned up.**

[PR #16](https://github.com/hutusi/monecraft/pull/16) is merged into `main` (`f6eb5c6`), the feature branch is deleted both remote and local, and you're back on `main` up to date with origin.

## What shipped

**Chests & persistent storage** — the game's first block-entity layer:
- Craft a chest (8 planks), place it, right-click to open a 27-slot grid; move items across both grids with click-to-swap.
- Breaking a chest spills its contents into your inventory (durability preserved), refused if there's no room.
- Save format **v4** — additive, `SAVE_KEY` unchanged, no worldgen impact; v1/v2/v3 saves still load.

Delivered as 7 commits (6 feature slices + 1 review-fix), all CI green: `verify`, `e2e`, and CodeRabbit passed. The review caught a genuine critical bug (inventory-rearrange corrupting an open chest) that's now fixed and regression-tested.

For when you want the next one: the roadmap memory still has **dungeons / pre-filled loot chests** as the natural fast-follow — it builds directly on this block-entity layer, though it'll touch worldgen (a `SAVE_KEY` bump + hash re-baseline). Just say the word.

> AGENT

Memory updated to reflect chests as shipped. Here's my read now that the block-entity layer exists:

## My recommendation: **Dungeons / loot structures**

This is the momentum play. We just built the container/block-entity layer — dungeons are what make it *pay off*: explore → find a buried room → fight what guards it → crack open a **pre-filled loot chest**. That's a complete gameplay loop, and it fills the roadmap's most-cited gap (no exploration payoff or endgame past mining diamond). It's also lower-friction than it sounds, because the pieces already exist:
- the `placeStructures` worldgen hook (where the ~120 houses are placed),
- chest containers that already persist (v4 — loot just needs to be written into them at gen time),
- the `rollMobDrops` loot-table pattern to reuse for chest loot.

The one real cost is that touching worldgen means a **`SAVE_KEY` bump + a worldgen-hash re-baseline** (a documented, routine policy here) — i.e. existing worlds regenerate. Medium–high effort.

## The alternatives, briefly

- **Caves & block-light** (dark caves, torches, glowing lava + lava/oxygen hazards) — the most *authentic* Minecraft gap, but by far the largest and riskiest: it needs a per-voxel light channel and changes to the mesher hot path (~weeks). Best saved for when you want a big atmospheric swing.
- **Ranged combat + boss** (bows/arrows, skeletons shoot back, a boss target) — the smallest and lowest-risk (a few days, no save/worldgen impact), and it gives the diamond grind a goal. A strong pick if you'd rather a quick, self-contained win — and it would also make dungeons more compelling later (something worth fighting through).

There's a nice sequencing here: **dungeons next, then ranged/boss** would complete the "explore → fight → loot → endgame" arc, with caves-light as the eventual big atmospheric project.

Which direction should I work up into a full plan?

> AGENT

Here's the lay of the land:

- **package.json** is at `0.5.0`; tags are `v0.2.0`…`v0.5.0` (so the convention is a `v`-prefixed git tag per release, **no GitHub Release objects** — just tags).
- The CHANGELOG follows Keep-a-Changelog; `0.5.0` shipped earlier today, and everything since (chests + the whole juice/UI/farming/beds/cooking/breeding/loot batch) sits under `[Unreleased]`.

So a release here is a small, mechanical chore:
1. Move `[Unreleased]` → `[0.6.0] - 2026-06-13` in `CHANGELOG.md` and add a fresh empty `[Unreleased]` scaffold.
2. Bump `package.json` `version` → `0.6.0`.
3. Commit on `main` (`chore(release): v0.6.0`), tag `v0.6.0`, push the commit + tag.

That matches the existing flow exactly. I'd do it straight on `main` (it's a release chore, not a feature), and confirm with you before the push/tag since that's outward-facing.

The one real decision is the version number. I recommend **0.6.0** — it's the consistent minor-bump-per-feature-batch cadence this project has used (0.2→0.3→0.4→0.5 were each a feature batch). That said, the unreleased scope is large enough that you could reasonably call this **1.0.0** if you feel the survival loop is now "complete enough" to leave 0.x.

- `CHANGELOG.md` — the `[Unreleased]` backlog is now `## [0.6.0] - 2026-06-13`, with a fresh empty `[Unreleased]` scaffold on top.

This release draws the line under a big batch: the UI overhaul, juice & atmosphere, farming, beds, cooking, breeding, mob loot, the camera toggle, and chests/persistent storage.

## Now — the next feature

With a clean baseline tagged, you're set to pick the next direction. The three open candidates (from the roadmap memory) remain:

- **Dungeons / loot structures** — my recommendation; rides on the chest/block-entity layer you just shipped and fills the exploration-payoff gap (cost: a `SAVE_KEY` bump + worldgen re-baseline).
- **Ranged combat + boss** — smallest/lowest-risk, no save impact, gives the diamond grind a goal.
- **Caves & block-light** — the most authentic but largest/riskiest.

You'd mentioned wanting to clarify something before choosing — happy to dig into effort estimates, the dungeons save-reset trade-off, scope, or an off-roadmap idea. What would you like to talk through?
